# Changelog

All notable changes to MDEMG will be documented in this file.

The format is based on [Keep a Changelog](https://keepachangelog.com/en/1.1.0/),
and this project adheres to [Semantic Versioning](https://semver.org/spec/v2.0.0.html).

## [Unreleased]

### Added

- **Sprint GO-IMPLEMENTS-002 — audit + fix the 79-pair analyzer/ingest gap** (2026-08-05). GO-IMPLEMENTS-001 disclosed follow-up (Q4 backlog). Audit found 83 discovered pairs failed to land in the graph. Root-caused via new `--dump-pairs` diagnostic + Cypher gap-diff: (1) 79 pairs (95%) were generated protobuf gRPC code from `/api/modulepb/*.pb.go` — analyzer walked ALL `types.Named` including files tree-sitter ingest correctly excludes, so `SaveRelationships` MATCH silently dropped them; (2) 2 pairs (`LocalExecutor`+`RemoteExecutor`) hit a stale-hash-formula issue where Neo4j has pre-refactor SymbolNodes with old `sha256("space|path|name|LINE")` IDs but current `GenerateSymbolID` excludes line — reverse-engineered live; (3) 2 pairs were fresh interfaces added in same-session sprints, need analyzer re-run. Fixes: `internal/symbols/go_types.go` gains `isGeneratedProtobuf()` filter mirroring existing `isVendored()` shape (matches `.pb.go` + `_grpc.pb.go`); `internal/symbols/relationships.go` gains 4 non-persisted diagnostic fields (`Source*/Target*` name+path) on `RelationshipRecord` so audits name what dropped; CLI `--dump-pairs <file>` emits TSV with header + 6 columns. Live Tier-3 on mdemg-dev: **discovered 269→169, gap 83→3** (96% resolved at source); remaining 3 are the stale-hash class flagged for operational re-ingest (no code change). 2 pin tests. Three arch rules pinned: (1) symbol analyzers must apply the SAME file-set filters the symbol ingest applies (both-sides mirror is REQUIRED); (2) `GenerateSymbolID` is a versioned contract — hash-formula changes require full re-ingest, no in-place migration exists, document the migration op in the same commit that changes the formula; (3) diagnostic dumps for symbol-graph audits MUST carry name+path alongside symbol_id (bare-ID dumps are useless for gap-audit). Sprint dir: docs/development/go-implements-002/.

- **Sprint HITL-ANALYTICS-TILE-001 — Grafana dashboard for HITL curation observability** (2026-08-04). Closes the observability loop for the HITL curation arc shipped this session (HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001 + JIMINY-CONTRADICTED-BRIDGE-QUALITY-001 + AUTOGRADE-SCHEDULE-001). New `mdemg-hitl` Grafana dashboard at `/d/mdemg-hitl/mdemg-hitl-curation` with 6 panels: (1) Grade Cadence stacked-bar timeseries (auto vs operator, 30d); (2) Auto/Operator Share donut (7d); (3) Pending Drafts Queue stat (green<5 / yellow / red>20 — matches `hitl-curation` alert threshold); (4) Draft Status Split (all-time approved/dismissed/pending); (5) Scheduled Autograde Run History table (30 rows from `scheduled_job_events WHERE job_name LIKE 'scheduled-autograde%'`); (6) Reinforcement Applied 7d stat — the ONLY grade class that mutates the substrate (auto:* grades ALWAYS have reinforcement_applied=false per HITL-CURATION-002 + HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001 invariants). Panel descriptions embed cross-references to arc invariants + sprint names so operators don't need to grep CLAUDE.md. Live-verified: dashboard loaded via `POST /api/admin/provisioning/dashboards/reload`, all 6 panel SQL queries return real data from mdemg-dev; grafana embed synced (16 files); pin tests green. Two arch rules pinned: (1) HITL dashboards MUST split `reinforcement_applied=true` counts into their OWN panel (mixing auto dismissals with operator approvals obscures "is the substrate actually changing?"); (2) panel descriptions should embed cross-references to arc invariants + sprint names (Grafana-side pattern that mirrors DASHBOARD-TRUTH-002's title-embedded design-intent numbers). Sprint dir: docs/development/hitl-analytics-tile-001/.

- **Sprint SCRUB-ENV-REF-001 — env-secret scrubber preserves shell env-var references** (2026-08-04). HIGH `scheduled-job export-auto` alert (2026-08-04T22:23:01Z, "EXPORT BLOCKED: 2 privacy scrub violations detected — training data contains PII") triaged to a false-positive class: 2 flagged rows in `retrieval_events.query_text` were my own diagnostic `bash error: ... PGPASSWORD=$TSDB_PASSWORD ... ${TSDB_HOST_PORT:-5433} ...` captures — shell env-var REFERENCES, not literal secrets. `envSecretPattern` matched them as `PASSWORD=<value>` and blocked the daily export. Fix: `scrubEnvSecret` now uses `ReplaceAllStringFunc` + `isShellEnvVarRef` to recognize three reference shapes (bash-plain `$FOO`, bash-braced `${FOO}` / `${FOO:-default}` / `${FOO:?err}`, Windows `%FOO%`) and preserve them; literal secret values (`PASSWORD=hunter2`) continue to be redacted. 22 pin tests including `TestScrubEnvSecret_LiveExportAutoRegressionPin` (the EXACT string that fired the alarm). One-time cleanup: 2 offending `retrieval_events` rows UPDATEd with a `[SCRUB-ENV-REF-001 cleanup: ... redacted 2026-08-04]` placeholder (rows preserved for retrieval-audit continuity, only free-text payload replaced). Live Tier-3: `mdemg data export-auto` completed cleanly with `Privacy: 0 violations`, total_rows=16379. Three arch rules pinned: (1) env-secret scrubbers MUST distinguish literal secrets from shell env-var references — pointers aren't secrets; (2) pattern-based scrubbers should be conservative on ambiguous shapes but explicit about what they preserve (malformed refs like `$!bang` still redact); (3) privacy-scrub false-positive regression pins use EXACT live-triggering content, not synthesized minimal cases. Sprint dir: docs/development/scrub-env-ref-001/.

- **Sprint AUTOGRADE-SCHEDULE-001 — scheduled autograde loop (default off, opt-in)** (2026-08-04). Closes the HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001 + JIMINY-CONTRADICTED-BRIDGE-QUALITY-001 arc: sprint B added invariant-preserving auto-dismiss (drain mechanism), sprint C tightened source-side gate (reduces accumulation rate), this ships periodic execution under supervisor so the queue self-drains without operator ceremony. New `internal/review/schedule.go::AutogradeScheduler` mirrors `ftloop.BenchScheduler` design (SUPERVISOR-002 nil-return-when-disabled, subprocess-spawned `mdemg review autograde` CLI reuses ALL shipped grade-writing logic including HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001's NonReinforcingApplier fall-through). Per-dataset iteration with distinct jobhealth `job_name=scheduled-autograde:<dataset>` (NOSILENT-001 contract — per-dataset failures don't share cooldown). 6 new config knobs (all defaults sensible: 6h interval, 15min initial delay, 50-item limit, contradicted_drafts default dataset). **NEVER passes `--force`** — regression pin (`TestAutogradeScheduler_RunOne_NoForceFlag`); the scheduled loop is for organic accumulation, force is operator-only for backfill after sink-logic changes. 9 pin tests. Live Tier-3 (mdemg-dev): boot log + 1min timer + successful CLI subprocess + jobhealth event `scheduled-autograde:contradicted_drafts success=t latency_ms=17595` end-to-end. Three arch rules pinned: (1) scheduled subprocess loops MUST NEVER pass operator escape-hatch flags (--force); (2) subprocess schedulers mirror BenchScheduler shape (SUPERVISOR-002 + runCmd test seam + per-item iteration + distinct jobhealth); (3) loopback endpoint resolution handles both `:9999` and `127.0.0.1:9999` ListenAddr shapes. Sprint dir: docs/development/autograde-schedule-001/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-CONTRADICTED-BRIDGE-QUALITY-001 — content-quality gate on contradicted-draft emission** (2026-08-04). Follow-up to HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001. Sprint B drained the queue AFTER-the-fact; this sprint attacks the noise SOURCE upstream so the queue stops re-accumulating. Analysis of the 9 pending drafts that fired the alert found 3 with content matching existing `ConstraintPromotionRejectPatterns` (Bash errors × 2, "Phase 92: gap analysis") + 3 with non-actionable guidance types (pattern × 2, learning × 1). New two-layer gate in `internal/jiminy/contradicted_bridge.go::ContradictedBridgeGate`, mirroring `hidden.ConstraintPromotionGate` design: (1) type filter (default: only constraint + correction eligible) fires first with reason `type:<type>`; (2) content-pattern filter reuses `DefaultConstraintPromotionRejectPatterns()` (single source of truth with constraint-promoter — future widenings benefit both gates) with reason `pattern:<regex>`. Nil/disabled → fail-open. Wired at `service.go:2001` emit site; gate built alongside dedup cache in `SetContradictedDraftWriter`. Three new config knobs: `JIMINY_CONTRADICTED_BRIDGE_GATE_ENABLED` (default true), `JIMINY_CONTRADICTED_BRIDGE_ALLOWED_TYPES` (default "constraint,correction"), `JIMINY_CONTRADICTED_BRIDGE_REJECT_PATTERNS` (default reuses constraint-promoter list). 9 pin tests including `TestBridgeGate_LiveMdemgDevNoiseClass` — 6 subtests using EXACT content from the live queue that fired the alert (regression pin: if the gate stops catching these, alert re-fires on same class). Three arch rules pinned: (1) downstream noise-filter gates SHOULD share their pattern list with upstream producer; (2) type filter fires BEFORE pattern filter so reason string reflects blocking class; (3) nil/disabled must be fail-open (preserves shipped pre-sprint behavior). Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-contradicted-bridge-quality-001/.

- **Sprint HITL-AUTO-DISMISS-001 — invariant-preserving auto-dismiss for contradicted drafts** (2026-08-04). Triage of MEDIUM `hitl-curation: MDEMG HITL Curation Stalled` alert (9 pending drafts, 0 operator grades in 7d) found the autograder was confidently labeling noise (dim=0 dismisses) but couldn't drain the queue because the sink was gated entirely behind `reinforce=true`. Design gap: dismissal touches only the draft's own row (no substrate mutation) but the platform treated it the same as approval (which mutates via `conversation.Correct`). Fix: new optional `review.NonReinforcingApplier` interface — sinks with mixed reinforcing/non-reinforcing verdicts opt in. `contradictedDraftsSink.ApplyNonReinforcing`: dim=0 → MarkDismissed + verb `correction_draft:dismiss:auto`; dim=4 → handled=false (invariant guardrail keeps approve operator-only). Handler dispatches through the interface when `reinforce=false`. Autograder CLI gains `--force` flag (was silently swallowing 409 idempotency, blocking backfill after sink-logic changes). 6 pin tests. Live Tier-3 on mdemg-dev: 9 pending → 5 pending (4 dim=0 dismisses drained, 2 dim=4 approves correctly refused, 0 substrate mutations); alert stall_signal 9→5 (below `gt 5` threshold, clears). Three arch rules pinned: (1) sinks with mixed verdicts MUST implement `NonReinforcingApplier` — Apply's semantics stay "substrate mutation"; (2) `:auto` suffix on `ReinforcementDetail.Verb` is the auditability contract for autograder-authored writes; (3) `--force` REQUIRED to backfill after sink-logic changes. Sprint dir: docs/development/hitl-auto-dismiss-001/.

- **Sprint RETRIEVE-CALLER-CANCEL-001 — HTTP handlers distinguish caller-cancel from server error** (2026-08-04). Triage of MEDIUM SLO alert `high_error_rate` (fired 2026-08-03T15:26:32Z) found 100% of the 5xx driver was `/v1/memory/retrieve` returning HTTP 500 on `context.Canceled` — client-cancellations misclassified as server errors. 24h pattern: 20 of 289 windows above the 0.1% threshold. Same class as LLM-HEALTH-INVESTIGATION-001 (LLM-recorder `caller_canceled:` tag) applied at the HTTP handler layer. Fix: new `isCallerCancelled(err)` helper + new `httpStatusClientClosedRequest = 499` const (nginx "Client Closed Request", outside the alert-rule `^5` regex). `sanitizeError` logs INFO (not ERROR) + returns "request cancelled during X"; `writeInternalError` returns 499 on caller-cancel, 500 otherwise. Fix lands in the ONE helper — all 28 sites (retrieve, consult, suggest, ingest, backward pass, archive node, etc.) inherit it. 8 pin tests. Live Tier-3: 4 curl `--max-time 0.1` cancels landed as `status=499 duration_ms≈100 level=WARN` (was 500 duration_ms=6-8s level=ERROR pre-fix). Three arch rules pinned: (1) HTTP handlers MUST distinguish `context.Canceled` from `context.DeadlineExceeded` — server-side deadlines still alert; (2) `mdemg_http_requests_total`'s `status` label is the SLO-alert regex contract, real server errors MUST land in 5xx; (3) Centralize the classification in the helper (`writeInternalError`), not per-handler — one edit covers current + future sites. Sprint dir: docs/development/retrieve-caller-cancel-001/.

- **Sprint ENFORCE-AUTO-EXECUTE — RSIC auto-execute for enforcement patterns (default-off + dry-run + 5-layer guards)** (2026-08-03). 3/3 (final) of the JIMINY-ENFORCE arc-adjacent follow-ups. New archive_constraint_by_code action wraps ArchiveConstraintByCode with 5 guards: empty-code → no-op; ENFORCEMENT_AUTO_EXECUTE_ENABLED=false (default) → skipped; RSICProtectedSpaces guard; global rate limit (default 3/hr sliding window) + per-code cooldown (default 24h); ENFORCEMENT_AUTO_EXECUTE_DRY_RUN=true (default) → log-only. Only when all 5 pass does mutation happen (with archive_reason stamp + MEDIUM alert with reversal Cypher). New Dispatcher.SetConfig setter (cfg read at dispatch time — flag flip via env+restart, no rebuild). enforcement_false_positive_high pattern updated to emit new action + TargetCode. AllowedLLMActions 17→18. adjust_guidance_confidence auto-execute DEFERRED (mutation not cleanly reversible). 7 pin tests. Four arch rules pinned: (1) auto-execute of substrate-mutating RSIC actions MUST layer at least 5 guards in strict order; (2) auto-execute defaults MUST be off + dry-run true; (3) reversibility check per action (archive safe, adjust needs HITL); (4) Dispatcher.cfg reads happen at dispatch time not constructor time. JIMINY-ENFORCE arc-adjacent follow-ups: 3/3 SHIPPED. Sprint dir: docs/development/enforce-auto-execute/.

- **Sprint ENFORCE-UI-OVERRIDES — Jiminy tab UI: active overrides + enforcement timeline** (2026-08-03). 2nd of 3 remaining JIMINY-ENFORCE arc-adjacent follow-ups. Jiminy tab extended with Active Overrides table (revoke buttons) + Recent Override Timeline (reads constraint_overrides hypertable). New GET /v1/jiminy/override/history endpoint with space_id + hours query params, defaults to 168h. 4 new UI API helpers. Uses Promise.allSettled so single endpoint failure only blanks its panel. Route inventory adjudicated. Live Tier-3: seeded UI-SMOKE-CODE override → both endpoints returned correct data → UI JS deployed. Two arch rules pinned: (1) related read-only paths ship as DISTINCT paths, not query-param toggles on method-multiplexed endpoints; (2) UI tab data loads MUST use Promise.allSettled not Promise.all when panels are independent. Sprint dir: docs/development/enforce-ui-overrides/.

- **Sprint ENFORCE-OVERRIDES-TSDB — override JSONL migrated to TSDB (queryable audit)** (2026-08-03). First of 3 remaining JIMINY-ENFORCE arc-adjacent follow-ups. V0033 migration adds constraint_overrides hypertable (7-day chunks, 2 indices, 180d retention). TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION 32→33. OverrideManager.SetTSDB(pool, defaultSpaceID) wired from server.SetTSDBClient; auditTSDB writes to both TSDB and JSONL (JSONL kept for forensic+portability). New DatasetProvider.OverrideHistory reader for RSIC + UI. Bug caught + fixed live: auditTSDB deadlocked when called under Get()'s lazy-purge mu.Lock() — removed the redundant mu.RLock (SetTSDB is one-shot startup wiring). 1 new pin test. Live Tier-3: CLI apply/revoke both land in constraint_overrides with correct op labels. Two arch rules pinned: (1) best-effort helpers called under a caller's held mutex MUST NOT reacquire the mutex; (2) when migrating a JSONL audit trail to TSDB, keep the JSONL sink (portability across instances). Sprint dir: docs/development/enforce-overrides-tsdb/.

- **Sprint ENFORCE-004-FOLLOWUP — RSIC self_reflect hookup for enforcement outcomes** (2026-08-03). Deferred follow-up from ENFORCE-004; closes the loop from operator-visible alert → RSIC-actionable insight. New DatasetProvider.EnforcementOutcomes returns per-constraint counts via GROUP BY constraint_code + FILTER on outcome_type. New SelfAssessmentReport.EnforcementOutcomes populated in Assess(). Two new self_reflect patterns: enforcement_false_positive_high (action archive_ineffective_constraints) + enforcement_missed_violation_high (action adjust_guidance_confidence). Reuses existing AllowedLLMActions entries — no action registry expansion. Reuses ENFORCE-004/005 threshold config knobs — single source-of-truth. 4 pin tests + mockDatasetProvider extension. Live Tier-3: /v1/self-improve/assess returns EnforcementOutcomes populated with the missed_violation row from ENFORCE-005's drill. Two arch rules pinned: (1) reflector-side threshold defaults MUST fall back safely (safe default 3, not disabled) even when paired alert-rule config treats ≤0 as disable; (2) new self_reflect patterns SHOULD reuse existing AllowedLLMActions entries when semantic fits, not mint new actions. Sprint dir: docs/development/enforce-004-followup/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE-005 — post-hoc missed-violation detector; ARC COMPLETE** (2026-08-03). Final sprint of the 5-sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE arc. /v1/conversation/observe on obs_type=correction spawns a detached goroutine that embeds the correction, matches against the constraint vector index (cosine sim ≥ 0.55), and writes OutcomeMissedViolation to constraint_outcomes on match (classifier_source=correction_observed). Reuses shipped matchConstraintCodeByEmbedding. Detached goroutine uses context.Background+10s timeout (request ctx dies when response written). New MEDIUM alert jiminy_missed_violation — twin of ENFORCE-004's rule, aggregate GROUP BY constraint_code with idle-safe COALESCE(MAX(cnt),0). 2 new config knobs. Live Tier-3 end-to-end: correction "commit directly to main" content → matched no-direct-main-commits code → TSDB row landed + INFO log fired. Three arch rules pinned: (1) correction observations ARE the retrospective judgment — hook the write path, don't build a scanner; (2) detached goroutines for post-response work MUST use context.Background + timeout, not request ctx; (3) enforcement-arc alerts share ONE per-code GROUP BY + MAX(cnt) template. ARC CLOSEOUT: 5 sprints (ENFORCE-001 through -005) shipped in ~72h wall time (2026-08-01 → 2026-08-03) with prereqs ESCALATION-ACCUMULATE-001 + JIMINY-TRACKER-TTL-001 + JIMINY-MODE-001. Stateless LLM now has stateful deterministic enforcement across Write/Edit + Bash mutation surfaces, human-in-the-loop escape hatch with audit trail, and RSIC-consumable learning signal on every enforcement decision (deny-wanted / deny-overridden / missed-entirely). 13 new arch rules pinned across the arc. Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-enforce-005/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE-004 — RSIC enforcement-learning outcome types + alert** (2026-08-03). Sprint 4 of 5 in the arc. Enforcement decisions flow into constraint_outcomes so the shipped RSIC constraint-effectiveness reader picks them up. Three new GuidanceOutcome enum values: OutcomeBlockedTruePositive (denied, no override — enforcement worked), OutcomeBlockedFalsePositive (would-be deny suppressed by operator override), OutcomeMissedViolation (reserved for ENFORCE-005). No schema change — reuses outcome_type variable-value column. Service.Classify wraps strictClassifier and writes per-code enforcement outcomes; extractOverriddenCodes parses "[override:CODE reason=…]" annotations from DenialReason (single source of truth, no parallel channel). New alert rule jiminy_blocked_false_positive (MEDIUM, GROUP BY constraint_code with idle-safe COALESCE(MAX(cnt),0) subquery). 2 new config knobs. 6 new pin tests. Live Tier-3: CLI + JSONL trail verified; TSDB write path unit-test-proven (requires WARNED escalation seeding to trigger naturally). Three arch rules pinned: (1) new outcomes MUST reuse the existing outcome sink not a new schema; (2) DenialReason annotations are the single source of truth for suppressed codes; (3) enforcement alerts MUST use per-code aggregates not per-constraint fan-out. Deferred to ENFORCE-004-FOLLOWUP: RSIC self_reflect pattern hookup (needs new SelfAssessmentReport field). Deferred to ENFORCE-005: missed_violation writer. Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-enforce-004/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE-003 — operator escape-hatch override CLI + audit trail** (2026-08-03). Sprint 3 of 5 in the JIMINY-ENFORCE arc. Ships time-boxed constraint overrides so a stuck classifier false-positive on a WARNED+ escalation doesn't deadlock the operator. New OverrideManager in internal/jiminy/override.go — session-scoped in-memory (session, constraint_code) → entry with applied_at + expires_at; Apply validates duration>0 (forgotten unbounded overrides silently disable enforcement); Get lazy-purges expired; List filters by session; Revoke removes early; every apply/revoke/expire writes JSONL to ~/.mdemg/jiminy-overrides.jsonl (best-effort). StrictClassifier.Classify consults the manager after computing deny → all-overridden verdict downgrades to pass with reasons annotated; partial-overridden keeps deny with (partial-override-suppressed) annotation. HTTP: POST/GET/DELETE /v1/jiminy/override multiplexed. CLI: mdemg jiminy override [apply|list|revoke]. 9 pin tests. Live Tier-3: apply→list→GET→audit JSONL→revoke→list-empty verified end-to-end. Three arch rules pinned: (1) enforcement gate MUST have operator escape-hatch — time-boxed (duration>0 required); (2) override apply MUST require reason + write to audit trail; (3) session-scoped overrides, NEVER global. Deferred to ENFORCE-004: TSDB audit table for RSIC learning consumption. Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-enforce-003/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE-002 — Bash coverage** (2026-08-02). Sprint 2 of 5 in the JIMINY-ENFORCE arc. Extends shipped Write/Edit enforcement (JIMINY-ENFORCE-001) to the Bash tool via extension to pre-bash-check.py. Two-check pipeline: destructive-pattern guard fail-CLOSED (unchanged; rm -rf, DROP TABLE, git push --force) THEN JIMINY-ENFORCE-002 classify fail-OPEN (POSTs to /v1/jiminy/classify with tool_name="Bash", agent_output=<command truncated 2000 chars>). Verdict deny → block. Fail-open branches emit stderr WARN + marker file (parity with pre-write-check.py). 5 default read-only whitelist regexes exempt commands that can't violate a durable constraint (ls|cat|git status|docker ps|kubectl get|--version); MDEMG_BASH_WHITELIST_REGEX for operator extension. Server-side emitJiminyBlockAlert branches on ToolName: Bash gets truncated command preview in the alert. 2 new pin tests. Live Tier-3 verified 4 subprocess scenarios (whitelist skip, classify pass, fail-open with marker, destructive still deny). Two arch rules pinned: (1) Bash hook enforcement layers stack destructive-guard (fail-closed) first, Jiminy classify (fail-open) second — never reorder or merge failure modes; (2) read-only whitelist entries MUST be commands that cannot violate a durable constraint — wrong-side entry creates a stealth bypass. Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-enforce-002/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-MODE-001 — first-class strict/suggest mode selector (env + CLI + UI)** (2026-08-02). Operator directive: users must be able to change the default Jiminy enforcement mode from strict to suggest FROM UI. Ships operator-facing enum JIMINY_MODE=strict|suggest (default strict) as semantic layer over the existing JIMINY_STRICT_DEFAULT_ENABLED boolean (kept as backward-compat lower-level flag). GET /v1/jiminy/strict added (was POST-only) — reads current mode + boot default + default session. mdemg jiminy mode [strict|suggest] CLI (no-arg prints state). New Jiminy tab in /ui/ dashboard with two-button toggle + status table + help panel. jiminy: mode startup log names mode + session + strict_enabled state. Mode-aware boot logic honors JIMINY-ENFORCE-001 state-file persistence — applies desired state only if it diverges from current. Live-verified across 5 surfaces: boot log, GET endpoint, CLI read + toggle strict→suggest→strict, UI tab serves. Two arch rules pinned: (1) config values with user-facing semantic meaning ship as named enums, not implementation-level booleans; (2) user-facing config toggles MUST be reachable from UI, not just env+CLI (env requires restart+shell, CLI requires terminal familiarity, UI is accessible to humans). Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-mode-001/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-TRACKER-TTL-001 — warm store persisted across restarts, fixes 129-dropped-feedbacks class** (2026-08-02). ESCALATION-ACCUMULATE-001's live drill exposed 129 fires of "jiminy: feedback dropped — guidance_id expired from tracker" / 12 distinct guidance_ids in the current log window on mdemg-dev. Root cause: EffectivenessTracker (in-memory, LRU-1000, 24h TTL) + WarmStore (in-memory) both wiped on restart, but hook-side .jiminy-guidance-state disk file survives → feedbacks for pre-restart guidance_id → nil tracker.Lookup → entire outcome loop skipped upstream of the ESCALATION-ACCUMULATE-001 fix. WarmStore now disk-persists per-space guidance entries at ~/.mdemg/warm-store/<space>.json with atomic tmp+rename writes, hydrate-on-boot, path-sanitized space_ids, malformed-file WARN-skipping. New constructor NewWarmStoreWithPersistence(dir); wired at server.go:1066. Post-restart, /v1/jiminy/latest's existing RefreshTrackedGuidance re-registers the hydrated guidance into the tracker on first read. 3 new config knobs (JIMINY_WARM_PERSIST_DIR / JIMINY_FEEDBACK_DROP_THRESHOLD / JIMINY_FEEDBACK_DROP_LOOKBACK_MIN). New alert rule jiminy_feedback_dropped (MEDIUM). 6 new pin tests. Live Tier-3: warm → 11KB JSON persisted → restart → boot log "warm store: hydrated from disk entries=1" → /latest returned warm:true with the pre-restart guidance. Two new arch rules pinned: (1) in-memory caches on the outcome-signal path MUST persist across restarts; (2) diagnostic conclusions about missing metrics require positive verification via SELECT DISTINCT metric_name FROM metric_samples (ESCALATION-DIAGNOSIS-001 concluded a metric wasn't emitting because it queried mdemg_jiminy_feedback_dropped instead of the actual name mdemg_jiminy_feedback_dropped_total — 806 samples were flowing the whole time). Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-tracker-ttl-001/.

- **Sprint ESCALATION-ACCUMULATE-001 — fix 3+1 defects preventing WARNED escalation (JIMINY-ENFORCE prerequisite)** (2026-08-02). ESCALATION-DIAGNOSIS-001 investigation found 100% of 73 J12EscalationState rows sat at level=surfaced ignore_count=0 despite 2,177 outcomes/7d on mdemg-dev — blocking every downstream step of the JIMINY-ENFORCE arc. Root cause: compound of 3 defects. Defect 1 (dominant) — RecordOutcome silently dropped outcomes on missing keys; fixed to CREATE state on-demand since the outcome is authoritative evidence the constraint was live. Defect 3 — decay wiped IgnoreCount alongside Level, making WARNED unreachable in low-cadence sessions; fixed to reset Level only. Defect 4 — onDirty only fired on new-key; fixed to fire on every surface. Defect 2 — session_id resolution asymmetry between warm + feedback handlers; new resolveJiminySessionID helper normalizes both. 3 new pin tests. Live Tier-3 drill produced first WARNED state ever on the substrate (8 warned + 1 escalated). Three new arch rules pinned. Bonus finding: 129 "feedback dropped — guidance_id expired" log entries in current window across 12 distinct guidance_ids — DIAGNOSIS-001 missed this by checking wrong metric; follow-up sprint JIMINY-TRACKER-TTL-001 disclosed. Sprint dir: docs/development/escalation-accumulate-001/. Investigation: docs/development/escalation-diagnosis-001/.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ENFORCE-001 — Jiminy enforcement: strict-by-default + alert-on-block + fail-open-with-warning** (2026-08-01). Operator architectural directive: Jiminy is an ENFORCER of hard constraints on the main LLM's behavior, not merely advisory; violations MUST alert the user; the enforcement cycle MUST be RSIC-governed. This sprint ships the foundation (sprint 1 of 5 in the JIMINY-ENFORCE arc): (E1) new config JIMINY_STRICT_DEFAULT_ENABLED (default false in code, true in mdemg-dev .env) + JIMINY_STRICT_DEFAULT_SESSION_ID (default "claude-core") — StrictModeManager auto-enables at boot. (E2) handleJiminyClassify — on verdict=deny, HIGH-severity alert dispatched server-side via alertDispatcher.Send() with Service "jiminy-block". Extracted to narrow emitJiminyBlockAlert() helper for testability; 4 unit tests. (E3) pre-write-check.py fail-open branch — every fail-open writes stderr WARN + persistent marker file ~/.mdemg/.jiminy-server-unreachable; successful call clears; prompt-context.sh surfaces the marker every prompt until cleared. Live Tier-3 verified end-to-end: boot log + state file present + fail-open subprocess test emitted stderr WARN + wrote marker + cleared on next successful call. Three new arch rules pinned: (1) Jiminy is an ENFORCER not advisor — supersedes prior advisory framing; (2) fail-open on enforcement gates MUST leave a persistent warning trail; (3) alert dispatch on enforcement is server-side (same authority as the decision), not hook-side. Feature doc: docs/features/jiminy-strict.md. Sprint dir: docs/development/jiminy-enforce-001/.

- **Sprint FOLLOW-RATE-CALIBRATE-001 — recalibrate raw jiminy follow-rate alert to honest steady state** (2026-08-01). HEBB-ETA-001 live-smoke surfaced a chronic MEDIUM alert `Jiminy Follow Rate Drop` on a substrate whose LEVER-C-TIGHTEN-001 revert tripwires were all CLEAR. Investigation: the rule was hardcoded Threshold=0.30 inside DefaultRules() — exactly the post-JIMINY-CORPUS-001 honest raw follow-rate steady state, so it flapped chronically on healthy substrate. Extracted to alert.JiminyFollowRateRules(floor) mirroring the shipped extraction pattern; new JIMINY_FOLLOW_RATE_ALERT_FLOOR config knob (default 0.15, sits below the ~0.30 steady state so only genuine collapse fires; floor≤0 disables). Default rule count 5→4 with test pin. Live-verified: prior fired alert cleared post-restart. New arch rule pinned: when a sprint changes substrate steady state, audit internal/alert/*.go for hardcoded thresholds gating on the affected metric — alert rules are as prone to steady-state-drift as RSIC patterns + Grafana panels. Sprint dir: docs/development/follow-rate-calibrate-001/.

- **Sprint HEBB-ETA-001 — precision-weighted Hebbian η primitives, default-off** (2026-08-01). Q4 stretch tier from research doc `docs/research/mdemg_sprint_ideas/02-precision-weighted-hebbian-eta.md`. Adds per-node ActivationConfidence [0.05, 1.0] computed as `sigmoid(α·log(1+n_reinforce) + β·recency_decay − γ·surprise_variance)`. Both Hebbian Cypher paths (`ApplyCoactivation` + `CoactivateSession`) gain a flag-guarded `precisionMult = coalesce(a.activation_confidence, 0.5) * coalesce(b.activation_confidence, 0.5)` (`1.0` when `PRECISION_WEIGHTED_ETA_ENABLED=false` → byte-identical to pre-sprint). Persists `r.eta_effective_pc` + `r.precision_mult` on the edge (null when flag off). New CLI: `mdemg confidence backfill --space-id <id> [--dry-run] [--batch-size N]` seeds confidence values from graph state (reinforce count via SUM(evidence_count), last_activated via MAX, surprise via node.surprise_score). Startup log `hebb: precision-weighted eta ...`. 7 unit tests. Ships default-off in both code AND `.env` — enabling requires explicit operator intent. Live Tier-3 (mdemg-dev): backfill wrote confidence on 69,655 non-archived nodes; distribution avg 0.515 / median 0.500 / max 0.9998. Two arch rules pinned: (1) behavior-changing flags default off in BOTH code AND .env; (2) flag with backfill prerequisite must document it in config comment + feature doc + startup log. Deferred as follow-ups: live observe→confidence-update wiring, RSIC adaptive-override, Grafana panel row, A/B benchmark harness, integration test, multi-sample surprise history. Feature doc: docs/features/precision-weighted-eta.md. Sprint dir: docs/development/hebb-eta-001/.

- **Sprint LEVER-C-TIGHTEN-001 — Jiminy Lever C tightening for substrate trust + follow-rate** (2026-08-01). Operator correction: J17 avg trust 25.4% + Jiminy actionable-compliance ~14% are substrate-quality problems, not "harmless-by-design" calibration artifacts. Trust is the receiver-side view of guidance quality. 6-agent read-only deep-dive workflow (wf_e576f7f8-625) produced data-decided values from 7d TSDB analysis of mdemg-dev: JIMINY_GUIDANCE_CONSTRAINT_INCLUDE_TOPK code default 5→4 (.env 5→4; live actionable-fraction mean 0.342 vs 0.30 quota → TOPK=5 over-supplied); JIMINY_GUIDANCE_CONSTRAINT_SIM_FLOOR code default 0.30→0.45 (.env 0.70→0.55; 0/257 followed below downstream-sim 0.40; the 0.70 .env was quota-starving, forcing over-reliance on cooldown-fallback which re-surfaces recently-ignored items). Startup log line `slog.Info("jiminy: lever c actionable bias", topk, sim_floor)` added — live-verified topk=4 sim_floor=0.55 at boot. Pin test TestLeverC_ShippedDefaults catches silent future regressions. Live Tier-3 immediately post-restart: /v1/jiminy/latest returned debug.leverc_actionable_merged=4 (was 5), 4 of 6 items typed actionable = 67% actionable share. Two new arch rules pinned: (1) trust is receiver-side quality signal — even under J17_TIER_GATE_MODE=comprehension bypass, low trust means the substrate produces untrustworthy guidance; do NOT frame low trust as harmless-by-design; (2) when tightening a shrink-the-denominator knob, enumerate consumers of downstream SIGNAL DENSITY (not just volume) — Lever C ~20% surface cut → ~20% slower GetConstraintEffectiveness samples-to-confidence (RSIC prune signal watch-item). CLAUDE.md JIMINY-CORPUS-001 note drift caught + corrected inline (claimed TOPK=8/SIM_FLOOR=0.30 but actual was 5/0.70). Feature doc: docs/features/jiminy-actionability.md §Lever-C-Tighten. Sprint dir: docs/development/lever-c-tighten-001/.

Ships: (E1-E3) Guidance Health / Follow Rate / Actionable Compliance Rate panel thresholds recalibrated onto the shipped honest steady-state (~0.14 compliance per JIMINY-CORPUS-001 + JIMINY-ACTIONABILITY-COMPLIANCE-CREDIT-001); Actionable panel renamed with the design-intent number embedded in the title ("Actionable Compliance Rate (expected 10-13% by design)"). (E4) Effectiveness Trends panel refB (`mdemg_jiminy_constraint_effectiveness` — the DASHBOARD-TRUTH-001-flagged lifetime multi-credit gauge) DELETED; only the honest windowed series remains. Two new arch rules pinned: RSIC pattern thresholds MUST be config-extracted when they gate on a metric with a known-honest steady state; panel titles carrying the design-intent number beat prose alone. Feature doc: `docs/features/rsic-guidance-health-floors.md`. Sprint dir: `docs/development/dashboard-truth-003/`.

- **Sprint GO-IMPLEMENTS-001 — Go IMPLEMENTS edge producer via go/types** (2026-07-31). Q3 deferral + Q4 deep-dive candidate #9. Replaces the nil-returning `GoTypesAnalyzer` stub with a real `go/types`-backed analyzer that discovers Go's IMPLICIT interface satisfaction (which tree-sitter fundamentally cannot detect — Go has no `implements` keyword; satisfaction is a method-set property only Go's own type checker can compute). New `internal/symbols/go_types.go::AnalyzeImplements` uses `packages.Load` with full type info on `./...` from `projectRoot`; collects every interface + concrete type; checks `types.Implements` (both direct + pointer-receiver + interface-embeds-interface); filters stdlib packages, vendored/module-cache files, empty interface (`any`), and self-implements pairs; emits `RelationshipRecord{Relation:"IMPLEMENTS", ResolutionMethod:"go_types", Tier:2, Confidence:1.0}`. New CLI `mdemg symbols analyze-go-implements --root <path> --space-id <id>` (with `--dry-run`) invokes the analyzer + writes edges via the existing `SymbolStore.SaveRelationships` path — symbol_ids computed via the same `GenerateSymbolID(spaceID, /relative/path, name, 0)` scheme the tree-sitter ingest uses, so cross-path SymbolNode MATCH clauses resolve. **Live on mdemg-dev**: 96 packages / 109 interfaces / 1464 concretes → 267 pairs discovered in 472ms → **188 IMPLEMENTS edges landed in Neo4j** (2→188). Sample landed edges verified real (`Server IMPLEMENTS DevSpaceServer` etc.). The structural RRF column + edge-attention (IMPLEMENTS weight 0.70) now have real Go data to walk. 5 unit tests (inline Go fixture asserts 5 required pairs + stdlib skip + self-skip + metadata contract; stdlib+vendored+relativizePath helpers). ⚠️ **Three rules pinned**: (1) Go IMPLEMENTS is implicit; tree-sitter CAN'T detect it — do NOT add `queries/go/inheritance.scm`; route through `GoTypesAnalyzer` instead. (2) `relativizePath` MUST produce the leading-slash shape the tree-sitter ingest uses in `SymbolNode.file_path` — a shape mismatch silently drops every edge at `SaveRelationships`' MATCH; pin-tested. (3) Filter the empty interface (`interface{}`/`any`) before pairing — every type satisfies it, so a naive iteration would emit N×M noise edges (~7300 in mdemg's case). Follow-ups disclosed: (a) automatic post-ingest invocation hook if IMPLEMENTS-driven retrieval quality proves out; (b) audit the 79-pair emitted-vs-landed gap; (c) UVTS-style retrieval-quality measurement of "who implements X?" queries before/after. Sprint: `docs/development/go-implements-001/`.
- **Sprint METRICS-VERIFIER-UOBS-UOTS-001 — verifier scans UOBS/UOTS spec paths** (2026-07-31). DORMANT-METRICS-CLEANUP-001 CI-cycle disclosed follow-up. `scripts/verify_metrics_consumers.py` `CONSUMER_ROOTS` extended with `docs/tests/uobs/specs` (runtime metric-presence contracts) + `docs/api/api-spec/uots/specs` (artifact-observability contracts). New auto-promotion step in `generate()`: an existing IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY entry becomes IN_USE the moment a UOBS/UOTS spec references it (one-directional — operator-set DORMANT_* still wins over passive discovery; idempotent). Four inventory entries correctly promoted: `neo4j_graph_total_spaces`, `probe_latency_ms`, `tsdb_pool_empty_acquire_total`, `tsdb_pool_max_conns` — all previously classified TSDB-only but coupled to real observability contracts. Distribution shift: 92 → **96 IN_USE**, 53 → **49 IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY**. Prevents the "orphaned assertion after removal" CI-FAIL class that surfaced during DORMANT-METRICS-CLEANUP-001 (removing `retrieval_cache_hits_total` broke 3 specs asserting its presence). ⚠️ **Rule pinned**: when removing a metric, still scan UOBS/UOTS specs for orphaned assertions in the same PR — the auto-promotion is a signal FOR keeping the metric alive, not a fix for the removal path. ⚠️ **Rule pinned**: auto-promotion is one-directional — operator-set DORMANT_*/IN_USE_SNAPSHOT_ONLY dispositions keep their manual adjudication over passive spec-reference discovery. Sprint: `docs/development/metrics-verifier-uobs-uots-001/`.
- **Sprint DORMANT-METRICS-CLEANUP-001 — remove 7 hard-dead metric declarations** (2026-07-30). DORMANT-CENSUS-003 disclosed follow-up. Reviewed all 60 IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY entries against a two-gate rule (0 `.Field`/`.Method()` writer sites in `internal/**/*.go` AND 0 samples over 7d on mdemg-dev). Seven metrics passed both gates and are removed: `cms_dedup_skips_total`, `cms_learning_edge_failures_total`, `cms_recall_total`, `cms_resume_total`, `jiminy_guide_timeout_total`, `retrieval_cache_hits_total`, `retrieval_cache_misses_total`. Removed 7 `StandardMetrics` fields + 1 factory-function assignment + 6 `r.New…` initializer statements + 1 test-only reference (`metrics_test.go`). Inventory: 152 → 145 declared entries; verifier passes clean. **11 wired-but-quiet metrics kept** as IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY (real error/failure counters that haven't triggered in 7d but would lose signal if removed — `cms_dedup_merge_failures_total`, `cms_embedding_failures_total`, `cms_observe_total`, `cms_stability_update_failures_total`, `cms_writejson_failures_total`, `eventgraph_writer_flush_failure_total`, `guidance_corpus_flush_failure_total`, `guidance_corpus_rows_dropped_total`, `jiminy_warm_errors_total`, `retrieval_column_failed_total`, `rsic_llm_semaphore_blocked_total`). **42 active metrics** are healthy and unchanged. ⚠️ **Rules pinned**: (1) HARD-DEAD verdict requires BOTH zero writer sites AND zero 7d samples — either alone misses cases (sample gate alone false-flags wired-but-quiet error counters; writer gate alone misses dead factories that a `_test.go` happens to keep referencing). (2) When removing a metric declaration, DELETE the inventory entry (metrics differ from TSDB tables here: no historical CREATE-TABLE persists, so REMOVED disposition doesn't apply). (3) When removing a metric, scan `_test.go` for stale references (they don't argue for keeping the metric alive but they'd break the build). Sprint: `docs/development/dormant-metrics-cleanup-001/`.
- **Sprint DORMANT-CENSUS-003 — metrics-registry adjudication registry + drift check** (2026-07-30). DORMANT-CENSUS-002 disclosed follow-up #4. Extends the DORMANT-CENSUS-001 (routes) + DORMANT-CENSUS-002 (TSDB tables) forcing-function pattern to the metrics-registry surface. New `scripts/verify_metrics_consumers.py` enumerates 152 declared metrics via regex over `internal/**/*.go` (counters, gauges, histograms), walks consumer roots (`deploy/docker/grafana/`, `internal/cli/grafana_templates/staged/`, `internal/alert/`, `internal/ape/`, `internal/api/`, `internal/tsdb/`, `internal/consulting/`), adjudicates each against `docs/api/metrics_consumer_inventory.json`. Both DORMANT-CENSUS-002 named-deferral-blockers handled: (a) **histogram-derivative expansion** — the walker searches for base name AND all five derivative names (`_p95`, `_p99`, `_bucket`, `_sum`, `_count`) with/without `mdemg_` prefix, so a consumer that only references `mdemg_retrieval_latency_seconds_p95` correctly hits the base declaration; (b) **snapshot-reader recognition** — files that reference `/v1/metrics/snapshot` are recorded as soft-signal consumers for operator adjudication. Auto-disposition: **IN_USE** (direct grep-hit) or **IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY** (recorder writes to `metric_samples` as the load-bearing consumer). Zero UNREVIEWED on first-pass: 92 IN_USE / 60 IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY across 100 gauges / 42 counters / 10 histograms. Merge-blocking CI step wired alongside the shipped route + TSDB guards. `docs/development/UXTS_FRAMEWORK_MATRIX.md` §5a new "Companion Adjudication Registries" table documents the three-strong DORMANT-CENSUS family as sibling build-time drift checks, complementary to (not overlapping) UOBS/UOTS. ⚠️ **Rules pinned**: (1) When adding a new metric, run `python3 scripts/verify_metrics_consumers.py --generate` locally + commit the inventory update in the same PR — CI fails on missing entries. (2) Histograms produce FIVE derivative names in TSDB; grep-based consumer detection MUST expand each declaration to all variants (this is the deferral-blocker class named by DORMANT-CENSUS-002). (3) The `metric_samples` recorder is a load-bearing consumer of every declared metric; auto-disposition for metrics without direct grep-hits is `IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY`, not `DORMANT_TO_REMOVE` — re-adjudicate only after a downstream review confirms no consumer value. Follow-up disclosed: **DORMANT-METRICS-CLEANUP-001** — future review pass on the 60 IN_USE_TSDB_ONLY entries to identify genuinely-dormant ones for removal. Sprint: `docs/development/dormant-census-003/`.
- **Sprint CORRECTION-CODE-GEN-001 — code-gen for correction nodes + taxonomy pin shift** (2026-07-30). Follow-up to JIMINY-CODE-BACKFILL-001. `role_type='correction'` nodes now carry `constraint_code`. New `BootstrapCorrectionCodes` in `internal/jiminy/service.go` mirrors the shipped `BootstrapCodes` for constraints (queries uncoded correction nodes → `ConstraintCodeGenerator.GenerateCode` LLM path → writes back with `constraint_code_assigned_by="mdemg-bootstrap-correction"`). Wired into the B3 startup goroutine alongside constraint bootstrap. **Live-verified on mdemg-dev: 35/35 correction nodes now have codes** (0/35 pre-sprint). Sample codes: `always-commit-before-goreleaser`, `always-follow-12-section-format`, `always-use-cuidv2` (mnemonic from LLM) + `auto-<hash>` fallbacks. Two downstream lookups widened: `matchConstraintCodeByEmbedding` and `FindConstraintCodeForNode` filter `role_type IN ['constraint', 'correction']` (was constraint-only). Defensive-backfill gate at `RecordOutcome` widened: `item.Type IN (GuidanceConstraint, GuidanceCorrection)`. Taxonomy pin test updated: `GuidanceCorrection` MOVED from `guidanceTypesWithoutCodes` to `guidanceTypesWithCodes` (disjoint partition + every-type-classified invariants preserved). ⚠️ **Rule pinned: when the startup B3 goroutine bootstraps codes across two categories, each phase gets its OWN context** — live-caught the shared-60s-budget bug (11/35 corrections codified on first attempt when constraint phase ran ~50s of shared budget); fixed by giving each phase its own 120s `context.WithTimeout`. Sprint: `docs/development/correction-code-gen-001/`.
- **Sprint JIMINY-CODE-BACKFILL-001 — empty-constraint_code investigation + defensive backfill + taxonomy pin** (2026-07-30). Q4 follow-up #5 / DORMANT-CENSUS-002 follow-up #3 / JIMINY-CEILING-INVESTIGATION-001 follow-up #4. Live diagnosis on mdemg-dev 7d: **221 empty-code rows / 1402 total = 15.8%** — three classes: **Class A (175 rows, 79%)** `pattern`/`concept`/`learning`/`decision` types are EXPECTED empty (source nodes aren't `role_type='constraint'`, no code exists); **Class B (26 rows, 12%)** `constraint`-typed against `emergent_concept`/`leaf` source (target nodes don't have codes either); **Class C (21 rows, 9%)** `correction`-typed against real correction nodes but 0/35 corrections carry codes today (no producer exists). **Zero rows in the 47 non-Class-A "broken" cases would be helped by the constraint_id→constraint_code join spec'd by DORMANT-CENSUS-002** — target nodes lack codes. Ship: (a) `FindConstraintCodeForNode(spaceID, nodeID)` narrow helper in `internal/jiminy/persistence.go` (returns code only for `role_type='constraint'` with non-empty code, fail-open); (b) defensive backfill at `service.go::RecordOutcome` — fires ONLY when `item.Type=GuidanceConstraint AND item.ConstraintCode=""` AND source node_id present, catches the edge case where a constraint-role source reached the writer without its code populated (e.g. concurrent code assignment or Guide()-time embedder failure); (c) `internal/jiminy/empty_code_taxonomy_test.go` pin test with two invariants (disjoint partition + every GuidanceType classified — new types force explicit code-semantic decision, CACHE-KEY-002-shape forcing function). ⚠️ **Rules pinned**: (1) Empty constraint_code is EXPECTED for `guidance_type IN (pattern, concept, learning, decision, correction, preference, risk, conflict)` — do NOT filter these as data holes or try to backfill from Neo4j; the target nodes have no code. (2) Adding a new GuidanceType enum value REQUIRES updating one of the two taxonomy maps in the same PR — pin test forcing function. Follow-up disclosed: **CORRECTION-CODE-GEN-001** — extend `correction_nodes.go` to mint codes at promotion time using the same generator as constraints, backfill 35 existing correction nodes, then move `GuidanceCorrection` from the "without codes" side of the taxonomy to the "with codes" side + update pin. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-code-backfill-001/`.
- **Sprint FT-DORMANT-CLEANUP-001 — DROP the two dormant FT tables** (2026-07-30). Q4 follow-up #6 / DORMANT-CENSUS-002 disclosed follow-up #1. V0032 migration DROPs `ft_benchmarks` (superseded by `benchmark_runs`+`benchmark_results` V0012) and `ft_hitl_decisions` (superseded by `review_grades` V0028). Both verified 0-row live before drop.
