Distinct Service `neo4j-cpu` (was colliding with memory's `neo4j` under the NOSILENT-001 cooldown key). **Live-verified:** during active consolidation the windowed AVG was 447 (<500) → the rule stayed silent, while the old LIMIT-1 rule (latest 377 > 80) would have fired; **0 new CPU alerts in 70+ min**. **(3) LLM error-count floor:** `llm_error_rate_spike` adds `RSIC_LLM_ERROR_MIN_COUNT` (default 5) — the rate-only gate (>5% & >10 calls) re-fired HIGH on a couple of transient errors at low volume. (The remaining truthful firing is `retrieval.rerank_cross` at a real 9.6%/9-error rate — the rerank-cancellation root cause is a separate track.) **(4) Stale NLI bias eliminated:** `GetNLICalibrationReport()` now returns nil when the NLI sidecar isn't operational — a gated-off sidecar left a stale window producing a phantom mean-bias (live: 0.638 at 0 requests) that pinned a continuously-firing `nli_bias_alert`. Gating at the source covers all three consumers (both `j17_nli_mean_bias` gauge emitters + the RSIC calibration-drift insight). **Live-verified:** gauge 0 (0/10 readings non-zero) post-fix. **(5) Latency histogram buckets** extended 10s→120s (added 20/30/60/120) — the set topped out at le=10s, so LLM-backed paths (retrieval includes the rerank; ape.reflect/jiminy.synthesize run 10–76s) clamped the p95/p99 estimate at 10, a permanent red. **(6) Dashboard panel bugs:** overview `P95 Latency` red 0.25s→120s (aligns with the `ALERT_RETRIEVE_P95_MS` SLO); neo4j `Neo4j Memory` 512MiB/1GiB→24GiB/27GiB; `Null-Weight Abstraction Edges` unit percent→short + thresholds 60/85→50/100 (it's a count); `Conversation Coverage Ratio` unit percent→percentunit + inverted thresholds red<0.2/green≥0.4; j17 `Avg Comprehension` calc `mean`→`lastNotNull` (the 0/real flush-interleave dragged the mean to 0.43 vs real 0.858). New config: `NEO4J_CPU_ALERT_THRESHOLD_PERCENT` (500), `RSIC_LLM_ERROR_MIN_COUNT` (5). Build + lint clean; alert/ape/jiminy/metrics suites green. Feature doc: `docs/features/alert-dispatcher.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/alert-truth-001/`.

- **Sprint FT-RECURSIVE-002 (Phase 6b) — the recursive-retrain actuator (shipped default-off; live cycle next)** (2026-06-28). Makes the no-op `trigger_training_pipeline` real — a supervised controller that runs a gated retrain cycle — but **dormant behind `FT_LOOP_ENABLED=false`** (nothing trains or mutates serving state until opt-in). **Ledger:** `ft_training_cycles` (V0002, first writer) — event-sourced state machine (`triggered→curating→training→gating→promote_pending→{promoted|failed|rolled_back}`), DB-backed single-flight + retrain-interval gate that survive restarts. **Trigger gate** (`internal/ftloop`): a cycle launches only on Ready ∧ fresh-fraction (`FT_LOOP_MIN_FRESH_FRACTION` 0.30) ∧ no cycle within `FT_LOOP_MIN_RETRAIN_INTERVAL_HOURS` (168) — **and the SF-2 fix: when disabled/open/within-interval the trigger is SUPPRESSED** (no alert), ending the per-reflection-cycle `rsic-trigger_training_pipeline` spam (live-verified: 0 dispatches post-restart). **Compute lease** (single-host lockfile, reclaimable on expiry so a crashed trainer can't wedge RSIC) **+ `OrchestrationPolicy.Quiesce`** (pauses new RSIC triggers during a retrain) **+ disk-floor preflight.** **Controller:** supervised, walks curate→train→gate as ctx-cancellable Python subprocesses, ledger + `ft-loop:<stage>` jobhealth per stage, FAIL→archived+one alert, lease-expiry/disk-floor→class-4 alert-and-halt, PASS→`promote_pending` (halts — promotion is operator-gated via `mdemg ft-loop promote --cycle-id [--reject]`). `[AMD-7]` `TRAINING_READINESS_THRESHOLD` (+per-task) replaces the hardcoded 500; `[AMD-1]` epoch cap passed to train_ft. New config: `FT_LOOP_ENABLED`, `FT_LOOP_{MIN_RETRAIN_INTERVAL_HOURS,MIN_FRESH_FRACTION,POLL_INTERVAL_SEC,LEASE_MAX_HOURS,LEASE_PATH,MIN_FREE_DISK_GB,PYTHON_BIN,MODEL_VERSION}`, `FT_LORA_EPOCHS_CAP`, `FT_EARLY_STOP_VAL_LOSS_FACTOR`, `TRAINING_READINESS_THRESHOLD(_OVERRIDES)`. Unit tests across ledger/gate/lease/quiesce/controller; config guard 756/756; lint 0. **Epic 6 (2026-06-29/30):** every pipeline stage validated **live against the real system** — export (`mdemg data export`) → curate (`paradigm_router`, 4660-row SFT split) → train (real 168 MB LoRA adapter; loss 3.28→2.41) → convert (`fuse --dequantize`→`convert_hf_to_gguf`→`llama-quantize Q5_K_M`, 11 GB candidate) → gate (`run_benchmark` real calls, no-zero-call) — and the controller **wired with those proven commands** (`controller_stages.go`, PR #492). The orchestration (lease/quiesce/ledger/FAIL) was validated live in "Option A" (a real cycle walked `triggered→curating→failed`, SF-2 held, lease released). **A real bug was caught + fixed along the way:** the base-model SHA drift-guard had rotted (E6-8, PR #489 — upstream `mlx-community/Qwen3-14B-4bit` re-published its config; would've broken any retrain) and the quiesce design was confirmed necessary (E6-12: training saturated the box). 14 findings ledgered (`epic6_issues.md`). Remaining: a single *enabled* tiny-subset integration drill (the heavy convert + slow contended-box train) + Phase 7 (auto-promote/canary). Still **default-off** behind `FT_LOOP_ENABLED`. 12 Epic-6 config knobs (`FT_LOOP_{WORK_DIR,BASE_MODEL,BASE_SHA,UAITS_SPEC,BENCHMARK_CONFIG,LORA_RANK,LORA_ALPHA,GATE_PORT,EXPORT_SINCE_DAYS,GATE_TASK_FILTER,GATE_MIN_SCORE}`). Feature doc: `docs/features/ft-recursive-loop.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/ft-recursive-002/` (incl. `epic6_issues.md`, `run_record.md`).

- **Sprint FT-RECURSIVE-001 (Phase 6a) — harden + instrument the manual retrain path** (2026-06-28). First *build* sprint of the recursive-retraining loop: the manual vertical slice (FT-CLASSIFY-002's path) is now fully observable and the silent-failure seams on it are closed — **no actuator, no autonomy, no serving mutation** (those are 6b/7/9). **(1) SF-3:** the 6 RSIC diagnostic alerts shared `("rsic",Medium)` and mutually suppressed (the dispatcher cooldown key is `(Service,Severity)`) — now each alerts under `rsic-<action>` (live: `rsic-trigger_training_pipeline`, `rsic-alert_llm_health`). **(2) SF-7:** `mdemg data status` now surfaces *why* a task is not training-ready (`insufficient_rows`/`error_rate_high`/`missing_system_prompt`) via a pure unit-tested `evaluateReadinessGates` (live: `jiminy.evaluate: 310 < 500`, `guardrail.evaluate: 3 < 500`). **(3) SF-1:** a heartbeat gauge `mdemg_rsic_readiness_assessed` set on each *successful* assessment + the `training_readiness_stale` evaluator rule (idle-safe, distinct `ft-readiness` service, `FT_READINESS_STALENESS_MIN`=30) catch a silently-dormant loop. **(4) `mdemg ft-loop report-stage`** records each manual stage (capture/curate/train/benchmark/gate/promote) to `scheduled_job_events` (`job_name=ft-loop:<stage>`) via the NOSILENT-001 CLI-job pattern; a failure fires a high alert under the distinct `ft-loop` service (`jobhealth.ReportWithService`). **(5) `[AMD-2]`:** the 16-task augmented promotion-gate eval is pinned as a committed artifact (`docs/development/ft-recursive-001/augmented_eval_manifest.json`: recipe + SHA-256 + per-task spec hashes + leak verdict CLEAN 0/240) instead of an ad-hoc rebuild of a gitignored set. **(6) SF-6 + docstrings:** export archives prune older than `MDEMG_EXPORT_RETENTION_HOURS` (default 168h); `evaluate_ft.py` docstrings `:8101`→`:8102`. All six live-verified Tier-3. New config: `FT_READINESS_STALENESS_MIN`, `MDEMG_EXPORT_RETENTION_HOURS`. **Also corrected stale FT docs** that listed the already-complete FT-CLASSIFY-002 (PR #446) as open. Feature doc: `docs/features/ft-recursive-loop.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/ft-recursive-001/`.

### Fixed

- **ORPHAN-ALERT-001 follow-up — `low_graph_health` false positives (same tiny-space-noise class)** (2026-06-28). The `low_graph_health` rule had the identical defect ORPHAN-ALERT-001 fixed for the orphan rules: `ORDER BY time DESC LIMIT 1` with no significance floor, so the degenerate `global` test space (graph-health 0.0) tripped the 0.5 `lt` threshold while mdemg-dev was healthy (0.995) — firing repeatedly. Extracted it into the floored `alert.OrphanRules` family: MIN health among spaces with `total_nodes >= ORPHAN_RATIO_MIN_NODES`, `COALESCE(...,1.0)` idle-safe, no `ORDER BY … LIMIT 1`. New config `GRAPH_HEALTH_SCORE_FLOOR` (0.5). Live: the rule SQL returns 0.995 (>floor → no fire); `global` excluded by the node floor. DefaultRules count 8→7; alert tests + config guard green.

- **Sprint ORPHAN-ALERT-001 — fix false "High Orphan Ratio" alerts + the is_archived-blind RSIC ratio** (2026-06-26). The graph-health orphan alert fired persistently — but on **1-node UATS/test spaces** (`uats-correct-test` 1/1 = 1.0), not the real substrate (mdemg-dev 693/83034 = **0.8%**, healthy). Three distinct defects: (1) `high_orphan_ratio` used `ORDER BY ratio DESC LIMIT 1` with **no node floor**, so any tiny space where the single node is an orphan tripped 0.10; (2) `high_orphan_count` used `ORDER BY time DESC LIMIT 1` (non-deterministic across spaces) with a threshold (50) below mdemg-dev's accepted ~693 baseline; (3) the RSIC self-assess orphan query (`self_assess.go:297`) was **`is_archived`-blind** — counting the 4,457 archived tombstones inflated `OrphanRatio` to 6.2% vs the true live 1.0% (HIDDEN-CHURN-001 class; the `mdemg_neo4j_graph_orphans` gauge already excluded archived and was correct). **Fix:** extracted both rules to config-parameterized `alert.OrphanRules(minNodes, ratioThreshold, countThreshold)` — per-space gauge join with a `total_nodes >= minNodes` **significance floor** + `COALESCE(MAX(...),0)` deterministic idle-safe aggregation (no `ORDER BY … LIMIT 1`, per the TSDB-CONSUME-001 alert-SQL contract) + distinct Service labels (cooldown-key contract); and the RSIC query now excludes archived from numerator AND denominator. New config: `ORPHAN_RATIO_MIN_NODES` (50), `ORPHAN_RATIO_THRESHOLD` (0.10), `ORPHAN_COUNT_THRESHOLD` (1000). **Live Tier-3:** new ratio SQL returns 0.0083 (<0.10) + count 693 (<1000), both sub-threshold; no orphan alerts fire post-restart; RSIC ratio now 1.02%. The 693 live orphans are accepted historical unclustered observations (EVENTGRAPH-004) — not a data problem. 1 new unit test + DefaultRules count 10→8; alert/ape/config tests + 729/729 config guard green. Sprint: `docs/development/orphan-alert-001/`.

- **Sprint INTENT-DISABLE-001 — disable intent translation (evidence-backed) + fix the 2000ms timeout-default bug** (2026-06-26). `retrieval.intent_translate` (LLM query-rewrite-before-embedding, synchronous + fail-open on the retrieval hot path) was the single largest LLM-error source — **70% of all LLM errors over 7 days, ~15% chronic timeout rate** — driving the recurring `alert_llm_health` spike + HIGH consecutive-failure alerts, and adding avg 3.8s (up to 15s) to retrieval. Operator question: does it actually improve retrieval quality? **UVTS A/B answered it:** quick 16q showed +0.006 (within noise) but the robust **120q full flipped to −0.010** (intent OFF 0.4170 vs ON 0.4070) — **net-negative**. Data-decided: **disable, not tune** (nothing to tune toward when the feature is slower *and* slightly worse). Changes: `.env` `INTENT_ENABLED=false` (shipped default was already false — now evidence-backed); `INTENT_TIMEOUT_MS` default **2000→15000** (2000ms was below avg local-model latency ~4400ms → guaranteed timeouts; violated the ≥15000ms rule; floor stays 200 since intent is fail-open); new `?intent=true|false` retrieve URL override (mirrors `?sparse=`) as the re-verification A/B tool, **UATS-contracted** (2 variants added to `memory_retrieve_sparse_context.uats.json`, hash re-pinned). Translator code retained (re-enableable — re-run the 120q A/B first). **Live Tier-3:** the 120q A/B itself; post-disable restart shows **0 new intent_translate interactions**; UATS 7/7 live; config guard 726/726; build/lint clean. Sprint: `docs/development/intent-disable-001/`.

- **Sprint EMBED-CALLSITE-001 — close the metaless-embed attribution gap (persistent `alert_embedding_regression`)** (2026-06-26). The RSIC zero-tolerance check #28 (`self_reflect.go:526`) fired a CRITICAL `alert_embedding_regression` every reflection cycle. Investigation: the check counts `embedding_events` rows with empty `call_site` (24h window); ~4k/day in `mdemg-dev`, **0/day before 2026-06-23** then 1,536→2,024→4,125 (06-23/24/25) — a real attribution gap whose onset matched the JIMINY-ACTIONABILITY-001 / Lever C work driving `Guide()` volume. Root cause: the embedding recorder reads its `call_site` from a context-attached `EmbeddingMeta`; a **full audit of every `.Embed`/`.EmbedBatch` in `internal/`** found exactly 3 metaless recorder-wired sites — `jiminy/service.go::deduplicateItems` (used `context.Background()`, re-embeds every guidance item per `Guide()` — the dominant producer) and the two context-fingerprint embeds in `derive`/`getOrBuild` (reached via `deriveQueryFingerprint`). **Fix:** attach `EmbeddingMeta{CallSite:…, SpaceID:…}` to all three (`jiminy.dedup`, `context_fingerprint`); CLI embed paths wire no recorder so they were correctly excluded. The zero-tolerance check is left intact — it's a valid guard that did its job. **Live Tier-3:** rebuilt binary, restarted server, drove 4 `Guide()` + 2 `?context=auto` retrieves → 325 new rows, **0 empty call_sites**, `context_fingerprint` (242) + `jiminy.dedup` (18) now attributed. **Data hygiene:** pre-fix empties in the 24h window relabeled to the sentinel `legacy-unattributed` (4,168 rows, non-destructive reversible UPDATE) → window now 0, alert clears next cycle; older empties in compressed chunks (outside the window) age out via 90d retention. 1 new unit test + signature update; `jiminy`/`api` suites green; lint 0. Feature doc: `docs/features/embedding-attribution.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/embed-callsite-001/`.

- **Sprint GRAFANA-AUDIT-002 — dashboard re-audit + new-metric panels** (2026-06-25). Re-ran the GRAFANA-AUDIT-001 harness (`scripts/grafana_panel_audit.py`) against the current live TSDB after ~10 metric-changing sprints. **Phase 1 (correctness):** 139 PASS / 7 EMPTY / **0 FAIL** / 18 SKIP — no broken queries (EMPTY down from audit-001's 17). **Phase 2 (improve):** fixed `overview/Request Latency Distribution` (dropped its dead `_p50` target — the recorder emits only `_p95`/`_p99`, so `_p50` was a permanently-blank series); added panels for new operator-valuable gauges that had none — **Surfaced Actionable Fraction** (`mdemg_jiminy_surfaced_actionable_fraction`/`_abstraction_fraction`) on `mdemg-jiminy`, **Null-Weight Abstraction Edges** (`mdemg_neo4j_graph_null_weight_edges`) + **Conversation Coverage Ratio** (`mdemg_neo4j_conversation_coverage_ratio`) on `mdemg-neo4j`. Post re-audit: **143 PASS / 6 EMPTY / 0 FAIL** (the 3 new panels confirmed PASS by name). The 6 remaining EMPTY are documented legit-empty (no in-window events) except one disclosed no-producer gap: `ft-training/Entropy Health` reads `llm_interactions.quality`, which has no writer (a metric-instrumentation follow-up, not a dashboard fix). Sprint: `docs/development/grafana-audit-002/`.

- **Sprint DASHBOARD-FIXES-001 — browser-dashboard (`:9999/ui`) tab correctness** (2026-06-25). Fixes the 10 pre-existing bugs the HITL-REVIEW-001 UI audit surfaced across the tabs (all endpoints returned 200; the bugs were client-side field mismatches + display-logic errors + a couple server response-field gaps). 🔴 **backup** read `created`/`size`/`space_id` but `/v1/backup/list` returns `created_at`/`size_bytes`/`spaces` (Created/Size/Space columns always blank) and filtered Restore on a non-existent `status` field (dropdown always empty) — fixed the field names + the handler now emits a response-time `status="completed"` (a listed manifest is always a completed artifact), with the **UATS `backup_list` spec** extended to assert `status` + the UI-depended fields (hash re-pinned). 🔴 **learning** sourced the frozen flag from `/v1/learning/freeze/status` (no top-level `.frozen`) → frozen space showed "active"; now reads `/v1/learning/stats` `freeze_state.frozen`. 🔴 **rsic** State badge rendered red when healthy (`'running'` not in `dom.js`'s green set) — added `running`/`completed`. 🟡 **plugins** Details label stuck (render-time ternary) + refetched on hide → toggles in-handler, fetches on show only. 🟡 **memory** surfaced `observation_count` + rendered the `memoryDistribution` subscription that was fetched-and-discarded (graph phase + edge count + phase alerts). 🟡 **rsic** immediate poll on tab mount (was "unknown" for up to 10s). 🟢 **status** State badge reflects `degraded`; per-subsystem `healthz.checks` surfaced. 🟢 **config** removed a dead double-assigned `onchange`. 🟢 **features** populated `config_key` for the features with a real enable flag (embeddings/anomaly_detection/hidden_layer/scraper); always-on cores honestly stay empty. 🟢 **training** renders `result.tables` explicitly. **Live Playwright:** 7 new `TestDashboardFixes001` assertions (backup columns + restore dropdown, learning freeze badge, rsic green-when-running, plugins label toggle, memory observations, no console errors) — all green against the live server; full browser-ui suite regression-checked. Sprint: `docs/development/dashboard-fixes-001/`.

- **Sprint HIDDEN-CHURN-003 — incremental hidden-layer clustering closes the CHURN-002 residual churn** (2026-06-25). HIDDEN-CHURN-002 was a partial fix (100%→~25% per-cycle identity churn); the residual was structural — the hidden step re-clustered all ~52k L0 nodes from scratch every cycle, so KMeans jitter + non-deterministic LLM reclassification (`RECLASS_ENABLED`) reshuffled membership and ~25% of patterns failed to match. **Fix:** the default consolidation hidden step is now `IncrementalHiddenNodes` (`HIDDEN_INCREMENTAL_ENABLED=true`) — fetch only **orphan** L0 nodes (no `GENERALIZES`→pattern edge), assign each to its nearest existing pattern (`nearestPatternByCentroid`, cosine ≥ `HIDDEN_INCREMENTAL_ASSIGN_SIM_THRESHOLD` 0.80, with a `GENERALIZES` edge + incremental-mean centroid update), and cluster only the unassigned remainder into new CUIDv2 patterns. **Existing patterns are never destroyed or re-clustered.** **Live Tier-3 (mdemg-dev):** **100% identity survival** across cycles (4484/4484, vs CHURN-002's 75%); patterns only added, never destroyed (4484→4758); steady-state cycle wall-time **~10s** (82 orphans) vs the ~360s full re-cluster — the Neo4j High CPU driver drops with it. Full re-cluster retained as the explicit `mdemg concepts recluster --space-id` command (consolidate `full_recluster` field → `hidden.WithFullRecluster`). Trade-off: incremental-only lets pattern count grow slowly (no periodic auto re-cluster) — `concepts recluster` is the quality-maintenance escape hatch; `HIDDEN_INCREMENTAL_ENABLED=false` reverts to the CHURN-002 full path. New config: `HIDDEN_INCREMENTAL_ENABLED` (true), `HIDDEN_INCREMENTAL_ASSIGN_SIM_THRESHOLD` (0.80). 3 unit + 1 integration test; build/lint/config-guard clean. Feature doc: `docs/features/hidden-pattern-identity.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/hidden-churn-003/`.

- **Sprint HIDDEN-CHURN-002 — hidden-pattern identity churn + the consolidation-never-completes silent failure** (2026-06-24). The L1 hidden-pattern layer (`role_type='hidden'`) was destroyed and recreated every consolidation cycle with fresh `randomUUID()` ids (~2,636 nodes + ~31,106 `GENERALIZES` edges), firing the CRITICAL `graph_node_drop` alert and orphaning every Hebbian/abstraction edge referencing a pattern each cycle — the bug class HIDDEN-CHURN-001 fixed for `conversation_theme` but never applied to `hidden`. **Fix:** `CreateHiddenNodes` now matches clusters to existing patterns and updates them in place (node_id survives), deletes only patterns matched by no cluster, and mints **CUIDv2** ids (`detachBaseNodeHiddenEdges` wipe-all removed). **Live Tier-3 surfaced a deeper latent defect** (operator-approved scope expansion): the consolidation pipeline re-clusters ~52k+ L0 nodes (~10 min) but ran under the caller's **cancellable** request context (`r.Context()`), so every trigger timed out and aborted it mid-write (`context canceled`; **236× consolidate-500 vs 58 200**) — it **never reached `deleteUnmatched`**. Fixed by detaching the cycle from caller cancellation (`context.WithoutCancel` + `CONSOLIDATE_TIMEOUT_MS`, default 30 min). Identity matching uses **member-overlap (Jaccard, primary) + centroid-cosine (fallback)** with deterministic L0 ordering (`fetchAllBaseNodes ORDER BY node_id` → reproducible KMeans). **Result (live):** consolidation completes for the first time; id churn **100% → ~25%/cycle** (survival 3093/4144); ids CUIDv2; total-node gauge oscillation **±2,676 → ±780**. **Known residual:** ~25% per-cycle churn remains, structural — non-deterministic LLM category reclassification (`RECLASS_ENABLED`) + full re-clustering of a growing L0 set; closing to ~95% needs incremental clustering → **HIDDEN-CHURN-003**. New config: `HIDDEN_PATTERN_MEMBER_JACCARD_THRESHOLD` (0.5), `HIDDEN_PATTERN_IDENTITY_SIM_THRESHOLD` (0.90), `CONSOLIDATE_TIMEOUT_MS` (1800000). 10 unit + 1 integration test; build/lint/config-guard (720/720) green. Feature doc: `docs/features/hidden-pattern-identity.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/hidden-churn-002/`.

- **Sprint RSIC-LLM-ALERT-GUARD-001 — false `Jiminy Service Unavailable` CRITICAL (the LLM reflector's unguarded second producer)** (2026-06-24). A CRITICAL `jiminy / Jiminy Service Unavailable` alert kept re-firing ~3–6/day **while `/healthz` reported `jiminy: ok`** — a genuine re-fire (distinct fresh timestamps, 1:1 with fresh dispatch log lines), not stale surfacing. Root cause: `alert_jiminy_critical` is produced by **two** independent paths. The rule-based reflector (guarded by JIMINY-SIGNAL-001) was correctly silent; the **LLM reflector** had the action in its `AllowedLLMActions` whitelist with **no `JiminyHealthy` cross-check** — fed the whole assessment JSON (including unrelated noise), it **hallucinated** a Jiminy CRITICAL even with `jiminy_healthy=true`, and `deduplicateInsights` admitted it (the rule-based path raised nothing to dedup against) while the planner allows critical actions at low confidence. JIMINY-SIGNAL-001 only guarded the rule-based path; the LLM whitelist was an unaccounted-for second entry point. Fix (durable, class-closing): (1) **removed** the 3 deterministic threshold-gated alerts (`alert_jiminy_critical`/`alert_memory_bloat`/`alert_synergy_overlap`) from `AllowedLLMActions` — the rule-based reflector owns them from real metrics, so zero coverage lost and the LLM is no longer even offered them; (2) a **structural merge guard** (`deterministicAlertActions` in `deduplicateInsights`) drops any LLM-originated deterministic alert regardless of the whitelist, preserving rule-based copies. General lesson: any deterministic CRITICAL the LLM reflector can emit must be cross-checked against the ground-truth signal the rule-based path uses. 3 new unit tests (incl. the exact production hallucination reproduced + dropped); 2 existing tests updated for the 20→17 whitelist count; `ape` suite green; lint 0; CI ULTS `--verify-hashes` passes (hashed prompt segment unchanged). Sprint: `docs/development/rsic-llm-alert-guard-001/`.

### Added

- **JIMINY-ACTIONABILITY-001 Epic 5 — Lever C (constraint-inclusion): the actionable-composition mover** (2026-06-25). The Epic-4 A/B proved Levers A/B couldn't move the surfaced *composition* because retrieval surfaces no `constraint`/`correction` candidates for most contexts. Lever C (`JIMINY_GUIDANCE_CONSTRAINT_BIAS_ENABLED`, default false) runs `fetchActionableCandidates` — a `vector.similarity.cosine` query over the **role-filtered** actionable partition (top `JIMINY_GUIDANCE_CONSTRAINT_INCLUDE_TOPK`=5, cosine ≥ `JIMINY_GUIDANCE_CONSTRAINT_SIM_FLOOR`=0.30) — and merges those nodes (dedup by node_id) into `Guide()`'s pool, correctly typed (the query filters role_type, sidestepping the discovered adapter gap where `RetrieveForJiminy` drops role_type and mis-types all retrieval items as `learning`). **Controlled live A/B: surfaced actionable fraction 11.1% → 47.7%** (4.3×), abstraction 88.9% → 52.3% — **clears the ≤60%-abstraction milestone** Lever A couldn't move; surfaced constraints query-relevant (`ab_results.md`). **RRF-SCALE-001-safe** (`leverc_score_audit.md`): gates the cosine `sim` ([0,1] stable), never the RRF `Score`; no hardcoded literal; consulting score-floors untouched. ⚠️ Live-smoke fix mid-sprint: the index-scan form (`db.index.vector.queryNodes` top-50 then role-filter) returned 0 because actionables are ~0.1% of nodes; the role-filtered cosine query is required. Also folded in the Epic-1 reusable A/B harness (`scripts/jiminy_actionability_ab.py`) + the missing `post.md`. Ships default-off; operator recommendation: enable Lever C + Lever B. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-actionability-001/`.

- **Sprint JIMINY-ACTIONABILITY-001 — surface bias + directive synthesis toward the actionable class** (2026-06-24; the near-term surfacing lever from JIMINY-RELEVANCE-001's diagnostic). Guidance is ignored because it is *not actionable* (the abstraction class `pattern`/`learning`/`concept` is ~90% of guidance, ignored 54–66%; the actionable class `constraint`/`correction` is ~10%, ignored 27–35%). Two independent, default-off, config-driven levers bias what `Guide()` surfaces and how it is phrased. **Lever A (surface-composition reweighting):** per-type sort weight (`JIMINY_SURFACE_ACTIONABLE_WEIGHT`), min-actionable quota (`JIMINY_SURFACE_MIN_ACTIONABLE`/`_FRACTION`) reserving slots before the cut to `max_items`, abstraction cap (`JIMINY_SURFACE_MAX_ABSTRACTION_FRACTION`) — all no-ops at defaults (byte-identical to the prior truncation; actionable never dropped). **Lever B (directive synthesis):** `JIMINY_DIRECTIVE_SYNTHESIS_ENABLED` augments the synthesis system prompt to render abstraction evidence as imperative directives ("Do X", "You MUST…"), bounded by `JIMINY_DIRECTIVE_SYNTHESIS_MAX_PROMPT_TOKENS`, reusing the existing `jiminy.synthesize` call. New surfaced-composition gauge `mdemg_jiminy_surfaced_actionable_fraction`/`_abstraction_fraction`. **Live A/B (Epic 4):** Lever B works (imperative narratives — "it is imperative… You MUST clean up… You MUST NOT delete…"); Lever A is mechanically correct but modest (surfaced actionable fraction 6.7%→10.5%) because the binding constraint is **upstream retrieval candidate composition** — retrieval surfaces no actionable candidates for most contexts, which the quota cannot manufacture. **Finding:** biasing retrieval toward actionable nodes (role-scoped boost) is the higher-leverage follow-up → **jiminy-actionability-002**. Ships default-off; operator recommendation is to enable Lever B. 7 unit tests; build/lint/config-guard green. Feature doc: `docs/features/jiminy-actionability.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-actionability-001/`.

- **Sprint HITL-REVIEW-001 — human-in-the-loop dataset review + live reinforcement** (2026-06-24, v0.11.0; the coordinated pair with JIMINY-RELEVANCE-001). A general-purpose, dataset-agnostic tool for a human SME to review curated datasets against a reproducible rubric, certify them (gold), optionally **reinforce the LIVE cognitive substrate auditably + reversibly**, and capture corrective examples that become training data. **Built once against a generic interface** — `ReviewableDataset` (`FetchCandidates`/`FetchItem`/`Rubric`/`Sink`) + a `Registry`; the platform (versioned anchored rubric engine, active+stratified sampler, `/v1/review/*` endpoints, persistence, UI) is written once and any dataset plugs in. **Persistence:** `review_grades` hypertable (V0028, schema 27→28) — `gold_score`, `gold_dimensions`, `grader_id`, `rubric_version`, `reinforcement_applied`/`reinforcement_detail` (the reversal payload), `reversed`/`reverses_grade_id`, plus `suggested_guidance` (V0029, schema 28→29) for SME corrective examples. **Endpoints** (`/v1/review/{datasets,next,grade,reverse}`, admin-gated): grade flow = validate→score→dry-run-preview-or-idempotency(409)→optional reinforce→persist+audit; reverse restores prior state + writes a reversal row. **The headline `GuidanceSink`:** a certified guidance grade moves **live J17 trust** (the JIMINY-EFFECTIVENESS-001 EMA) + **node confidence**, capturing prior state so reversal restores it exactly — opt-in per submission (`REVIEW_REINFORCE_DEFAULT=false`), idempotent, dry-run-previewable. **Datasets registered:** the Guidance Corpus (live-reinforcing, with an SME-context-enriched item view — auto-verdict + confidence + provenance — and corrective capture) + **all 16 MDEMG LLM call sites** (`ape.reflect`, `consulting.classify`, `jiminy.*`, `hidden.*`, `retrieval.*`, `guardrail.evaluate`, …) as gold-only datasets reading `llm_interactions`, each with an LLM-output rubric + a 'more info' description. **UI:** a functional Review tab in `:9999/ui` (vanilla JS) — dataset picker + ⓘ panel, rubric form, dry-run preview, reinforce, reverse, progress counter; Playwright e2e 5/5. **Live-verified operator-driven:** guidance reinforce→reverse (trust 0.297→0.367→restored, confidence 0.41→0.46→0.41); 2 real SME grades on the Guardrail-Evaluate LLM dataset persisted with correct rubric math; corrective `suggested_guidance` captured. 13 `REVIEW_*` config knobs. Follow-ups: `hitl-review-002` (GUIDANCE_OUTCOME edge sink, SFT/DPO sinks, multi-grader consensus); the corrective corpus feeds `jiminy-actionability-001` + the recursive-retraining loop. Feature doc: `docs/features/hitl-review.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/hitl-review-001/`.

- **Sprint JIMINY-RELEVANCE-001 — guidance training corpus: collect, label, measure, curate** (2026-06-24, v0.11.0; the collection+measurement infrastructure for the operator-decided 3–6 month effort to raise Jiminy follow-rate toward >90%). Built on the Step-1 diagnostic (`docs/development/jiminy-relevance-001/diagnostic_ignored_population.md`): MDEMG could not retrain its guidance because the **training evidence did not exist** — verdicts were persisted everywhere (`constraint_outcomes`, Neo4j `GUIDANCE_OUTCOME`) but the surfaced-guidance text + the agent `action_summary` were classified then **discarded**, so every `(guidance → action → did-they-follow)` triple was unrecoverable; ~51% of the labels kept were heuristic noise; and ">90%" is partly the wrong target (much guidance is correctly-ignored advisory). **Epic 1 — capture:** new TSDB hypertable `guidance_training_rows` (V0027, schema 26→27) + buffered async writer persists the evidence per feedback item (guidance content + action text + source role/layer via a bounded Neo4j lookup + verdict + classifier_source; CUIDv2 `row_id`; 365d retention/30d compression; never blocks the hot path). Live: a real guide→feedback cycle landed 6 fully-populated rows. **Epic 2 — label quality:** a supervised periodic auto-relabel job (`job_name='guidance-audit'`) re-classifies heuristic/blank rows in place with the LLM classifier (only upgrades to real `llm`/`tier1`/`explicit` verdicts, never downgrades), with gauge `mdemg_guidance_corpus_heuristic_label_fraction`. Live: upgraded 12/12 heuristic rows → gauge 0 (corpus 100% real-labelled). **Epic 4 — honest measurement:** the "should-follow follow rate" alert rule (`guidance_should_follow_rate_low`, unique Service, aggregate+COALESCE idle-safe, reads `time`) + Grafana panel on `mdemg-jiminy` compute follow-rate over the ACTIONABLE types (`constraint`/`correction`) only, excluding correctly-ignored advisory. Live: SQL returns one non-NULL row (0.222 over real data; 1.0 on an idle window). **Epic 5 — curation:** `mdemg data curate-guidance` builds a versioned, leak-audited, distribution-summarized corpus + manifest from the evidence table, preferring gold labels (HITL-REVIEW-001 `review_grades`, when present) over auto-labels. Live: 103-row corpus, label_source {llm:74, tier1:23, explicit:6}, leak audit clean. **Epic 3 (human review) lands with HITL-REVIEW-001** (the guidance corpus is its first reviewable dataset). The retrain is an explicit FUTURE-TRIGGER (FT Phases 6/7/9 + FT-CLASSIFY-002), not built here; the near-term surfacing lever is the sibling `jiminy-actionability-001`. 12 new config knobs (`GUIDANCE_CORPUS_*`, `GUIDANCE_AUDIT_*`, `GUIDANCE_SHOULD_FOLLOW_*`); all gates green (build, unit, lint 0, config-scanner, TSDB schema-version CI). Feature doc: `docs/features/guidance-training-corpus.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-relevance-001/`.

- **Sprint JIMINY-EFFECTIVENESS-001 — trust as a recoverable signal (J17 T1 unblocker)** (2026-06-23, second of the two guidance sprints; built on the honest signals from JIMINY-SIGNAL-001). **Premise corrected by live recon before any code:** the approved plan assumed the agent was penalized for ignoring `confidence:0` junk, but surfaced guidance is filtered above `JiminyMinConfidence=0.3` (confidence 0.7–0.9) and the **LLM** classifier (not the heuristic) judges ~79% `Ignored` — the real J17-T1 blocker is the trust mechanic, not junk guidance. Re-scoped (operator-approved) to **trust as a recoverable EMA**. `TrustScorer.RecordOutcome` was a **monotonic ratchet** (Followed +0.05 / Ignored −0.02): 1,445 mostly-ignored outcomes drove the live session's trust to the **0.0 floor** with no recovery path — it could never climb back to the 0.75 T1 threshold even if recent guidance improved. Replaced with an **EMA** toward per-outcome effectiveness anchors (`trust ← trust + α·(target − trust)`; Followed=1.0, Partial=0.6, Ignored=0.2, Contradicted=0.0): trust now **tracks recent effectiveness and recovers** — a floored session climbs past 0.75 once guidance is followed, while a genuinely all-ignored session converges to ~0.2 (honestly low, correctly < 0.75). It does NOT fake promotion — T1 still requires trust > 0.75 = genuinely-effective guidance, so actual promotion awaits the guidance-relevance work (Option B, disclosed follow-up); this sprint makes trust able to *honestly reflect and recover with* effectiveness instead of being permanently pinned by history. Config `JIMINY_TRUST_MODE` (`ema` default, `ratchet` rollback) + `JIMINY_TRUST_EMA_ALPHA` (0.1); forward-only — existing Neo4j scores self-heal. **Tier 1:** 7 EMA unit tests (recovers-off-floor, steady-ignored-converges-to-~0.2-not-0, tracks-recent-regime, ema-is-default) + legacy ratchet tests pinned to `Mode:ratchet`. **Tier 3 (live):** the actual floored session (`score=0.0` over 1,445 feedbacks) was sent one real `Followed` via the live `POST /v1/jiminy/feedback` wire and moved **0.0 → 0.10** in live Neo4j — the EMA signature `0.1·(1.0−0)`, proving the deployed binary recovers the previously-pinned session off the floor. Build + lint + config-scanner (690/690) clean. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-effectiveness-001/`.

- **Sprint JIMINY-SIGNAL-001 — honest Jiminy health signals** (2026-06-23, P0; first of two sprints from the guidance-not-reaching-agent investigation). Two Jiminy health signals were lying in opposite directions and between them hid a real problem while raising a false alarm. **(1) False-positive CRITICAL** — "Jiminy Pipeline Critical — guidance not reaching agent" fired ~8×/day on a healthy, delivering Jiminy. It is not a delivery check; it's an RSIC reflection insight gated on `!report.JiminyHealthy`, set only inside a synergy block that is conditionally skipped — when skipped, the Go zero-value `false` (= "down") fired the CRITICAL. Added `report.SynergyAssessed` (true only when the block runs) + guarded the insight; renamed the alert to "Jiminy Service Unavailable — catastrophic-forgetting risk" (its real purpose is a presence guard). Live Tier 3 caught a second instance one layer below — `ReadSynergyMetrics` defaulted `JiminyHealthy` to `false` when `jiminyCheck` was nil — fixed by defaulting to healthy (real outages are covered by `/healthz` + the watchdog). **Verified: 0 fires post-fix.** **(2) Inflated follow-rate gauge** — `mdemg_jiminy_follow_rate` read 0.725 vs the dashboard panels' ~0.24 because the Neo4j `count(DISTINCT CASE WHEN followed THEN guidance_id)/count(DISTINCT guidance_id)` double-credits multi-outcome guidance_ids. New `DatasetProvider.GuidanceEffectiveness` reads `constraint_outcomes` TSDB with the panels' exact math over a config window (`RSIC_GUIDANCE_EFFECTIVENESS_WINDOW_HOURS`, default 168=7d); `Assessor.applyHonestFollowRate` overrides the rate. Live Tier 3 caught that the gauge has **two publishers** (the assessment path + the 15s `live_collectors.go` Prometheus collector) — both now apply the override. **Verified: gauge 0.732 → 0.235, matching the TSDB panel (0.2353).** Making the gauge honest correctly LOWERS RSIC's guidance-health dimension (it was masking the real ~24% effectiveness). Build + lint + config-scanner (688/688) clean; 3 guard unit tests. Carried forward: **JIMINY-EFFECTIVENESS-001** (the real fix — stop surfacing irrelevant guidance + don't decay trust on empty guidance; the J17 T1 unblocker), now validated against these honest signals. Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-signal-001/`.

- **Sprint BASELINE-RECOMPUTE-001 — honest adapter-promotion baseline** (2026-06-15, training-integrity remediation capstone). The promotion gate's baseline was a stale frozen constant **0.8338** (computed on the 99%-leaked `valid_golden` eval, under old length-biased/mismatched rewards, served via decommissioned `mlx_lm.server`). Recomputed through the **fixed harness** — leak-free `valid_clean` + RC-001/002 corrected rewards + GGUF llama-server :8102 — the honest baseline is **0.8655** (deterministic rewards, judge off; `status: ok`, 12 tasks, 50 samples/task, 0 zero-call). **The two numbers are not comparable** (different eval + rewards + serving); future retrains compare against 0.8655. `regression.py::evaluate_gate_5a` now **derives the target from the loaded baseline REPORT** (single source of truth) rather than the frozen constant — which is retained only as a >5pp drift tripwire (0.8338 → 0.8655). Also fixed the stale `rl_phase11.yaml mlx_port 8101→8102` (EVAL-INTEGRITY-001 follow-up). Per-task: jiminy.codegen 1.00, jiminy.evaluate 0.967, hidden.name_emergence 0.95, …, ape.reflect 0.696 (the last dragged by `json_valid=0.24` — an eval-harness artifact: the benchmark replays stored ~7.5k-token ape.reflect prompts straight to :8102, bypassing the runtime APE-PROMPT-BUDGET-001 bound, so they truncate; not a model regression — fresh ape.reflect is 100% valid live). Recompute used `--rows-per-spec 10` after the full run proved impractically slow (single-threaded client + long-output gens); disclosed follow-up: add client-side concurrency to `run_benchmark.py` (~4× faster) + optionally a full-corpus recompute. The training-integrity arc (EVAL-INTEGRITY → REWARD-CORRECTNESS-001/002 → APE-PROMPT-BUDGET → DATAPRUNE → BASELINE-RECOMPUTE) is now complete: trustworthy gate, correct rewards, sound corpus, honest baseline. Sprint: `docs/development/baseline-recompute-001/`.

- **Sprint REWARD-CORRECTNESS-002 — schema/reward-mismatch fixes** (2026-06-15, training-integrity remediation; the REWARD-CORRECTNESS-001 live-findings follow-ups). Three reward/schema mismatches that graded CORRECT responses wrong: (1) **hidden.summarize** ULTS schema `object`→`string` — production emits bare prose (`cluster_summarizer.go`), so the object schema mis-flagged 72 valid summaries as invalid-JSON (prose now validates 1.0 vs 0.0); (2) **`explanation_quality` schema-aware** — jiminy.evaluate / jiminy.evaluate_llm nest reasoning in `violations[].reasoning`, not a top-level field, so the flat lookup scored every correct response 0.0; now credits nested reasoning and treats a valid no-violation verdict as a correct "no issues" answer, with a flat fallback; (3) **keyword-bag `specificity_score`/`actionability_score` substantive-floored** — a valid non-hedging response floors at 0.7, indicator words are a bounded bonus, hedging/empty/repetition stay low (`follow_rate` inherits); preserves specific-beats-generic ordering while no longer dropping valid concise guidance for lacking ~6 magic words. **Live Tier 3** (real production rows, old→new kept@0.8): jiminy.evaluate 0/60→60/60 (mean 0.667→0.967), jiminy.synthesize 3/60→59/60 (0.725→0.879), ape.reflect 47/60→60/60 (0.848→0.956), evaluate_llm unchanged 60/60. New means 0.88–0.97 = correct production output scoring correctly, no over-inflation. 87 reward unit tests + 609 neural tests + ruff green. Unblocks the honest baseline recompute (corpora now sound). Sprint: `docs/development/reward-correctness-002/`.

- **Neo4j space hygiene + 2 space-tool bug fixes** (2026-06-14, operator-requested clean slate for live testing, DATAPRUNE line). Removed **24 junk/test/demo Neo4j spaces** (~143k nodes; export-backed-up first to `.mdemg-backup-<ts>/spaces-backup/`): demo/dormant spaces (lnl-demo-whk 51,483, whk-wms 49,470, linear 10,032 dormant, ubts-load 6,019), symbol-only test spaces (e2e-test 10,918, whk-wms-test, uats-snapshot-test), template-artifact invalid space-ids ({{SPACE_ID}}, ${test_space}, uats-batch-{{$timestamp}}, ${test_space}:templates, uats-ingest-{{$timestamp}}), and assorted uats-*/ubts-*/test spaces. Retained: mdemg-dev, demo, mdemg, mdemg-codebase, global. The blank/null-space_id nodes were resolved as **global infrastructure** (SchemaMeta/SchemaMigration/Migration/SignalState/InterviewPrompt/ScrapedContent/ScrapeJob/CapabilityGap — all queried without space_id by design, retained null) plus **155 empty J17/J12 test MemoryNodes** (junk, backed up + staged for deletion). **Two real bugs fixed:** (1) `mdemg space delete` gated its pre-check on `count(MemoryNode)` but deletes all labels, so SymbolNode-only spaces (e2e-test = 10,918 SymbolNodes) silently survived with "no nodes"; pre-check now counts all labels. (2) `mdemg space list` panicked (`sid.(string)` on a nil space_id) when any MemoryNode had null space_id; the query now excludes null/empty space_id (non-space nodes) and the assertion is nil-guarded. Disclosed follow-up: the template-artifact spaces came from UATS/UBTS runs with unsubstituted template variables — the runners should reject placeholder space_ids. Record: `docs/development/dataprune-audit-001/space_cleanup.md`.

- **DATAPRUNE-AUDIT-001 Category B + C prune** (2026-06-14, training-integrity remediation; operator-approved continuation of #463). **Category B:** removed **21,254 error / silent-failure rows** from `llm_interactions` via `mdemg data clean` across 4 spaces (mdemg-dev 20,856, lnl-demo-whk 271, whk-wms 126, demo 1), backed up first to `.mdemg-backup-<ts>/dataprune_BC/B_errorrows.csv` (267M). Verification catch: the `data clean` dry-run's per-task table is the *surviving* good-row count (`fillPerTaskCounts`), not the delete set — the real delete count is the JSON `errors_found + silent_fails_found`, cross-checked against direct SQL before `--force`. **Category C:** a reference scan changed scope — only the rerank mislabeled pre-fix archive (6,894 events / 21M, **no code references**) was pruned (moved to backup, reversible); `valid_golden.jsonl` (9 references incl. leak-source role) and the ~14 `baseline_*.json` (referenced by `regression.py`/`run_benchmark.py`/configs) are **stale but load-bearing** and were **retained** — their retirement is the baseline-recompute sprint's job, after the regression harness + leak-filtering move off them. **Final state:** `llm_interactions` 79,461 rows, **0 non-conforming** (0 error/empty + 0 invalid-JSON after Category A). All deletions backed up + reversible. The training-integrity remediation arc (EVAL-INTEGRITY-001 → REWARD-CORRECTNESS-001 → APE-PROMPT-BUDGET-001 → DATAPRUNE) is substantially complete; remaining: retire the stale-but-referenced artifacts during baseline recompute, and the schema/reward-mismatch fixes. Sprint: `docs/development/dataprune-audit-001/bc_execution.md`.

- **Sprint DATAPRUNE-AUDIT-001 — prune non-conforming training data (audit-first)** (2026-06-13, training-integrity remediation; the operator's standing data-cleanup directive, executed after the three correctness fixes defined "conforming"). Non-destructive audit enumerated all non-conforming TSDB/file data, then pruned the operator-approved corrupt-JSON category. **Audited targets:** (A) invalid-JSON rows in object/array tasks, (B) 21,135 error/empty rows, (C) rerank mislabeled archive (6,894 events/21M) + leaked `valid_golden` (108) + ~14 stale April baselines; with an explicit **NOT-targets** list — schema/reward mismatches (hidden.summarize prose-vs-object, jiminy.evaluate) where the data is fine and the *definition* is wrong. **Pruned (Category A, backup-first):** the raw `pg_input_is_valid` predicate flagged 2,111 rows, but small-batch verification through a faithful replica of the production `llmclient.SanitizeResponse` parser found **213 recoverable** (valid JSON behind markdown fences / think-tags: rerank_cross 184, query_classify 18, ape.reflect 11) — spared as good data — and **1,898 genuinely corrupt** (ape.reflect 1,879 from the pre-APE-PROMPT-BUDGET-001 truncation window, jiminy.evaluate_llm 18, consulting.classify 1). Deleted those 1,898 by unique `trace_id` after backing up all rows to `.mdemg-backup-<ts>/dataprune/` (reversible): 102,415 → 100,517, `remaining_corrupt = 0`, live stack healthy, recent ape.reflect 14/14 valid. **Audit pitfall recorded:** a corrupt-data prune predicate must mirror the production sanitizer, not raw JSON validity (else it deletes valid wrapped responses) — and never run a jsonb-validity predicate against string-schema tasks (intent_translate/codegen/synthesize emit valid bare strings). Categories B + C deferred to a future gated pass. Sprint: `docs/development/dataprune-audit-001/`.

- **Sprint APE-PROMPT-BUDGET-001 — bound the ape.reflect prompt so output can't truncate** (2026-06-13, training-integrity remediation; operator-sequenced after REWARD-CORRECTNESS-001 surfaced this live). ape.reflect — the largest LLM training target (54k rows) — was emitting **~87% truncated, invalid-JSON responses**. Live root cause: the assembled prompt had grown to **7489 tokens** (Current Assessment ~3895, dominated by verbose TSDB dataset fields; 5-cycle history ~2693), leaving only ~700 of the 8192 per-slot KV budget (`--ctx-size 32768 / --parallel 4`) for output — 191/200 invalid responses clustered at 490–520 `tokens_out`, cut off mid-array exactly at the ceiling. Not a `max_tokens` cap (floors at 2000; valid rows exceeded 500), not fixable by compression (already on); the prompt was **structurally unbounded**. Fix (`internal/ape/llm_reflector.go::buildUserPrompt`): a token budget — (1) gate the verbose TSDB dataset fields behind `RSIC_LLM_REFLECT_INCLUDE_DATASETS` (default false; scalar health metrics always kept); (2) cap history via `RSIC_LLM_REFLECT_HISTORY_CYCLES` (default 3, was hardcoded 5); (3) a final guard (`RSIC_LLM_REFLECT_PROMPT_BUDGET_TOKENS` default 3500, 0 disables) that drops history oldest-first then trims the assessment tail, logging loudly (never silent). **Live Tier 3:** 3/3 fresh post-restart rows valid JSON (**100%, up from ~13%**), `tokens_in` down to 2549–2596 (dataset gating + history cap alone cleared the budget — the hard guard never fired), `tokens_out` 607–981 (complete arrays). The serving-slot change (Lever B) was considered and **not applied** — the budget alone restores ~4000 tokens of output headroom. 3 range-validated config fields (no hardcoding), 6 Tier-1 tests. Feature doc: `docs/features/rsic-reflection-prompt-budget.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/ape-prompt-budget-001/`.

- **Sprint REWARD-CORRECTNESS-001 — inclusion selects for correctness, not length** (2026-06-13, training-integrity remediation; operator-sequenced after EVAL-INTEGRITY-001). The distill inclusion gate (`mean(reward_vector) >= 0.8`) selected for verbosity — the corpus-skew mechanism behind 3 discarded retrains. Shipped: (1) **length-neutral rewards** — `coverage_score`, `explanation_quality`, `coherence_score`, `insight_count` rewritten so a substantive, valid response scores high regardless of length; verbosity is never rewarded upward; empty/pure-repetition still scores low (78 unit tests re-pinned to the new semantics). (2) **per-task inclusion thresholds** — `--reward-threshold-map` JSON in `x9_distill_capture_v2.py` overrides the global gate per task (recorded per-row + in the manifest). **Live Tier 3** (scoring REAL production rows, `docs/development/reward-correctness-001/live_findings.md`): validated the fix — `hidden.summarize` recovered **69 of 72** concise summaries the old length ladder dropped; the per-task map applied live end-to-end (`{"consulting.classify": 0.6}`, real OpenAI + TSDB). The live run also **surfaced the dominant remaining corpus-correctness problems, which are NOT length bias**: ape.reflect (54k rows, largest target) is **~87% truncated mid-JSON** (`json_valid` mean 0.133 — prompt ~5800 + ~3000-token array output overflows the 8192-per-slot KV bound; a production serving defect the gate correctly rejects); jiminy.evaluate's `explanation_quality` scores 0.0 on correct `{violations,warnings}` responses (reward/schema mismatch); jiminy.synthesize's keyword-bag `follow_rate`/`specificity` sit just below the gate. **Deferred (operator-sequenced):** the baseline recompute (planned Epic 4) is held behind the ape.reflect truncation fix — recomputing over a known-truncated corpus would bake in the corruption. Sprint: `docs/development/reward-correctness-001/`.

- **Sprint EVAL-INTEGRITY-001 — the adapter promotion gate becomes trustworthy** (2026-06-13, training-integrity remediation lead; operator-sequenced after the data-correctness audit). The gate that discarded 3 retrains as "worse than baseline" couldn't tell good from bad. Shipped (harness correctness): (1) **leak-free eval** — re-pointed from the 99%-leaked `valid_golden` to a rebuilt leak-free `valid_clean` (240 rows / 12 tasks), config + holdout SHAs re-pinned; `make test-eval-leak` re-verifies disjointness every run (script had zero callers; verified 0/240 overlap). (2) **dynamic-prompt matching** — `Spec` now carries `dynamic_prompt`; enum-templated prompts match by `task_name` not a drifting hash. This recovered `ape.reflect`'s **71,033 production rows** (the largest training target) that a stale strict-hash filter (`39b2bc…` vs production `ef068ab…`) had silently excluded from eval AND curation; coverage 10→12 tasks. (3) **zero-call hard-fail** — a run with zero successful calls reported aggregate 0.0 (indistinguishable from a low score; the false-0.0 class behind FT-CLASSIFY-002's 4 silent failures) — now `status=error` + nonzero exit. (4) **capture-gap fix** — `summarize.generate` (default-on) ran in the `mdemg ingest` subprocess where the LLM recorder was never wired (only `mdemg serve` did), silently dropping its entire corpus; recorder now wired in ingest (flush-on-exit). The other 4 zero-row tasks are correctly gated-off (SYNTHESIS/GUARDRAIL/METALEARN/RERANK default-false). **Sequenced with the reward sprint** (closing steps, deliberately deferred): GGUF serving in `regression.py` (off the OOM-prone MLX side-server — only exercised during a retrain) and the baseline recompute (an honest baseline needs the *fixed reward*, so it's the reward sprint's closing step; a provisional one now would bake in the current reward bug). Feature doc: `docs/features/adapter-promotion-gate.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/eval-integrity-001/`.

- **Sprint SIDECAR-LOOP-001 — rerank collector correctness + training-pipeline audit** (2026-06-13, Roadmap Q3 Phase 4). Operator-decided fix-but-defer. The dormant rerank training collector (2.5 months, 6,814 events) was found to mislabel **100%** of records — it logged `req.Candidates[:topN]` (unsorted input) against `rerankScores` (rerank-sorted output), so candidate↔score correspondence was broken (84% length-mismatch, the rest positionally wrong). Fixed: log `result.Results`+`result.RerankScores` (aligned 1:1) + a `Collect` guard dropping misaligned/empty/all-zero records (clean by construction); pre-fix data archived (recoverable). Reranker TRAINING deferred (rerank is default-off; superseded by the training-integrity remediation below). **Operator-triggered training-pipeline audit** (the 3 prior LLM-adapter retrains were discarded as worse-than-baseline): root-caused to one mislabeling class in THREE places — (1) the `mean>=0.8` distill filter over length/keyword-biased rewards skews the corpus toward verbose output (`summary_quality` was one instance of a systemic pattern; `ape.reflect`/`summarize`/`synthesize` unfixed); (2) the promotion gate evals on the 99%-leaked `valid_golden` vs a frozen stale-reward MLX-form baseline (0.8338) with no zero-call hard-fail — a worse-than-baseline verdict is uninterpretable; (3) `dpo_builder` assigns chosen/rejected by list position, ignoring the outcome it computes. All file:line-verified. Remediation sequenced eval-integrity first; no retrain until fixed. Audit: `docs/development/sidecar-loop-001/training_pipeline_correctness_audit.md`. Feature doc: `docs/features/rerank-training-collection.md`.

- **Sprint NEGFEED-001 + COOLER-001 — complete the memory lifecycle** (2026-06-13, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2, ranked #9). The Hebbian lifecycle was half-open at both ends and its dominant producer had wrong semantics (live-verified): the graph could only strengthen (2 weaken rows ever, **1 CONTRADICTS edge in the whole graph**); nothing graduated by default (two divergent paths, cooler default-off, retrieval read no graduation signal); and **CoactivateSession** — 73% of reinforcement writes (244k rows/14d) — ran synchronously on the Observe path regenerating the full unbounded C(n,2) clique every call (O(n²); `evidence_count` = session length). Shipped: **(1)** CoactivateSession off-request (panic-recovered background goroutine, detached ctx) + **delta emission** (new obs × prior members, `LEARNING_SESSION_CLIQUE_WINDOW` default 50) — O(window) per observe, `evidence_count` now counts genuine co-activations, cumulative graph unchanged; DH-004 stability raise preserved. **(2)** anti-Hebbian producer: Bridge A (Jiminy `contradicted` → weaken co-activations among the guidance source nodes; `q<>r` self-pair guard makes single-source a safe no-op; `JIMINY_CONTRADICTED_WEAKEN_ENABLED`) + Bridge B (MCP `memory_reject`, 24 tools, contract-tested) — both into the existing EVENTGRAPH-003/004 federation (no new sink). **(3)** graduation unified: RSIC `executeGraduateVolatile` delegates to `ContextCooler.ProcessGraduations` (0.8 + pinned-guard + `graduated_at`; snapshot predicate aligned via `SetGraduationThreshold` per RSIC-STORM-001), and the cooler runs per-space from `mdemg maintenance` (new Step 2 — Graduation). **(4)** "graduated" now means something to retrieval — graduated-incident CO_ACTIVATED_WITH edges resist decay (`GRADUATED_DECAY_PROTECTION_FACTOR` default 0.5; learning-side, no scorer change / UVTS gate). **Tier 3** (throwaway space; mdemg-dev baseline 191457 coact / 1 contradicts unchanged): delta 3 obs → 6 directed edges, `evidence_count` uniformly 1; weaken 0.119→0.0 with the `apply_negative_feedback` federation row flushed (delta −0.119); maintenance graduated 5 via the cooler with `graduated_at` set on all; decay query graduated-incident clause validated live. Feature doc: `docs/features/memory-lifecycle.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/negfeed-cooler-001/`.

- **Sprint CONTEXT-LIVE-001 — context fingerprinting goes live** (2026-06-13, Roadmap Q3 stretch tier, first pick). The 5th RRF column was benchmark-only: derivation required a `?context=auto` param no live caller passed; mdemg-dev held **76,906 nodes on fingerprint v1 vs active catalog v3** (cross-version Jaccard = noise, bit positions reallocate per build); `RefineWithCoactivations` had ZERO callers; the always-empty live context column **hard-capped every live query's consensus at 0.8**; per-category protections only fired on benchmark `?category=` calls. Shipped: (1) **version guard** — derivation returns its catalog version, ContextColumn/strict-mode score mismatched candidates 0; (2) **honest consensus** — columns structurally unable to vote are not appended (errored columns still count); scorer namespace v2 + per-category maps hashed in (CacheKey reflection guard caught the new field unprompted); (3) **stage-6 self-heal** — `RecomputeStaleFingerprints` (recomputation, NOT refine — refine merges old-catalog bits, disclosed deviation) heals up to 2000 stale nodes/cycle + Phase-B refine wired (200/cycle, drains to steady state); one-shot CLI heal moved mdemg-dev to **77,749 on v3**; (4) **classifier→category dispatch** (`QUERY_CLASSIFY_CATEGORY_MAP`) — live-proven: a data-flow query tripped the data_flow_integration sparse floor (20 vs 15); (5) **derivation default-on** (`CONTEXT_QUERY_AUTO_DEFAULT`, opt-out `?context=off`) — live-proven via v3 vec-cache build on a bare retrieve. **Gate**: 120q UVTS A/B — strict mean verdict failed at −0.004 but root-caused to pure citation_bonus generation noise (13 flips, 9−/4+); the retrieval components (evidence/semantic/concept) are **bit-identical on all 120 questions** (analysis + unmodified verdict committed). The deliberate binary swaps live-fired the TSDB-CONSUME-001 scorer-drift tripwire as designed. Sprint: `docs/development/context-live-001/`.

- **Sprint DORMANT-CENSUS-001 — the standing dormancy guarantee** (2026-06-12, Roadmap Q3 — the FINAL committed sprint). The quarter's bug classes (24-day Hebbian no-op, 9-week guidance dormancy, dead actuators, zero-write tables, the flat-dead surprise chain) share one shape: a surface with no consumer, invisible until someone looks. The looking is now CI. (1) **Route↔consumer inventory + merge-blocking gate**: `docs/api/route_consumer_inventory.json` (one adjudicated entry per route: consumers, uats_specs, disposition) + `scripts/verify_route_consumers.py` in ci.yml beside the config-consumer guard — extracts the live route table from server.go and fails on bidirectional drift or any UNREVIEWED entry. Census results (all 187 routes evidence-adjudicated, orchestrator re-verified): 109 ACTIVE / 60 OPERATOR_SURFACE / 13 INTERNAL / 4 PRUNED / 1 DEFERRED. **Census reversals prove the method**: `/viz/topology` + `/api/graph/*` were on the prune list but are LIVE Grafana consumers (topology iframe + nodegraph datasource); `/v1/conversation/snapshot*` is NOT called by pre-compact.sh (recon claim failed verification — it saves via `/v1/conversation/observe`); PREDICTS/FORESHADOWS exist nowhere in code (recon hallucination, no-op); the embedded `/ui/` dashboard consumes ~35 routes that look dormant from hooks/CLI/scripts alone. (2) **SignalLearner.GetStrength WIRED** — a fully-shipped Hebbian persistence layer (V0024 SignalState, supervised flush, startup hydration, live emission/response stream) whose read side had ZERO production callers. Guide() now orders within equal priority by `(1-w)·confidence + w·strength` (`JIMINY_SIGNAL_STRENGTH_WEIGHT`, default 0.2; 0 = off). Live SignalState variance is real: strengths 0.1–0.75 over thousands of emissions (`guidance:pattern` 0.75 vs `guidance:concept` 0.1). Live extreme test: at w=1.0 a strength-0.9 item ordered ABOVE a higher-confidence strength-0.1 item — learned effectiveness inverts pure confidence. (3) **Prune pass** (each re-verified zero-producer + named successor): `/v1/feedback` (live channel is `/v1/jiminy/feedback`; isolated `gaps.ProcessFeedback` removed), `/v1/memory/ingest-codebase[/]` (deprecated since Phase 94; successor `/v1/memory/ingest/*`), `POST /v1/alerts/grafana` (native evaluator; compose env `MDEMG_GRAFANA_ALERT_WEBHOOK_URL` removed); 6 UATS specs removed, UXTS matrix 220→214. (4) **Live-smoke catch (own fix commit)**: `ConstraintCodeGenerator`'s collision branch re-locked its own non-reentrant mutex — the first LLM-returned code colliding with a registered one **self-deadlocked the generator permanently**, wedging every constraint-typed `/v1/conversation/observe` behind it (UATS `conversation_observe_pinned` hung 45–90s deterministically; goroutine dump showed the holder 18 min wedged). Fixed with `fallbackCodeLocked` + a fake-LLM regression test driving the real collision path. Post-fix UATS: 388 passed / 0 failed. Feature doc: `docs/features/dormant-census.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/dormant-census-001/`.

- **Sprint JIMINY-BUDGET-001 + OUTCOME-ATTRIB-001 — guidance budgets coherent, attribution clean at the source** (2026-06-12, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2–3, last committed member before DORMANT-CENSUS-001). **Budgets**: `JIMINY_TIMEOUT_MS` default 15s→**0 = derive from the 90s warm-compute budget** (both paths run the same ~50s synthesis; the independent 15s starved every fresh install — the GUIDANCE-SYNTH-001 class, previously patched only on this machine's `.env`); `/reformulate`'s hardcoded 10s → `JIMINY_REFORMULATE_TIMEOUT_MS` (0=derive); `config.Validate()` warns when an explicit direct budget undercuts the warm budget. **Attribution**: `PersistGuidanceOutcome` now receives the real session — the call site passed literal `""` so **every GUIDANCE_OUTCOME edge ever has null session_id** (forward-only fix; live-verified: first attributed edge `session_id='jiminy-budget-smoke'`); surface-vs-outcome split via `mdemg_jiminy_guidance_surfaced_total{space_id}` (the honest denominator — `TotalGuidanceIssued` only ever counted guidance that RECEIVED feedback) + `mdemg_jiminy_feedback_dropped_total{space_id}` (tracker-expiry drops, was Warn-log-only). **Token floors**: `JIMINY_OUTCOME_LLM_MAX_TOKENS` 100→3000 (truncation risk on the classifier `reasoning` field → JSON parse fail → heuristic fallback, the JIMINY-OUTCOME-002 artifact class), SYNTHESIS and EVALUATE 2000→3000 (standing ≥3000 rule; completions stop at natural end — floors are free). Hook-budget chain documented: 5s fire-and-forget curl → 60s detached server budget → 86400s tracker TTL (stale 1800 comment fixed). Sprint: `docs/development/jiminy-budget-001/`.

- **Sprint SURPRISE-TOPK-001 — honest novelty + a multiplier that fires** (2026-06-12, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2–3; HEBB-ETA-001's named prerequisite). Live baselines escalated the roadmap's diagnosis from 'noisy' to **flat dead**: ALL 221,504 reinforcement events ever carried surprise_factor=1.0; node surprise_score averaged 0.023 (max 0.503) vs hardcoded 0.7/0.4 thresholds. THREE compounding causes found: (1) the unordered `LIMIT 50` novelty sample (no ORDER BY — similarity-decoupled); (2) **78% of conversation observations carry EMPTY-ARRAY embeddings** (4,564/5,810 — pass `IS NOT NULL`, but `cosine()` yields NULL, poisoning the average; follow-up: embeddings backfill); (3) thresholds unreachable on the real score scale. Fixes: exact ORDER-BY top-K nearest-neighbor novelty (`SURPRISE_EMBEDDING_NOVELTY_TOPK` 50, `_SIM_FLOOR` 0=off; `size(embedding)=dims` guards the empty rows; the vector-index route was live-REJECTED — the label-wide index's top-200 near a conversation seed were ALL emergent_concept centroids, pruning role-filtered hits to zero); multiplier thresholds config-driven at BOTH Cypher sites (`SURPRISE_FACTOR_HIGH/MEDIUM_THRESHOLD`, defaults 0.4/0.15 **live-calibrated**: corrections score ~0.455, noise band 0.02–0.10 — never recalibrate against the old scale, the RRF-SCALE lesson); the silent embedding-novelty error path now logs loudly. Live Tier 3: first non-1.0 factors in system history (`{1.0:4, 1.5:3}` in the smoke session). Recorded follow-ups: empty-embedding backfill; weak non-correction discrimination (novel 0.10 vs mundane 0.08 — term-novelty component, own item). Sprint: `docs/development/surprise-topk-001/`.

- **Sprint TSDB-CONSUME-001 — the telemetry plane gets consumed, bounded, and honest** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2, last committed Phase 2/3 member). (2) **Windowed percentile synthetics** — `*_p95/_p99` gauges were lifetime-cumulative: one slow call pegged them forever (live: a constant 9.95 top-bucket clamp firing a perpetual latency CRITICAL); now computed over the inter-flush bucket delta, idle windows emit nothing. (4) **Fake Neo4j pool gauges deleted, real pgx pool gauges shipped** — the old values were a `VerifyConnectivity` probe counter + perpetual zeros (the neo4j driver has no pool API), which made `neo4j_pool_exhausted` unfireable (rule deleted, server-native + Grafana); `mdemg_tsdb_pool_*` now reads `pgxpool.Stat()`; `/v1/system/pool-metrics` + the mdemg-neo4j dashboard pool panels + 3 UOTS specs rewired to the real data. (5) **Rate-limit collector fixed and wired** — it had ZERO callers and added the cumulative total per flush (quadratic inflation); now delta-tracked in both collection paths. (6) **Writer flush health** — all 8 buffered writers self-register their `Stats()` into `mdemg_tsdb_writer_*{writer}` gauges + rule `tsdb_writer_flush_failures` (a wedged writer previously dropped rows in silence; only the reinforcement writer was visible). (7) **Scorer-drift tripwires** — the RRF-SCALE-001 class self-detects: `scorer_version_change` (>1 distinct version in 24h — the prompt to re-audit every Score consumer) + `consensus_shift` (|recent−baseline| mean `consensus_strength` > 0.10 ≈ 1σ live, sample-gated); both hand-verified on live data (steady state quiet; a wide window correctly flags the 3 historical versions); `RETRIEVAL_AUDIT_ENABLED` default flipped **true** + compose-forwarded. (8) **Idea-09 measurable + DBSCAN deferral observable** — `mdemg_guidance_conflicts_total{space_id}` counts detected conflicts; `mdemg_emergence_cycle_duration_seconds{space_id,cycle}` records every consolidation cycle (was computed and discarded) + rule `emergence_cycle_slow` (>60s window MAX; gauge not histogram — the registry's fixed ≤10s buckets would clamp multi-minute cycles, disclosed deviation). (9) **V0020 writer-or-drop → writer**: `context_catalog_versions` had zero writes ever; each catalog build now records one row (sync single-row, model_install precedent). (10) **ft_* dashboard disposition**: the 4 reader-without-writer panels removed (tables stay — they're the FT recursive-loop's designated sinks); status notice records it.
