- **Sprint RSIC-STORM-001 — the cycle storm is dead, archival is honest, rollback is real** (2026-06-11, off-roadmap, earned by live evidence). (1) **Atomic trigger admission** — `EvaluateTrigger` checked active/cooldown records that only `RecordTrigger` wrote, AFTER `RunCycle` completed: for a cycle's whole multi-second run every concurrent trigger passed every gate (~20–30k micro cycles/day live, 4 spawning within 50 ms; the 300s cooldown effectively nonexistent; llama-server permanently saturated — the recurring synthesize/evaluate_llm/intent_translate timeout cascades and the per-session `trigger_training_pipeline` alert spam). Admission now reserves the slot under the checking lock; live: **1 cycle admitted / 132 rejected in 4 min** (was ~47), zero LLM errors since. (2) **Attributable archival** — `tombstone_stale` stamped only `is_archived` (no timestamp/reason), which mis-attributed the morning's 5,397-node CRITICAL drop to RSIC for hours; the real actor was the **Context Cooler** sweeping its pre-DH-004 never-graduated backlog in ONE uncapped run via the session-start hook's graduate call. Tombstones now stamp `archived_at`/`archive_reason`/`archived_cycle_id`; canonical property `archive_reason` (the `archived_reason` split also fed the mis-attribution); cooler capped at `COOLER_TOMBSTONE_MAX_PER_RUN` (500) with a loud cap-reached warning (live re-fire: bound at exactly 500, all error-debris, recovered nodes untouched). (3) **Rollback predicate drift fixed** — RSIC-VALIDATE-001 updated the executor's correction-linkage predicate but not the snapshot's, so rollback captured a different node set and restored nothing (`restored_count=0` live); both now share ONE Cypher const (pin-tested) and a live drill restores 3/3 with metadata cleared. (4) **Recovery executed (operator option b)**: 952 non-error cooler-tombstone victims un-archived + graduated (`recovered_reason='cooler_backlog_recovery'`, one-query reversible; LIMIT-5-first); 4,451+ error-debris stays archived. Feature doc: `docs/features/rsic-orchestration.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/rsic-storm-001/`.

- **Sprint BACKUP-RESTORE-VERIFY-001 — backups that provably restore** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2 #2). The biggest catch came from the mandatory live round-trip itself: **retention was deleting every backup the moment it completed** — `BACKUP_RETENTION_MAX_STORAGE_GB` had a comment/code default drift (documented 50, code read 2), below the size of one whole-database export (3–4 GB), and `RunAfter` retention removed each new backup ~80 ms after `backup completed` (the backup system was a no-op for any database whose export exceeded the quota; nobody noticed because nothing watched the scheduler — see jobhealth below). Fixes: quota retention NEVER deletes the newest backup of each type (degrades to "over quota, keep it" with a loud warning; sparse-file pin tests), default quota 50 GB. Restore-path hardening: (1) **checksum gate** — the manifest SHA-256 was written and never read; corrupted files restored silently; now fails closed before import (legacy manifests warn). (2) **Snapshot race fixed** — the pre-restore safety snapshot was raced with `time.Sleep(2s)` against an async job; now polls to completion, failing closed (`BACKUP_SNAPSHOT_WAIT_TIMEOUT_SEC`, default 3600 — the live export runs ~15 min, which the original 300s default would have failed). (3) **Count validation** — manifest counts were whole-database (useless for partial-backup validation); new `file_*_count` manifest fields counted from exported chunks; restore re-counts and hard-fails truncation, with a full validation block in the job result. (4) **dockerbin routing** for legacy `.dump` restores (was bare `"docker"`, the launchd-PATH class). (5) **neo4j-backup jobhealth** — the default-ON scheduler had zero coverage (inverted NOSILENT); scheduled runs now wait on the queue-async job and report to `scheduled_job_events` + a generalized `jobStalenessRule` factory registers `neo4j_backup_no_recent_success` (gated on `BACKUP_ENABLED`, window = partial interval × 2 or `BACKUP_JOB_STALENESS_HOURS`). (6) **Initial backup on start** (`BACKUP_INITIAL_DELAY_MIN`, default 5) — fresh installs get a backup (and a quiet staleness rule) within minutes instead of 24h. Feature doc: `docs/features/backup-restore.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/backup-restore-verify-001/`.

- **Sprint SUPERVISOR-002 — the always-on guarantee actually holds** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2, first "next in line" member). (1) **12 unsupervised background loops registered** under `internal/supervisor` (panic recovery + restart budget): the 6 api.Server loops (periodic-consolidation, context-cooler, space-prune, gap-interviews, scheduled-sync, rsic-macro-cron — via injected `SetSupervisor` + `goSupervised`), 3 ape loops (rsic-watchdog, rsic-store-flush, signal-learner-flush), both backup schedulers (construction-time `Start()` moved to `StartSupervisedBackground()` so the hook exists first), and the llm-fastfail burst flush — previously a panic in any of them silently killed that subsystem forever; nil-hook fallback keeps tests/non-server callers unchanged (TSDB event writers deliberately deferred to TSDB-CONSUME-001). (2) **Sliding-window restart budget** — the counter only ever incremented, so one transient/week permanently killed a worker in 3 weeks; budget is now `SUPERVISOR_MAX_RESTARTS` (3) within `SUPERVISOR_RESTART_WINDOW_MIN` (60), with `Go()` late registration and nil-return-means-done semantics. (3) **Rule-health meta-alert** — evaluator query failures promoted Debug→Warn; after `ALERT_RULE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD` (3) consecutive failures a high alert fires directly via the dispatcher: per-rule `rule-health-<id>` when peers are succeeding (broken-SQL class — bitten twice in one week), or ONE `alert-evaluator-degraded` per outage when nothing succeeds (drill-caught fix: the discriminator is streak-relative, since at outage onset the last success is always fresh — 2 per-rule leaks in drill 1, 0 in drill 2). (4) **RSIC recency gate** — `llm_error_rate_spike` evaluated a 24h window with no freshness requirement, so a 35-min timeout burst at 02:00Z kept firing HIGH/CRITICAL alerts for 12+ hours after self-resolving (5 false criticals live); now requires the most recent error within `RSIC_LLM_ERROR_RECENCY_MIN` (60; 0=off) via new `LLMPerformanceSummary.LastErrorAt`; live-verified suppressing the stale synthesize spike while the fresh evaluate_llm failure still fired. Live-smoke surprise (own fix-commit): **`/v1/jiminy/feedback` outcome processing detached from the hook's 5s curl lifetime** (`context.WithoutCancel` + `JIMINY_FEEDBACK_TIMEOUT_MS` 60000) — the request ctx was cancelling Tier-2 outcome classification mid-flight (657 "context canceled"/24h, 94.9% error rate; outcomes silently degraded to the keyword heuristic). Feature doc: `docs/features/goroutine-supervisor.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/supervisor-002/`.

- **Sprint HIDDEN-CHURN-001 PR-B — coverage retune + grounding tools** (2026-06-11, part B of 2 — sprint complete). (1) **Coverage gap mechanism closed**: KMeans never emits noise labels, so the min-samples/max-themes cluster drops silently excluded observations from the hierarchy forever (the 94%-unthemed audit finding); dropped members now density-assign to their nearest theme when cosine ≥ `HIDDEN_THEME_ASSIGN_SIM_THRESHOLD` (0.70; edges only, below-floor stays honestly unthemed), and the themes-per-observation ratio is config-driven (`HIDDEN_THEME_TARGET_RATIO`, 0.1). (2) **Clustering no longer ingests archived debris** — the observation fetch had no `is_archived` filter and was clustering 4,838 observations of which 183 were live (MAINT-LIVE tombstones); after the fix the 24 debris-built themes swept to 5 clean ones and the next cycle ran `themes_updated: 5 / created: 0` — stable identity on real data. (3) **Coverage self-reports**: per-space gauge `mdemg_neo4j_conversation_coverage_ratio` (gated on `CONVERSATION_COVERAGE_MIN_OBS`, 50 — tiny test spaces would otherwise alarm forever) + evaluator rule `low_conversation_coverage` (`CONVERSATION_COVERAGE_ALERT_FLOOR`, 0.2); live mdemg-dev coverage 0.331 and rising. (4) **`mdemg concepts repair`** tombstoned the 10,395 childless layer≥2 churn-era concepts (recoverable `is_archived`, dry-run default, LIMIT-5-first verified; predicate pin-tested on `ABSTRACTS_TO` — the real grounding edge — not `GENERALIZES`, which over-counts 19k); **`mdemg concepts trace`** audits any node's grounding chain to L0 with an honest grounded/UNGROUNDED verdict. (5) Consolidate API + periodic log now surface `themes_updated`/`noise_assigned` (with stable identity, `created` is 0 on healthy cycles — the old log condition would have gone silent). Bonus: pre-flight SQL audit caught HIDDEN-WEIGHT-001's `null_weight_abstraction_edges` rule querying `metric_samples` with `recorded_at` (column is `time`) — silently erroring every evaluation since it shipped; both rules fixed + a pin test bans the pattern. Verification: `docs/development/hidden-churn-001/verification_pr_b.md`.

- **Sprint HIDDEN-CHURN-001 PR-A — stable concept identity + emergence-skip fix** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2 #8, part A of 2). (1) **The 5-minute theme churn is dead**: `ClusterConversations` detached every observation→theme edge and recreated all themes from scratch each cycle — new node_ids every run, evidence chains destroyed, recall flooded with near-identical concept stacks. Clusters now MATCH existing themes by centroid cosine (`HIDDEN_THEME_IDENTITY_SIM_THRESHOLD`, default 0.90) and update them IN PLACE (theme-scoped edge rewire; node_id + inbound references survive); only themes matched by no cluster are deleted; the global-detach helper is removed. Live churn test: **5/5 sampled theme node_ids survived a full consolidation cycle** (23→23, `themes_created: 0`), with freshly-rewired real-weight member edges. (2) **Automated consolidation no longer skips LLM emergence**: `dynamicEmergenceStep` is phase 22 but `RunConsolidation` ran hardcoded ranges (10,20)+(25,30) — the gap silently dropped it; now delegates to the single range source with the `EMERGENCE_ENABLED` gate, and a pin test fails if the phase ever leaves the range. PR-B (follow-on): coverage retune, childless-L2 repair, `mdemg concepts trace`. Sprint: `docs/development/hidden-churn-001/`.

- **Sprints RRF-SCALE-002 + CACHE-KEY-002 — score-scale contract finished, cache-key class CI-caught** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2 #7). (1) **Rerank failure alerting re-armed**: per-call `llmclient.New` in `doRerankWithOpenAI/Ollama` reset the consecutive-failure counter every call — `LLM_CONSECUTIVE_FAILURE_THRESHOLD` could never fire for `retrieval.rerank_cross`/`rerank_nli`; per-provider persistent clients now share the counter/breaker/transport via `WithContext`. (2) **Three score-literal leftovers config-driven**: `/v1/memory/suggest`'s 0.5 floor → `CONSULTING_SUGGEST_MIN_CONFIDENCE` (**0.2, live-calibrated** — real distributions put good hits at 0.2–0.45; verified revival: 2 on-point suggestions where the old floor returned none); MCP `memory_reflect` tiers 0.7/0.4 (high tier unreachable) → `MCP_REFLECT_SCORE_HIGH/_MEDIUM` (0.45/0.25); guardrail Cypher `sim > 0.3` → `GUARDRAIL_CONSTRAINT_SIM_FLOOR`. (3) **CacheKey now covers ALL result-affecting fields** — the audit's 5 (extensions filters, temporal bounds, policy_context) plus **8 more caught by the new reflection forcing-function on its first run** (sparse-gate `?sparse=` overrides, pagination, context-fingerprint params) + a query-embedding hash. (4) **Two forcing functions**: a reflection test (every `RetrieveRequest` field must be keyed or justified result-neutral) and a repo-wide score-literal scan (triaged 3 scale-local sites on first run) — the RRF-SCALE and cache-key bug classes are now CI-caught. Sprint: `docs/development/rrf-scale-002/`.

- **Sprint RSIC-VALIDATE-001 — RSIC validates for real (fail-closed self-improvement)** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 2 #6). Self-improvement was open-loop — five confirmed defects: (1) **vacuous validation** — the cycle baseline populated 10 metric keys while task criteria referenced ~15 others (2 intersecting) → `missing_data` → skip → ~16/17 actions "succeeded" with zero evidence and rollback was unreachable; fixed with single-source `reportMetricsMap()` feeding both MetricsBefore/After (19 keys, mismatch class can't recur); (2) **fail-closed rule** — for the 15 mutating actions a criterion with missing evidence now counts as NOT met (`missing_data_failclosed`); observational actions stay advisory; (3) **`tombstone_stale` eroded unrelated memory** (any recent correction → archive 50 arbitrary older observations) — now requires correction linkage (same session or 1-hop co-activation); (4) **`refresh_stale_edges` could only inflate weights** (SET ordering read `last_activated` after bumping it → staleness=0 → decay term vanished) — staleness now captured before the bump, real decay restored; (5) **counter-free calibration** — RSIC's synthetic "followed"/"ignored" injections polluted the exact effectiveness counters it reads next cycle (circular); new `AdjustConfidenceDirect` adjusts confidence with zero counter writes. Tier 3: a real cycle now records `criteria_met=false` with concrete per-criterion deltas — the honest verdict where last night everything passed vacuously. Sprint: `docs/development/rsic-validate-001/`.

- **Sprint DOC-TRUTH-001 — documentation matches reality** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #6 — Phase 1 complete). Stale docs seeded the Q3 roadmap audit with a dead architecture; all verified-stale assertions corrected: CLAUDE.md's FT section rewritten to the post-pivot shipped state (dense `mdemg-llm-v1`; the Qwen3.6 MoE plan documented as superseded with rationale, never deleted); the guardrail "bypasses llmclient" exception marked CLOSED (migration shipped, re-verified in code); the memo-07 provenance break disclosed (cited as canonical, never existed — `00_README_v2.md` is canonical, now with a top-of-file STATUS block incl. the NOT-STARTED recursive-retraining loop + its trigger and the R-LT-4 adjudication); `MDEMG_ADAPTER_BASE` env-name drift fixed; **preflight errors no longer direct operators to the decommissioned mlx_lm.server :8101 stack** (now llama-server :8102 guidance); three stale "deferred to MODEL-DIST-002" help texts fixed; the month-stale `AGENT_HANDOFF.md` retired to a pointer stub; operational: the `.env` `J17_SIDECAR_TIMEOUT_MS=200` override (re-pinning the exact value DH-004 remediated) removed — the 1000ms default applies. Sprint: `docs/development/doc-truth-001/`.

- **Sprints EMBED-WIRE-001 + INGEST-EXEC-001 — embedder wiring + ingest exec resolution** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #5). Two silent failures in core data paths: (1) **the embedding circuit breaker was never wired in any default deployment** — `embeddings.New` returns `*CachedEmbedder` under the default `EMBEDDING_CACHE_ENABLED=true`, so the server's outermost-value type assertions failed silently; the recorder assertion had the inverse fragility (cache off → training-data recording dies). Fixed with an interface-driven `Unwrap()` chain (`embeddings.Base`/`FindCached` — future wrappers join by adding `Unwrap()`), loud warns when nothing matches. Live: "circuit breaker wired to OpenAI embedder" logged for the first time in project history; `openai-embeddings` visible in `/v1/admin/breakers`. (2) **Server-triggered ingest exec'd a hardcoded relative `./bin/mdemg`** — broken in Docker and any non-repo-root CWD. New `resolveMdemgBin()` (`MDEMG_BIN` env → `os.Executable()` → PATH → legacy fallback) at both exec sites; scheduled-sync outcomes now report to `scheduled_job_events` via jobhealth (`codebase-sync`). Live: a real `/v1/memory/ingest/trigger` job completed through the resolved binary. Sprint: `docs/development/embed-wire-001/`.

- **Sprint MAINT-LIVE-001 — scheduled maintenance actually runs** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #4). Weekly decay+prune had **never executed**: the CLI defaults `--dry-run=true` and the LaunchAgent passed no override — every scheduled cycle previewed and reported success (NOSILENT's blind spot; the 79k-node Memory-Bloat backlog). Shipped: (1) the plist passes `--dry-run=false` (CLI default unchanged — manual runs still preview); (2) `dry_run` recorded in job-event metadata + evaluator rule `maintenance_no_live_run` (`MAINT_LIVE_ALERT_ENABLED`/`MAINT_LIVE_LOOKBACK_DAYS`) — the only-ever-dry-runs pattern self-reports; the rule was **born firing against real data** and was silenced by the first genuine run; (3) **`--exclude-role-types`/`PRUNE_EXCLUDE_ROLE_TYPES`** — orphan disposition is context-dependent (operator decision): the schedule excludes `constraint,conversation_observation`; (4) `mdemg upgrade` (darwin) refreshes installed LaunchAgents + mdemg-managed hooks from the new binary's templates (refresh-only; substitutions single-sourced). **First live run:** deleted 20,236 orphan SymbolNodes; 5,010 tombstone candidates all protected (recency shield + exclusions); retrieval + weight-integrity gauges healthy after. Live smoke caught a real bug (own commit): the orphan sweeps' batched CALL-IN-TRANSACTIONS deletes ran inside ExecuteWrite (illegal; dry-run never executes that statement, so only live execution could surface it) — and the failure proved the NOSILENT dispatcher+evaluator+hook chain end-to-end. Feature doc: `docs/features/scheduled-maintenance.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/maint-live-001/`.

- **Sprint HIDDEN-WEIGHT-001 — real weights on the abstraction hierarchy** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #3). `point.distance()` is a spatial-Point function — on embedding lists it silently returns NULL, so **every weight at the three abstraction-edge creation sites was never set** (100% of GENERALIZES + 95% of ABSTRACTS_TO edges weightless; the CASE guards passed on good embeddings then the THEN expr evaluated NULL — edges with good embeddings got nothing while embedding-less ones got the 0.5 fallback). Decay, prune, the backward pass, and the RRF graph column were computing over missing data. Fixes: (1) `vector.similarity.cosine` at all 3 sites (returns [0,1] directly — live-verified; site 1 gains the null-guard it never had) + CUIDv2 `edge_id` (was `randomUUID()`); (2) **`mdemg graph backfill-weights`** — standalone, idempotent, batched backfill (deliberately not folded into `graph repair`, whose orphan sweep would delete operator-kept historical orphans); executed dry-run → LIMIT-5 hand-verified (stored ≡ recomputed to 6dp) → full: **0 NULL / 57,395 abstraction edges across all spaces** (~56k healed; mid-run the count grew because the running server predated the fix — restarted, swept); (3) per-space gauge `mdemg_neo4j_graph_null_weight_edges` + evaluator rule `null_weight_abstraction_edges` (`NULL_WEIGHT_EDGE_ALERT_THRESHOLD`, default 100) so the bug class self-reports. Tier 3: a real consolidation minted edges with varied real cosine weights (0.83–0.94) + CUIDv2 ids; gauge holds 0. Known data note: ~50% of backfilled weights are ≈1.0 from single-member-cluster degeneracy (centroid ≡ member) — HIDDEN-CHURN-001 scope. Sprint: `docs/development/hidden-weight-001/`.

- **Sprint HOOKSYNC-001 — drift-proof + self-monitoring hook channel** (2026-06-11, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #2). The channel HOOKWIRE-001 revived now guards itself: (1) **CI hook-parity gate** — live hooks must byte-match the installer templates modulo `{{SPACE_ID}}` (the bidirectional drift this catches had severed alert delivery AND nearly lost the live-only T1 bootstrap block, now single-sourced); (2) **alert Cleared lifecycle** — `POST /v1/alerts/clear` + hooks clear exactly what they display (cleared = delivered, not resolved; the 50-entry backlog drained 10/prompt live instead of re-rendering forever); alert IDs now CUIDv2; (3) **absence detection** — hooks POST heartbeats to `POST /v1/hooks/event` (V0024 `scheduled_job_events` via jobhealth, no new sink; `hook:prompt-context` per fire, `hook:post-tool-observe` throttled), and evaluator rule `hook_channel_silent` fires when sessions are demonstrably active but the per-prompt channel records nothing — the "job never ran" guarantee applied to the channel whose months-long outage was previously only catchable by manual audit; (4) **`mdemg hooks doctor`** — 11-check one-shot triage (template parity, registration, healthz, stdin-contract self-test, alert file, heartbeat age), non-zero exit on FAIL; (5) **PORT-TRUTH rider** — compose publishes on `${MDEMG_BIND_ADDR:-127.0.0.1}` (was 0.0.0.0 with unauthenticated admin routes), neural sidecar binds `127.0.0.1:8101` (config + both plists), and the 39-day-old sidecar process serving pre-J17-fix code was replaced. New config: `HOOK_HEALTH_ALERT_ENABLED` (true), `HOOK_SILENT_LOOKBACK_HOURS` (24), `HOOK_ACTIVITY_MIN_EVENTS` (5), `HOOK_HEARTBEAT_COOLDOWN_SEC` (300), `MDEMG_BIND_ADDR`, `SIDECAR_HOST`. Feature doc: `docs/features/hook-channel-health.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/hooksync-001/`.

### Fixed

- **`mdemg ingest` consolidation call had a hardcoded shared timeout — config-driven budget added** (2026-06-11, caught live during the HIDDEN-WEIGHT-001 corpus reingest). The post-ingest `/v1/memory/consolidate` call shared the batch-ingest `--timeout` client (default 300s); consolidating a ~10k-node space exceeds that, so the client reported failure while the server completed the work — the GUIDANCE-SYNTH-001 bug class (long graph/LLM work needs its own budget). New: `--consolidate-timeout` flag / `INGEST_CONSOLIDATE_TIMEOUT_SEC` env (default 1800s) with a dedicated client. Live-verified: `running consolidation timeout_sec=1800` → complete.

- **Sprint HOOKWIRE-001 — the per-prompt delivery channel was silently dead; hooks now read the real Claude Code stdin contract** (2026-06-10, Roadmap Q3 Phase 1 #1). `prompt-context.sh` read `.user_prompt` but Claude Code's UserPromptSubmit sends `prompt` — `USER_PROMPT` was always empty → `exit 0` → per-prompt CMS recall, Jiminy guidance, /strict reformulation, the warm trigger, and retrieve-time Hebbian reinforcement **never fired in any session**. `post-tool-observe.py` read `tool_output` but PostToolUse sends `tool_response` — output always empty → every `go build`/`go test`/`pytest` recorded "Build/test succeeded" sight-unseen and real errors were never observed. Fixes (both live + installer-template copies, backward-compatible fallbacks): `.prompt // .user_prompt`; `tool_response` normalized (string|object|list); success classification requires non-empty clean output; the `RESULT_COUNT=0` early-exit no longer skips guidance/warm/reinforcement (independent deliveries); pre-compact transcript extraction reads the real `{message:{content:[…]}}` shape. Live-verified in the real session: first-ever guidance delivery (J17 T1 bootstrap + DICT, 5363 bytes vs 0 forever); real failing build → `error` observation with actual compiler output in CMS. Documented limitation: PostToolUse fires only on successful tool completion, so non-zero-exit commands aren't observed unless output is surfaced in a successful completion. Sprint in `docs/development/hookwire-001/`.

### Added

- **Automated post-merge dev-branch sync (`.github/workflows/sync-dev-after-merge.yml`)** (2026-06-10). Squash merges never advance the dev branch's merge-base, so every sprint touching CHANGELOG.md/CLAUDE.md hit CONFLICTING on its next PR (first bitten: PR #419). After each merged `*_dev*` PR, the workflow merges `main` back into the source branch and pushes (GITHUB_TOKEN pushes don't trigger other workflows, so no empty auto-PR — the PR #420 failure mode). Conflicts fail loudly for manual resolution; `workflow_dispatch` for manual runs. `auto-pr.yml` additionally skips PR creation when branch content is identical to `main` (guards manual sync pushes).

- **Sprint EVENTGRAPH-004 — `CONTRADICTS` event federation (last Hebbian write covered)** (2026-06-10). The `ApplyNegativeFeedback` contradict action (no co-activation edge → `MERGE CONTRADICTS`) now emits `reinforcement_events` rows with `trigger_path='apply_negative_feedback_contradict'` — completing Hebbian-write telemetry coverage. The `CONTRADICTS` MERGE lived inside a Cypher `FOREACH` (edge variable invisible to `RETURN`), so the single statement is split into two statements in the same transaction (weaken + contradict); classification is unchanged and the weaken telemetry is untouched. `created_new_edge` via the `updated_at IS NULL` invariant; `delta_weight` is the CONTRADICTS edge's **own** weight delta (+`negWeight` create / 0 re-match) — negative-feedback semantics carried by `trigger_path`, not the sign (consumer warning in the feature doc). **Data-decided scope:** reuses the existing V0022 sink (zero `CONTRADICTS` edges existed anywhere at ship time; `/v1/learning/negative-feedback` has **no automated producer** — disclosed follow-up) — this is telemetry-before-the-producer, the inverse of the dormancy pattern. Live-verified (Tier 3): contradict create + re-match rows, weaken unchanged, federation CLI surfaces the new trigger_path; UATS 5/5 (spec extended to pin zero-counts on nonexistent nodes). Also closes the EVENTGRAPH-003 follow-up with a 30h post-revival health review of `CoactivateSession` (no tuning needed; pre-fix orphans stay as historical record). Sprint in `docs/development/eventgraph-004/`.

- **Sprint EVENTGRAPH-003 — reinforcement-event coverage for the other Hebbian paths** (2026-06-09). EVENTGRAPH-001 federated only one of four Hebbian write paths (`ApplyCoactivation`). Now all four feed `reinforcement_events`, distinguished by `trigger_path`: `ApplySymbolCoactivation` (`apply_symbol_coactivation`), `CoactivateSession` (`coactivate_session`), and `ApplyNegativeFeedback` weaken-path (`apply_negative_feedback`, negative `delta_weight`). Each via a Cypher `RETURN`-extension + parse-and-record hook reusing the existing writer — **no schema/writer/endpoint change**; the federation API + `mdemg eventgraph reinforcement-neighborhood` surface the new events for free. The contradict path (`CONTRADICTS` edges) is deferred (not traversed by the federation walk). Cypher edits are `EXPLAIN`-validated + behavior-preserving (weight `SET` unchanged). Live-verified: all four `trigger_path`s emit rows; the federation read surfaces them. Sprint in `docs/development/eventgraph-003/`.

### Fixed

- **`CoactivateSession` was never invoked — session co-activation learning silently dead** (2026-06-09, discovered via EVENTGRAPH-003 live smoke). `conversation.NewServiceWithConfig` set `learningService=nil` pending `SetLearningService`, but that setter had **no caller** — so `Observe()`'s `if s.learningService != nil` guard always skipped `CoactivateSession`, and **0 `CO_ACTIVATED_WITH` edges between conversation observations had ever been created** (across 5495 such nodes in mdemg-dev). The function + Cypher were correct (proven by running it directly); it was just never called. Fix: `convSvc.SetLearningService(lea)` at construction. Live-verified: observations now create session co-activation edges. Same latent-dormancy class as EVENTGRAPH-001's RRF-`Activation`-drop — only live testing surfaces it.

## [0.10.1] - 2026-06-08

### Added

- **`jiminy-governance` Claude Code skill + MDEMG MCP registration** (2026-06-08). Ships the agent-facing front door to the J17 governance protocol: a Claude Code Agent Skill (`.claude/skills/jiminy-governance/`) that makes Jiminy the authoritative source of project context + constraints — query Jiminy at session start and before governed actions instead of relying on memory/stale docs. `.mcp.json` registers the MDEMG MCP server (`mdemg mcp`, stdio) exposing `jiminy_guide` / `validate_changes`, so the agent can *pull* guidance, not only receive the hook *push*. The skill is a routing/handshake shim (rules stay in the graph). Live-verified: full handshake (identify→request→comprehend→act→report) against the real instance with `GUIDANCE_OUTCOME` edges written. Wire-up resolution + integration-gap analysis + verification in `docs/development/jiminy-governance-skill/`.
- **Per-conversation SessionID across hooks + skill** (2026-06-08). Hooks and the skill previously hardcoded `session_id="claude-core"`, collapsing trust/escalation/observations from all Claude Code conversations into one shared MDEMG session. They now resolve `MDEMG_SESSION_ID` env → Claude Code stdin `session_id` (per-conversation) → `~/.mdemg/.claude-session` → `claude-core`, realizing J17's per-`(session,constraint)` isolation (env override preserves the old shared-identity behavior). `pre-write-check.py` (the `/strict` Write/Edit `/classify` gate) is now a tracked, installer-registered hook so its fix propagates via `mdemg hooks install`. Live-verified: an observation keyed to the per-conversation id in Neo4j. See `docs/development/jiminy-governance-skill/session-id-verification.md`.

- **Sprint NOSILENT-001 — Fail-loud scheduled jobs (no silent failures)** (2026-06-08). Triggered by a live-discovered silent failure: with `TSDB_BACKUP_ENABLED=true`, the backup scheduler ran every 24h, its `docker compose pg_dump` failing every time (docker not on the launchd PATH), surfacing only a buried `slog.Warn`. A core data-protection job, silently broken. This sprint kills the *class*.
  - **V0024 `scheduled_job_events` hypertable** + synchronous `RecordJobEvent` writer (mirrors V0021). One row per run of a scheduled job: `job_name`, `success`, `latency_ms`, `error_message`, `metadata`. Schema 23→24.
  - **`internal/jobhealth.Report`** — single policy point: record the outcome + fire a high-severity alert on failure (pool + dispatcher nil-safe). Wired into all three scheduled jobs: TSDB backup scheduler (decoupled result hook + server dispatcher), `maintenance` + `export-auto` (CLI → short-lived pool + file-backed dispatcher writing the same `~/.mdemg/alerts/current.json` the hooks surface).
  - **Two server-native alert rules** over `scheduled_job_events`: `scheduled_job_recent_failure` (any failure in `JOB_FAILURE_LOOKBACK_MIN`) and `backup_no_recent_success` (zero successful backups in the staleness window — **the "job never ran" guarantee**, firing from the server observing *absent* success, gated on `TSDB_BACKUP_ENABLED`). Distinct alert services so the `(service, severity)` cooldown never lets one mask the other.
  - **Config-driven** (no hardcoded literals): `JOB_HEALTH_ALERT_ENABLED` (default true), `JOB_BACKUP_STALENESS_HOURS` (0 → derive from backup interval × 2), `JOB_FAILURE_LOOKBACK_MIN` (60).
  - **Live-verified (Tier 3):** induced a real `maintenance` failure → immediate alert + failure row + evaluator failure rule fired; the staleness rule fired for the never-succeeded backup; both surfaced as distinct alert-file entries. A live-caught cooldown-collision (both rules sharing one service → one masked the other) was fixed in-sprint. Feature doc: `docs/features/scheduled-job-health.md`. Sprint: `docs/development/nosilent-001/`.

- **Sprint EVENTGRAPH-002 — Guidance-outcome federation (Pattern Y1, second event class)** (2026-06-08). Federates the guidance-outcome event stream: walk a constraint's Neo4j neighborhood, surface the time-windowed `constraint_outcomes` (followed/ignored/contradicted) for the constraint **and its graph-related constraints**. Answers "how well is this constraint and its conceptual cluster being followed?" — which `GetConstraintEffectiveness` (per-constraint, no neighborhood, no time-series) can't.
  - **Endpoint:** `POST /v1/eventgraph/guidance-outcome-neighborhood`. **CLI:** `mdemg eventgraph guidance-outcome-neighborhood` (`--seed`/`--query`/`--hops`/`--since`/`--limit`/`--json`; table shows followed/ignored split + per-outcome code/outcome/sim/guidance_id, or `--json`).
  - **Reuses the existing `constraint_outcomes` sink** — no new hypertable/writer/enqueue site (RRF-SCALE-001 already populates it). Data-decided: a parallel sink would duplicate populated data.
  - **Joins graph↔events on `constraint_code`** (TSDB `constraint_id` is a UUID that doesn't match the Neo4j `node_id` CUID; code is the only viable key). Walk collects neighborhood codes → query `constraint_outcomes WHERE constraint_code = ANY(codes)` → Go-side join resolves each outcome's `constraint_node_id`.
  - **Single-source refactor:** extracted the shared federation gate (`eventgraphGate`) + default-resolution (`resolveFederationDefaults`, hops/since/limit/ceiling) into helpers used by **both** the reinforcement and guidance-outcome handlers — one place to change the rules. Reinforcement endpoint un-regressed (UATS still 6/6 live).
  - **Tests:** Tier 1 (`-race`: validation, empty-arrays-not-null, join, CLI request-mapping/render); Tier 2 (live integration round-trip hops=1/0/unknown-seed + UATS 6/6 live); Tier 3 (real binary cross-checked against direct SQL — **11 outcomes = 11, all followed**; the `--query` "0 outcomes" result traced to SQL ground truth as correct, not a join bug). Sprint in `docs/development/eventgraph-002/`. Feature doc: `docs/features/event-graph-federation.md`.

### Changed

- **TSDB schema version 23 → 24** (NOSILENT-001 V0024). Additive migration `024_scheduled_job_events.sql` adds the `scheduled_job_events` hypertable (scheduled-job run outcomes). `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` default bumped to 24. No data change; idempotent; rollback = `DROP TABLE` + revert the default.

- **TSDB schema version 22 → 23** (EVENTGRAPH-002 V0023). Additive migration `023_constraint_outcomes_code_index.sql` adds `idx_constraint_outcomes_code (space_id, constraint_code, time DESC)` to back the guidance-outcome federation join. `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` default bumped to 23. No data change; idempotent; rollback = `DROP INDEX` + revert the default.

- **Sprint EVENTGRAPH-CLI-001 — `mdemg eventgraph reinforcement-neighborhood`: the first consumer of the federation API** (2026-06-08). EVENTGRAPH-001 shipped the `POST /v1/eventgraph/reinforcement-neighborhood` federation endpoint but nothing consumed it. This sprint ships the operator-facing CLI consumer + a live-testing harness for the EVENTGRAPH line (002/003 follow on).
  - **CLI:** `mdemg eventgraph reinforcement-neighborhood` walks the graph from a seed and renders the reinforcement (Hebbian co-activation) events touching that neighborhood — a summary + table (`new` column = `created_new_edge`, `nbhd` = which endpoints fell inside the N-hop walk), or `--json` for piping. Seed via `--seed n_…` or `--query "<text>"` (resolves the seed from the top `/v1/memory/retrieve` result). `--hops`/`--since`/`--limit` are **omitted from the request when unset** so the server applies its own config defaults — no second copy of the defaults hardcoded in the CLI (single source of truth).
  - **UATS contract:** `eventgraph_reinforcement_neighborhood.uats.json` (6 cases, validated 6/6 live) backfills the contract-test gap EVENTGRAPH-001 left: happy-200 response shape, required-field 400s, negative-hops 400, over-ceiling 400, GET 405. sha256-hashed.
  - **Live verification (Tier 3):** the real binary against the real stack surfaced **20 reinforcement events in a 5-node neighborhood**, demonstrating the full Hebbian-write → federation-read loop closing in one command (the `--query` retrieval fired `ApplyCoactivation`, and the federation read those very events back). `--limit` truncation, `--json`, unknown-seed, and no-arg error paths all verified live. See `docs/development/eventgraph-cli-001/verification.md`.

### Fixed

- **`docker` CLI unresolvable under the launchd minimal PATH — server-runtime shellouts silently failing** (2026-06-08). The native server (launchd) inherits `PATH=/usr/bin:/bin:/usr/sbin:/sbin`, which excludes the Docker Desktop symlink (`/usr/local/bin/docker`). Two server-runtime `docker` shellouts failed with "executable file not found in $PATH": (1) Neo4j container CPU/mem stats logged an ERROR every 60s and left the `neo4j_container_*` gauges empty (so the `neo4j_high_cpu/_memory` alert rules had no data); (2) the TSDB backup scheduler's `docker compose pg_dump` failed with only a `slog.Warn`. The data plane was never affected — Neo4j (Bolt) + TSDB (pgx) connect over mapped TCP ports, not the docker CLI. Fix: new `internal/dockerbin` resolver (`MDEMG_DOCKER_BIN` env → `exec.LookPath` → well-known install locations → graceful unavailable), wired into both shellouts; the perpetual ERROR downgraded to a one-shot WARN when docker is genuinely absent; a sane `PATH` added to the launchd server plist template. Live-verified: container gauges now land in `metric_samples`; backup resolves docker under a simulated minimal PATH. (`export-auto` was **not** a victim — it exports via network SQL, not docker.)

- **`neighbor_node_ids` serialized as `null` (not `[]`) for an empty neighborhood — caught in EVENTGRAPH-CLI-001 live contract testing** (2026-06-08). `eventgraph.walkNeighborhood` returns a nil slice when the seed has no neighborhood (e.g. an unknown seed), which JSON-marshals to `null`, while the sibling `events` field was already initialized to `[]`. Both are array fields and must serialize consistently — `null` breaks any consumer asserting an array type (incl. the new UATS contract's `type_is array`). `EventsInGraphNeighborhood` now coalesces the nil slice to `[]string{}`; `TestFederationResult_EmptyArraysNotNull` pins the contract. Standard Go unit tests missed it (they don't marshal the empty result); the live UATS happy-path against the running server caught it immediately — its own fix-commit per the live-smoke precedent.


- **Follow-up C — `/v1/jiminy/latest` JSON control-char escaping: investigated, closed as NON-ISSUE** (2026-06-08). The last open item from the RRF-SCALE-001 live-smoke triage. Determined to be a client-side shell artifact, **not** a server bug — no code change. Evidence: `writeJSON` uses `encoding/json` (`json.NewEncoder().Encode`) which always escapes control chars; the synthesized guidance narrative is double-`StripControlChars`'d (synthesizer + service); and `prompt-context.sh` already strips control chars via `perl` before `jq` (with `2>/dev/null` + `// empty` fallbacks). The hook's exact `guidance_id` extraction returns correctly live, and 5 rapid `/latest` fetches all parse as strict-valid JSON. **This closes the entire RRF-SCALE-001 follow-up triage (A → JIMINY-OUTCOME-001, B → GUIDANCE-SYNTH-001, C → non-issue); the guidance→feedback→outcome loop is fully functional end-to-end.** See `docs/development/followup-c-closure.md`.

- **Sprint GUIDANCE-SYNTH-001 — Fix guidance synthesis timeout (Follow-up B)** (2026-06-08). LLM guidance **synthesis failed on every production warm call** (`synthesis_error: context deadline exceeded`; 6/6 `jiminy.synthesize` errored), so the hook never delivered the synthesized guidance narrative — it fell back to static formatting. Root cause (measured): the hook's `/v1/jiminy/warm` path runs a background `Guide()` with a **hardcoded 30s timeout** (even though `JIMINY_TIMEOUT_MS=240s` is configured), inside which the per-node constraint classifier ran **serially** (`consulting.classify` ~1.55s avg × ~10 nodes ≈ 15s on cache-miss), leaving only ~15s for synthesis which needs 8–27s (max 27s) → deadline-exceeded every time.
  - **Fix 1 — parallelize the per-node classifier:** `consulting.findApplicableConstraints` now classifies candidates with **bounded concurrency** (`CONSULTING_CLASSIFY_CONCURRENCY`, default 4 to match llama-server `--parallel 4`; floor 1 = serial). Gate-first to fix a stable order, classify into position-indexed slots via a semaphore-bounded worker pool, then collect-in-order + dedup — **output is identical to serial** (Tier 1 determinism test). Extracted `constraintClassifierIface` so the concurrent path is unit-testable; Tier 1 `-race` clean.
  - **Fix 2 — config-drive the warm-compute timeout:** replaced the hardcoded 30s (`handlers_jiminy.go`) with `JIMINY_WARM_COMPUTE_TIMEOUT_MS` (default 90000) — headroom for the parallel classifier + a slow synthesis. No-hardcoding rule.
  - **Live verification:** the warm path now produces a synthesized narrative — `synthesis_used=true`, **no `synthesis_error`**, 1892-char augmentation. A fresh `jiminy.synthesize` call **succeeded at 50.7s latency** — it completed only because the budget is now 90s (would have died at 30s; even 60s was risky), validating the default. Both fixes needed: parallelizing alone wouldn't survive a 50s synthesis in 30s; raising the budget alone leaves guide calls slow.
  - **Tests:** Tier 1 (5, `-race`: parallel==serial determinism, parallel-faster, error→keyword fallback, score-gate preserved, concurrency default); Tier 2 integration (skip-on-empty + LLM-tolerant: warm path produces guidance without `synthesis_error`). No behavior regression — guidance still surfaces constraints + codes still attach (RRF-SCALE-001 + JIMINY-OUTCOME-001 intact). Sprint in `docs/development/guidance-synth-001/`.
  - **Note:** local-model synthesis latency is high (~50s for a ~1900-char narrative) — config-tunable via the budget / `JIMINY_GUIDANCE_OUTPUT_MAX_CHARS`; worth watching but the warm path is fire-and-forget so it doesn't block the hook. **Closes Follow-up B**; Follow-up C (`/v1/jiminy/latest` JSON control-char escaping) remains the last open item.

- **Sprint JIMINY-OUTCOME-001 — Revive the Neo4j GUIDANCE_OUTCOME edge sink (completes the guidance-loop revival)** (2026-06-08). RRF-SCALE-001 revived guidance surfacing + the **TSDB** `constraint_outcomes` sink, but the **Neo4j** `GUIDANCE_OUTCOME` edge sink stayed dormant (893 edges, last 2026-04-12). This was **Follow-up A**: a distinct root cause from the score scale. `matchConstraintCode` linked guidance items to constraint codes by **keyword overlap (≥3 shared words)**, but retrieval surfaces `emergent_concept` abstractions whose content rarely shares 3+ literal words with raw constraint text → no `constraint_code` assigned → `PersistGuidanceOutcome` fell back to the concept SourceNode → its `role_type='constraint'` filter rejected `emergent_concept` → no edge. (Confirmed: all 17 recent outcome rows had `constraint_code=(none)`.)
  - **Fix:** new `matchConstraintCodeByEmbedding` (`internal/jiminy/service.go`) queries the **constraint vector index** (`db.index.vector.queryNodes`, `role_type='constraint'`, `sim ≥ threshold`) and returns the closest constraint's code — mirroring the proven `Evaluator.findMatchingConstraints` pattern. `Guide()` tries this first, falling back to the keyword matcher when the embedder is unavailable, content is empty, or nothing clears the threshold — **never regresses**. The existing `PersistGuidanceOutcome` + `findConstraintNodeID` then create the edge on the *correct* constraint node (so `GetConstraintEffectiveness`, which reads `role_type='constraint'` edges, works too). The guidance content is already normalized to natural language (`normalizeGuidanceContent`, ~0.70 cosine) — embedding matching is what that prep was for.
  - **Config:** `JIMINY_CONSTRAINT_CODE_SIM_THRESHOLD` (default 0.55, zero-value fallback) — validated live; tune-able. No-hardcoding rule.
  - **Live verification:** guidance items carrying a `constraint_code` went **0 → 6/10** (matched code `no-direct-main-commits` — semantically exact for "commit to main"); the full loop produced **6 fresh `GUIDANCE_OUTCOME` edges on real `role_type='constraint'` nodes** (893 → 899, dated today); `/v1/constraints/effectiveness` updated ("NEVER commit directly to main | surfaced: 30 followed: 28 rate: 0.93"). **Both sinks now revived** — TSDB (RRF-SCALE-001) + Neo4j (here); the constraint-effectiveness loop is fully restored.
  - **Tests:** Tier 1 (4 — guards, threshold fallback, keyword non-regression); Tier 2 integration (skip-on-empty + LLM-warmth-tolerant — passes on idle stack, skips under LLM contention, never false-fails). Sprint plan + verification + post in `docs/development/jiminy-outcome-001/`.
  - **Still-open follow-ups (unchanged):** B (LLM guidance synthesis timeout — the per-node classifier serializes ~31s/call; `synthesis_error: context deadline exceeded`), C (`/v1/jiminy/latest` JSON control-char escaping).

- **Sprint RRF-SCALE-001 — RRF score-scale consumer remediation (revives the dormant Jiminy guidance loop)** (2026-06-03). The Jiminy guidance→feedback→outcome loop had been silently inactive for ~9 weeks. Root cause: `internal/consulting/service.go` gated constraint/suggestion/conflict extraction on **hardcoded legacy-scale score thresholds** (`r.Score < 0.55`, `> 0.6`, `> 0.65`, `> 0.7`) calibrated for the legacy linear scorer. When Column-Voting RRF became the default scorer (Phase 13.1, ~2026-05-03), the fused-score scale dropped — strong semantic matches now top out ~0.49–0.58 — so every retrieved node fell below the gates, `consulting.Suggest` returned empty, and Jiminy synthesized no guidance → no `guidance_id` captured → no feedback → no constraint outcomes recorded. **Third instance of this bug class** (after EVENTGRAPH-001's RRF `Activation`-drop and the Hebbian no-op): downstream consumers hardcoding the retrieval-score contract that Phase 13.1 silently changed.
  - **Audit sweep** (`docs/development/rrf-scale-001/audit_findings.md`) catalogs all 12 post-RRF score/activation/confidence consumers: 4 High (the consulting constraint-gate cluster + confidence sigmoid), 5 Med (conflict gates + the already-config-driven minConfidence pre-filter), 3 Low (retrieval/jiminy.go Activation *display* gates — traced, always-additive at RRF scale, intentionally left with rationale), 2 None (a J17 trial score + trust-score clamp, not retrieval scores).
  - **Fix:** replaced the 7 hardcoded gates + the score→confidence sigmoid (`retrievalScoreMidpoint/Steepness` 1.5/1.5, in both `consulting/service.go` and `jiminy/retrieval_source.go` — they must stay in sync) with **config-driven, RRF-calibrated** values (no-hardcoding rule: the bug *was* a hardcoded value). 5 new knobs: `CONSULTING_CONSTRAINT_SCORE_FLOOR` (0.45), `CONSULTING_AUTHORITY_SCORE_FLOOR` (0.45), `CONSULTING_CONFLICT_SCORE_FLOOR` (0.45), `RETRIEVAL_CONFIDENCE_SIGMOID_MIDPOINT` (0.45), `RETRIEVAL_CONFIDENCE_SIGMOID_STEEPNESS` (8.0). All with zero-value fallbacks. Sigmoid recalibration maps a strong 0.5 match from 0.18 confidence (legacy) → 0.60.
  - **Key Epic-2 finding:** `keywordClassifyConstraint` has an *inner* authority gate that binds tighter than the outer constraint gate — so the authority floor must also be 0.45 or the binding gate re-rejects the strong-match band and the loop stays dormant. The RRF band (0.49–0.58) is too compressed to subdivide into tiers; the knobs stay separate so operators can raise any one independently.
  - **Considered + rejected:** gating on `NormalizedConfidence` percentile (Option A) — the live distribution showed it is a *positional* rank (spreads 100→0 even on uniform-score sets), so it would admit noise. Chose config-driven absolute thresholds (Option B), disclosed per `feedback_plan_options_pattern.md`.
  - **Live verification** (`docs/development/rrf-scale-001/verification.md`): `/v1/jiminy/guide` guidance items **0 → 10**, `source_counts.constraints` **0 → 2**; the full warm→latest→feedback loop revived the **TSDB `constraint_outcomes` sink** (dead since 2026-05-01 → fresh rows dated today). Tier 1: 4 new/updated consulting unit tests; Tier 2: 2 integration tests (suggestions surface for a matching context, gibberish doesn't flood).
  - **Surfaced follow-ups (distinct root causes, not score-scale, documented not bolted on):** (A) the Neo4j `GUIDANCE_OUTCOME` edge sink stays dormant because retrieval surfaces `emergent_concept` abstractions and `PersistGuidanceOutcome` only writes edges for constraint/correction-typed targets — a node-type-targeting issue independent of RRF (candidate sprint JIMINY-OUTCOME-001); (B) LLM guidance synthesis timeout now that synthesis runs; (C) `/v1/jiminy/latest` unescaped control chars break `jq`/json parsers (the hook uses `jq`).
  - **Structural defense:** `CLAUDE.md` records a "score-scale contract" — downstream consumers must not hardcode absolute thresholds against `RetrieveResult.Score`, since the scorer scale is not a stable contract. Sprint plan + audit + verification + post in `docs/development/rrf-scale-001/`.

### Added

- **Sprint EVENTGRAPH-001 — Reinforcement-event TSDB hypertable + graph federation (Pattern Y1)** (2026-05-27). First implementation of the TypeDB-inspired Neo4j refactor: federate "events about edges" into TSDB rather than reify them as graph nodes; preserve graph traversal via a Go orchestration layer.
  - **V0022 `reinforcement_events` hypertable** — one row per Hebbian co-activation pair update written by `internal/learning/service.go::ApplyCoactivation`. 21 columns including `prev/new/delta_weight`, `evidence_count_after`, `eta_effective`, `surprise_factor`, `activation_product`, `path_sim`, `role_a/b`, `obs_type_a/b`, `session_id`, `direction` (`forward|reverse|bidirectional`), `created_new_edge` (distinguishes "new connection formed" from "existing connection strengthened"), `trigger_path` (v1: always `apply_coactivation`). 7-day chunks; 4 indexes for federation lookups + session drilldown. `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` default bumped 21→22.
  - **Buffered CopyFrom writer** (`internal/tsdb/reinforcement_writer.go`) — mirrors V0019 SparseGateMetricsWriter pattern. 30s auto-flush, FIFO eviction on buffer-full with droppedRows counter, Close() drains. Pattern explicitly chosen over V0021 sync-INSERT because Hebbian writes are per-retrieve volume, far higher than CLI-driven model install events. Tier 1: 9 unit tests.
  - **Cypher RETURN-shape change** in `ApplyCoactivation` — extended from `count(*)` to 17 per-pair columns. `parseReinforcementRow` helper in `internal/learning/reinforcement_parser.go` translates `neo4j.Record` (or any `(key)→(any,bool)` getter) into a `tsdb.ReinforcementEventRow`; defensive against missing keys, nil values, wrong types. Tier 1: 6 unit tests.
  - **Plan-deviation disclosure** (per `feedback_plan_options_pattern.md`): plan called for 2 rows per pair in asymmetric mode; final implementation emits 1 row per logical pair regardless of mode (`direction` column carries the distinction). Rationale: the Cypher mirrors `rr.weight = r.weight` at all times — forward and reverse weights are identical, so 2 rows would double-count without adding signal.
  - **Federation API** — `POST /v1/eventgraph/reinforcement-neighborhood` orchestrates a Cypher graph walk from a seed (depth 0..hops via `CO_ACTIVATED_WITH | GENERALIZES`) + TSDB query for events touching the neighborhood + Go-side join with `src_in_neighborhood` / `dst_in_neighborhood` annotation. Empty seed short-circuits before TSDB call. Auth: same `AUTH_API_KEYS` convention as `/v1/admin/breakers`. 503 when feature disabled or TSDB unavailable at boot; 400 on missing/negative/over-ceiling inputs.
  - **Configurability Contract** — 7 new env vars: `EVENTGRAPH_ENABLED` (default true), `EVENTGRAPH_WRITER_FLUSH_INTERVAL_SEC` (30, floor 5), `EVENTGRAPH_WRITER_BUFFER_SIZE` (1000, 0=unlimited), `EVENTGRAPH_MAX_PAIRS_PER_EVENT_BATCH` (200), `EVENTGRAPH_MAX_EVENTS_PER_QUERY` (500), `EVENTGRAPH_FEDERATION_DEFAULT_HOPS` (2), `EVENTGRAPH_FEDERATION_DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_HOURS` (24).
  - **Observability** — 3 Prometheus counters (`mdemg_eventgraph_writer_rows_{enqueued,dropped}_total`, `mdemg_eventgraph_writer_flush_failure_total`) wired via a narrow `PrometheusCounter` interface that avoids the tsdb↔metrics import cycle. New Grafana panel "Reinforcement Event Rate (events/min)" on `mdemg-graph-topology`, plots all three rates with dropped colored orange + failures red.
  - **Tier 2 integration tests** (3 against real Neo4j + TSDB): writer roundtrip with flush-window, writer Close drain, federation end-to-end (seed--mid--leaf + off-node, hops=0 vs hops=1, in-neighborhood flags).
  - **Tier 3 live e2e** — 3 retrieves against `mdemg-dev` → 10 reinforcement events landed in TSDB within 35s flush window; federation API at hops=1 returned 5-node neighborhood + 10 in-neighborhood events. Documented in `docs/development/eventgraph-001/verification.md`.
  - **Surprise-bug fix-commit (separate)** — `internal/retrieval/scoring_rrf.go::ScoreAndRankRRF` was dropping the `Activation` field when converting `ConsensusResult.Ranked → []RetrieveResult`. The legacy `ScoreAndRank` path at `scoring.go:883` sets it; the RRF path (default-on since Phase 13.1, 2026-05-03) silently dropped it. Net effect: `ApplyCoactivation` filtered out every L0 candidate (Activation=0 < `LearningMinActivation`=0.20), and the retrieve-time Hebbian goroutine has been a silent no-op for ~24 days. CO_ACTIVATED_WITH edges still got written via sidecar paths (`CoactivateSession`, `ApplySymbolCoactivation`, consolidation walks). One-line fix: `Activation: act[c.NodeID]` in `scoring_rrf.go`. Forward-only — no backfill. Discovered during Epic 7 live smoke per CLAUDE.md "Testing — Live System Testing Is Required" policy.
  - Sprint plan + verification + feature doc + post in [`docs/development/eventgraph-001/`](docs/development/eventgraph-001/) and [`docs/features/event-graph-federation.md`](docs/features/event-graph-federation.md).

- **Sprint MODEL-DIST-002 — Adapter-only distribution path shipped** (2026-05-25). Lifts the adapter-deferral from MODEL-DIST-001. `mdemg model pull --adapter` now resolves `reh3376/mdemg-llm-v1-adapter:latest` from Ollama Library (257 MB GGUF LoRA, f16, 560 tensors over 40 layers × 7 target modules × 2). Operators with their own Qwen3-14B base (or who want a small download instead of the ~9 GB fused GGUF) can layer mdemg's Phase 5 SFT via `llama-server --model <base.gguf> --lora <adapter.gguf>`.
  - **Pipeline**: MLX safetensors → `scripts/mlx_adapter_to_peft.py` (key renaming + tensor transposition for PEFT single-adapter layout) → `scripts/vendor/llama_cpp/convert_lora_to_gguf.py` (pinned to llama.cpp b9000 release — upstream master refactored into a `conversion/` package and lost self-containedness) → GGUF LoRA. 14 Tier 1 unit tests pin the converter (`scripts/mlx_adapter_to_peft_test.py`).
  - **Epic 3 live verification**: side-by-side inference on port 18103 (base + adapter via `llama-server --lora`) vs production port 8102 (fused merged GGUF) returns semantically-aligned outputs on sanity prompts. Documented in `docs/development/model-dist-002/verification.md`.
  - **Ollama Library publication**: `ollama create reh3376/mdemg-llm-v1-adapter:latest -f packaging/ollama/Modelfile.adapter` + `ollama push`; published manifest digest `sha256:57b98b97ede0e340e8c530aabf579136616ba670281fe04b14777164e655c278`, adapter blob SHA `0cfaf4bae3215a4aea664a8d28ae9a41d73ee740cbcce5c2eef950232cfe1de5`, mediaType `application/vnd.ollama.image.adapter`.
  - **CLI enablement**: `internal/cli/model_fetcher_ollama.go::readModelBlobDigest` now switches on `req.Adapter` to target the `image.adapter` mediaType (vs `image.model` for fused); `destFilename()` helper writes adapter symlinks at `<name>-adapter.gguf` (no quant suffix, since a single adapter applies to any quant level of the base). `internal/cli/model.go::runModelPull` SHA-verifies against `mf.Adapter` for adapter pulls. `ErrAdapterDeferred` sentinel retained for future non-Ollama backends that ship fused-only first.
  - **Manifest updates**: both embedded (`internal/cli/quant_manifest.json`) and canonical (`docs/development/model-dist-001/quant_manifest.json`) carry the adapter SHA + Ollama manifest digest + `tensor_count: 560` + `target_base: qwen3:14b`.
  - **Tier 3 e2e**: `mdemg model pull --adapter` end-to-end in 987 ms wall-clock; SHA verify ok against embedded manifest; symlink at `~/.mdemg/models/mdemg-llm-v1-adapter.gguf`; llama-server load of base + adapter returns coherent inference.
  - Sprint plan + verification + post in [`docs/development/model-dist-002/`](docs/development/model-dist-002/).

### Fixed

- **Sprint GRAFANA-AUDIT-001 — Dashboard audit + 5 panels recovered** (2026-05-21). Rigorous per-panel audit of all 165 target executions across 146 panels in 8 Grafana dashboards using new harness `scripts/grafana_panel_audit.py` (17 Tier 1 unit tests). Pre-sprint: 125 PASS / 19 EMPTY / 3 FAIL / 18 SKIP. Post-Epic-3: 130 PASS / 17 EMPTY / **0 FAIL** / 18 SKIP.
  - `mdemg-llm-routing.json` (3 panels) — fixed unquoted `$space_id` in WHERE clauses that caused PostgreSQL to parse `mdemg-dev = ''` as the subtraction operator. All 3 LLM-routing panels (call distribution / latency percentiles / error rate) recovered.
  - `mdemg-j17.json :: Total Events` — fixed `metric_type='counter'` filter that mismatched server's `'gauge'` emission for `mdemg_j17_events_total`.
  - `mdemg-rsic.json :: Action Success Rate (t0)` — fixed `labels->>'status'='success'` filter that mismatched server's `'completed'` emission for `mdemg_rsic_action_total`.
  - Surfaced server-side observability regression: 4 metrics (`mdemg_rsic_calibration_confidence`, `mdemg_rsic_snapshot_created_total`, `mdemg_rsic_trigger_rejected_total`, `mdemg_rsic_safety_blocked_total`) stopped emitting around 2026-05-07/08; current codebase grep finds zero references to these names — emission code was removed somewhere. Documented as known gap; server-side restore queued as follow-up.
  - New feature doc: [`docs/features/observability-dashboards.md`](docs/features/observability-dashboards.md) — per-dashboard inventory, audit verdicts, known gaps, operator playbook for detecting + fixing dashboard regressions via the harness.
  - Sprint plan + audit results + findings + post in [`docs/development/grafana-audit-001/`](docs/development/grafana-audit-001/).

## [0.10.0] - 2026-05-11

### Added

- **Sprint MODEL-DIST-001 — Local LoRA Distribution via Ollama Library** (2026-05-11, Epics 0–5 + 7 landed; Epic 3 ollama push + Epic 6 Tier 3 e2e gated on operator confirmation; adapter path deferred to MODEL-DIST-002).
  - **New CLI: `mdemg model pull|list|verify|remove|where`.** One-command path from `brew install mdemg` to a working local LLM. `pull` resolves quant via RAM auto-detection (default: Q4_K_M for <16 GB Macs, Q5_K_M for <24 GB, Q8_0 for 32+ GB) or explicit `--quant`. Symlinks the resolved blob into `<MDEMG_MODEL_DIR>` and prints the `.env` lines to wire in. Feature doc: `docs/features/local-model-distribution.md`.
  - **Pluggable `Fetcher` interface** (`internal/cli/model_fetcher.go`) — v1 ships `OllamaFetcher` only; future backends (`HFFetcher`, `S3Fetcher`, `GitHubReleaseFetcher`, `FileFetcher`) plug in via factory dispatch on `MDEMG_MODEL_BACKEND` without touching the CLI surface. Adding the next backend is one new file + one factory branch.
  - **Configurability Contract** — every operator-visible value is dynamic (memory: `feedback_no_hardcoded_values.md`). 11 env vars + flag overrides + sensible defaults tuned to the v1 production reality, so `mdemg model pull` with no flags Just Works. Knobs: `MDEMG_MODEL_BACKEND`, `MDEMG_MODEL_NAMESPACE`, `MDEMG_MODEL_NAME`, `MDEMG_MODEL_QUANTS`, `MDEMG_MODEL_RAM_TIERS`, `MDEMG_MODEL_QUANT`, `MDEMG_ADAPTER_BASE`, `MDEMG_MODEL_DIR`, `OLLAMA_MODELS`, `OLLAMA_HOST`, `MDEMG_MODEL_MANIFEST_PATH`. 22 unit tests pin the contract (`internal/cli/model_test.go`).
  - **3 fused GGUF quants built** (Epic 1, all live-smoke-verified):
    - Q4_K_M: ~9.0 GB, 12 GB min RAM, 4.87 BPW, SHA `401161710c22f0ae...411d42ea`
    - Q5_K_M: ~11 GB, 14 GB min RAM, 5.69 BPW, SHA `144ad723101d688f...d5f5d54` (production canonical, Phase 13.5)
    - Q8_0: ~16 GB, 20 GB min RAM, 8.50 BPW, SHA `fc14dcb40af1bb58...8db6089`
    - Pipeline: `mlx_lm.fuse --dequantize` (mlx-community/Qwen3-14B-4bit + adapters/tier1/) → bf16 HF safetensors → `convert_hf_to_gguf.py --outtype f16` → `llama-quantize`. Required installing gguf/sentencepiece/protobuf into `neural/.venv` (homebrew's `/opt/homebrew/bin/convert_hf_to_gguf.py` uses system python which lacks these). SHAs canonicalized in `docs/development/model-dist-001/quant_manifest.json`; runtime mirror embedded at `internal/cli/quant_manifest.json` via embed.FS for SHA verification.
  - **3 Modelfiles + local `ollama create` done** (Epic 3): `packaging/ollama/Modelfile.{Q4_K_M,Q5_K_M,Q8_0}` with `FROM ./../../.local-models/...` relative paths, `num_ctx 32768`, `num_predict 4096`, stop tokens, Apache-2.0 LICENSE. Local ollama IDs captured. `ollama push` deferred — one-way action, gated on operator confirmation (`reh3376` namespace claim + API token on ollama.com).
  - **TSDB V0021 `model_install_events` hypertable** (Epic 5) — 7-day chunks, 3 indexes (quant-time, failed-events partial, backend-event-time). Columns: event_id CUIDv2 PK, recorded_at, event_type (pull/verify/remove), backend_name, namespace, model_name, quant, adapter, success, latency_ms, sha256, size_bytes, err_message (1 KB cap). `internal/tsdb/model_install_writer.go::RecordModelInstallEvent` is synchronous single-row INSERT (CLI is one-shot, writes infrequent — different pattern from V0017/V0018/V0019/V0020 retrieval-path buffered writers). Wired into all 3 mutating CLI commands with 2s connect timeout + nil-pool no-op for degraded mode. `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` default bumped 20 → 21. Grafana panels deferred to Sprint B.
  - **Adapter (LoRA-only) path deferred to MODEL-DIST-002** per the planned contingency. The MLX adapter at `adapters/tier1/adapters.safetensors` (514 MB, Phase 5 SFT Iter 2400 best) requires MLX → PEFT tensor transposition (`lora_a` `(in, rank)` → PEFT `(rank, in)`; same for `lora_b`) + a `convert_lora_to_gguf.py` script that ships with llama.cpp source but not in `brew install llama.cpp`. Estimated 80–95 min vs ~30 min Epic 2 budget; hit the contingency criterion documented in `docs/development/model-dist-001/epic_2_forensic.md`. `--adapter` flag accepted at parse time but errors with `ErrAdapterDeferred` and the MODEL-DIST-002 forward reference; flag machinery preserved for forward-compat.
  - **Scope explicitly out for v1**: Linux/CUDA inference path, vLLM multi-LoRA, HF Hub publish (slot reserved as `HFFetcher`), GitHub Releases fallback, sigstore signing, gated/enterprise access, `mdemg model fuse|export|import`. Apple Silicon + Ollama Library only for v1.
  - **Sprint plan**: `docs/development/model-dist-001/sprint_plan_model_dist_001.md` (12-section v1.0 format). Estimated 5–7 dev-days, OpenAI spend $0, risk Medium.

## [0.9.0] - 2026-05-06

### Breaking

- **Phase 13.5 production LLM runtime cutover (port 8101 → 8102)** — `mlx_lm.server` on port 8101 has been replaced by `llama-server` (llama.cpp b9000+, OpenAI-compat) on port 8102 serving `mdemg-llm-v1.Q5_K_M.gguf`. Operators with `LLM_ENDPOINT=http://127.0.0.1:8101/v1` (or `http://host.docker.internal:8101/v1` in a containerized `.env`) will fail mdemg's startup preflight after upgrading. **Migration**: edit `.env` and change the port `8101` → `8102` everywhere. The `com.mdemg.llama-server.plist` LaunchAgent is auto-installed by the formula's `post_install` hook (`mdemg upgrade --docker-only`). Rollback path: restore `~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.mdemg.mlx-server.plist.disabled-phase13_5`, set `LLM_ENDPOINT=http://127.0.0.1:8101/v1`, bootout llama-server, bootstrap mlx-server (will reintroduce the ~14-min Metal-OOM crash cycle that motivated the cutover). Full rationale in CLAUDE.md "Local LLM Runtime — llama.cpp llama-server (Phase 13.5 cutover)".
- **`MLX_*` env vars deprecated in favor of `LLM_*`** — Phase 13.6 renamed the watchdog/preflight env-var family. Legacy `MLX_*` names continue to work but emit `WARN config: env var deprecated, please rename` at boot. Aliases removable ≥1 release cycle from this commit. Migration: rename `MLX_WATCHDOG_ENABLED` → `LLM_WATCHDOG_ENABLED`, `MLX_PROBE_INTERVAL_SEC` → `LLM_PROBE_INTERVAL_SEC`, `MLX_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SEC` → `LLM_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SEC`, `MLX_FAIL_FAST_ENABLED` → `LLM_FAIL_FAST_ENABLED`, `MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_MLX` → `MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_LLM`. Internal Go package (`internal/mlxprobe/`) and Prometheus metric prefix (`mdemg_mlx_*`) retained as operator-invisible / dashboard-coupled.

The Phase 14.2.2 forensic identified 3 categories where the column consistently regressed (`service_relationships` -0.043, `business_logic_constraints` -0.023, `relationship` -0.017). This sprint zero-weights the column on those 3 categories while keeping default `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_WEIGHT=0.10` elsewhere — mirror of Phase 14.1's per-category sparse-gate dispatch. New env knob `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_CATEGORY_WEIGHTS` (JSON map) with the 3-category zero-weight default seed; operator JSON value REPLACES seed. **Defaults flipped**: `CONTEXT_FINGERPRINT_ENABLED` `false→true`, `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_ENABLED` `false→true`. Per-category breakdown post-flip: `architecture_structure` +0.030, `computed_value` +0.070, `data_flow_integration` +0.010, `business_logic_constraints` +0.005 (was -0.023, now neutral), `service_relationships` -0.010 (was -0.043). std dropped 0.072→0.049 — much tighter retrieval scores. min jumped 0.000→0.350 — zero-score rescues stick. Operator opt-out: `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_ENABLED=false`. Post: [`phase_14_2_3_post.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_14_2_3_post.md). ### Added

- **POST-FT-LORA-PHASE14.2.1: Vector-based query→fingerprint derivation (narrow close)** (2026-05-05) — Replaces Phase 14.2's tokenize-and-match `?context=auto` helper with cosine-similarity matching against pre-embedded catalog refs. Caches per-(space_id, catalog_version) snapshot of 231 ref embeddings in-process; refreshes automatically on catalog version bump. New `internal/api/context_fingerprint.go` (~190 LOC) + `Server.contextFPCache` field initialized iff `cfg.ContextFingerprintEnabled && emb != nil`. New env knob `CONTEXT_FINGERPRINT_QUERY_TOPK` (default 8, validated [1, 64]). 16q A vs B' (vector) verdict: same shape as Phase 14.2 (mean Δ=-0.006 within eps, 1 improvement, 13 unchanged, 0 regressions). Diagnosis: catalog tag selection (LLM-summary buckets like `api`, `architecture`) is the bottleneck, not the derivation method. **Default state unchanged** — `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_ENABLED=false`. Phase 14.2.2 (Builder tag-retune toward code-symbol n-grams + path segments) queued. Removed unused `tokenizeForFingerprint` helper (superseded). Post: [`phase_14_2_1_post.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_14_2_1_post.md). OpenAI spend: ~$0.50.

- **POST-FT-LORA-PHASE14.2: Note 05 sparse context fingerprints + adaptive ContextCatalog + 5th RRF column** (2026-05-04, Epics 0–5 landed; Epic 6 A/B + Epic 7 docs in progress) — Per-observation 256-bit sparse vectors that let retrieval discriminate the *same* MemoryNode in *different* contexts. Two-phase design (operator-approved 2026-05-05): observe-time fingerprint from local features (path + role_type×layer + tags); post-hoc refinement via CO_ACTIVATED_WITH walks at the macro-cycle stage 6 hook. Adaptive Builder allocates 256 bits proportionally to per-space density (Epic 0 forensic: every surveyed production space has 0 distinct symbols + 0 distinct roles, so the static 64/64/64/64 spec wasted 128 bits). Plan: `sprint_plan_phase_14_2_note_05_sparse_fingerprints.md`. Forensic: `phase_14_2_forensic.md`. Feature doc: `docs/features/context-fingerprinting.md`.
  - **Schema** — Neo4j V0025 (MemoryNode `context_fingerprint_active []uint16` + `_version int` + index on `_version`). Neo4j V0026 (`ContextCatalog` node label per (space_id, version) + `(space_id, is_active)` and `(space_id, version)` indexes). TSDB V0020 (`context_catalog_versions` hypertable). `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` 19 → 20.
  - **Adaptive Builder** (`internal/hidden/context_catalog_builder.go`, ~620 LOC) — neo4jBuilder collects per-space density via 4 Cypher queries (top paths, top tags, top role_type×layer tuples, distinct counts); allocateBits is pure deterministic (32 bits → top-N rtl; up to 32 bits → top-N tags; remainder → paths; symbols deferred); persistCatalog atomic in one tx. neo4jLoader implements LoadActive/LoadVersion/Freshness on the CatalogLoader interface.
  - **Stage 6 refresh hook** — `internal/ape/cycle.go::CycleOrchestrator.maybeRefreshContextCatalog` runs after Stage 5 Validate; gated on `cfg.ContextFingerprintRefreshEnabled` (default true); time-bounded by `ContextFingerprintRefreshTimeoutMs` (default 60s); cold-start + age-vs-interval check. Weekly cadence default (`CONTEXT_FINGERPRINT_REFRESH_INTERVAL_HOURS=168`).
  - **Observe-time computation** — `internal/conversation/fingerprint.go::ComputeContextFingerprintLocal` walks observation-local features only. `Service.Observe` loads active catalog, computes fingerprint, sets fields on Observation before createObservationNode Cypher. `RefineWithCoactivations` is the post-hoc helper for Stage 6 (no-op today since 0 production spaces have populated symbols; kept for forward compatibility).
  - **5th RRF column + strict mode** — `internal/retrieval/column_context.go::ContextColumn` ranks candidates by Jaccard similarity to query fingerprint; pure virtual column over upstream cands (no extra I/O). Strict-mode pre-aggregation filter drops candidates with Jaccard below `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_STRICT_THRESHOLD` (default 0.25). Cache namespace `v1-rrf4|...` → `v1-rrf5|...|c=W|...|ctx=B|strict=T`.
  - **Backfill CLI** — `mdemg migrate context-fingerprint --space-id <id> [--build] [--dry-run] [--batch-size 500]` for historical observations. Idempotent (skips obs already at active version).
  - **Request shape** — `RetrieveRequest.QueryContextFingerprint []uint16` + `StrictContextMode bool`; `?strict_context=true` URL fallback.
  - **12 new env knobs** — `CONTEXT_FINGERPRINT_*` (5), `CONTEXT_CATALOG_*` (4), `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_*` (3); Validate cross-field check enforces `TopNPaths + TopNTags + RoleTypeLayerBits ≤ BitBudget`.
  - **State after Epic 5**: 5 commits on `reh3376_dev01` (`011d7e6`, `6e3aad6`, `77b5b8d`, `d6dba0f`, `0aab999`); `RETRIEVAL_CONTEXT_COLUMN_ENABLED` defaults `false` until Epic 6 verdict + conditional flip.
