- **POST-FT-LORA-PHASE13.5: MLX Server Stability cutover** (2026-05-03, all epics) — production LLM substrate migrated from `mlx_lm.server` (port 8101, ~17 crashes/4h on M5 Max + macOS 26.3.x) to `llama-server` from llama.cpp b9000+ (port 8102, GGUF Q5_K_M, 0 crashes / 160 min × 301 calls). Decision was data-cited per operator constraint; no opinion-based picks. Plan: [`sprint_plan_phase_13_5_mlx_stability.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/sprint_plan_phase_13_5_mlx_stability.md). Bake-off results: [`phase_13_5_bakeoff_results.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_13_5_bakeoff_results.md). Synthesis: [`phase_13_5_mlx_research_synthesis.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_13_5_mlx_research_synthesis.md).
  - **Research-first sprint**: 4 parallel streams (crash forensics, mlx-lm/Apple Metal community evidence, alternatives matrix, internal LLM call profile) cited 35+ external sources. Synthesis disqualified mlx_lm.server (maintainer-stated "not for production"; unbounded KV cache → Metal OOM → SIGABRT every ~14 min), Ollama (8+ open M5/26.3.x issues, matmul2d static_assert), LM Studio (closed-source operability risk for cognitive-substrate framework).
  - **Empirical bake-off F1 (llama.cpp Q5_K_M GGUF) vs F2 (MLC-LLM q4f16_1 TVM)** — F1 wins every measured dimension: stability tied (both 0 crashes); latency 1.6× faster (3.10s avg vs 5.02s; p95 4.28 vs 6.56); UVTS quality at perfect mean parity (0.396 = 0.396) vs F2 −0.006; ~4× larger community + MIT licensed; GGUF format runs in many other tools.
  - **Production cutover artifacts**:
    - New launchd plist `~/Library/LaunchAgents/com.mdemg.llama-server.plist` (KeepAlive on crash, ThrottleInterval=30s)
    - GGUF model `~/mdemg/.local-models/mdemg-llm-v1-gguf/mdemg-llm-v1.Q5_K_M.gguf` (10 GB, mean parity with MLX form on UVTS)
    - `.env` `LLM_ENDPOINT=http://127.0.0.1:8102/v1` (was 8101); watchdog re-enabled (probe is backend-agnostic)
    - Old `com.mdemg.mlx-server.plist` renamed `.disabled-phase13_5` (kept for emergency rollback)
  - **Model conversion path** (~5 min on M5 Max): MLX 4-bit safetensors → `mlx_lm.fuse --dequantize` → 28 GB bf16 HF safetensors → `convert_hf_to_gguf.py --outtype f16` → 29.5 GB f16 GGUF → `llama-quantize Q5_K_M` → 10.5 GB Q5_K_M GGUF
  - **Code surface**: zero. The framework was already backend-agnostic by design (`cfg.EffectiveLLMEndpoint() + /models` probe works for any OpenAI-compat backend). Only configuration + plist + docs changed. Identifier renames (`MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_MLX` → `MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_LLM`, etc.) deferred to follow-up sprint Phase 13.6 with backward-compat aliases.
  - **CLAUDE.md**: "MLX Watchdog" section replaced with "Local LLM Runtime — llama.cpp llama-server" describing the new architecture, why migrated, rollback path, conversion pipeline, why the alternates lost.
  - **Phase 13.1 (column-weight ablation) now unblocked** on a stable substrate. Phase 13.6 (naming cleanup) queued.
  - **Operator-driven discipline**: research synthesis explicitly captured operator constraints "no short-term patches" + "data-decidable questions are not operator-input questions" as new MEMORY entries (`feedback_no_short_term_mlx_patches.md`, `feedback_data_decides_not_operator.md`).

### Added

- **POST-FT-LORA-PHASE13: Note 04 Column-Voting Retrieval** (2026-05-02, Epic 0-8) — RRF aggregator + 4 columns (Embedding + BM25 + Graph + Structural; Temporal + RoleScoped deferred per Epic 0 data audit) shipped as feature-flagged infrastructure. Plan: [`sprint_plan_phase_13_column_voting.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/sprint_plan_phase_13_column_voting.md). Post: [`phase_13_column_voting_post.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_13_column_voting_post.md). A/B verdict: [`phase_13_ab_verdict_quick.json`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_13_ab_verdict_quick.json).
  - **`internal/retrieval/columns.go`** — `Column` interface + `ColumnQuery` + `ColumnResult` (non-fatal-error contract: failed columns lower consensus_strength but don't abort the aggregate).
  - **`internal/retrieval/column_{embedding,bm25,graph,structural}.go`** — 3 refactor wrappers + 1 NEW Structural column (variable-length Cypher walk across `contains|defined_in*1..N` with exponential hop decay; default 2 hops via `RETRIEVAL_STRUCTURAL_HOPS`).
  - **`internal/retrieval/consensus.go`** — `Aggregate` function. Parallel column execution via errgroup + per-column timeout (default 80% of parent ctx remaining via `RETRIEVAL_COLUMN_TIMEOUT_FRACTION`). RRF formula `score(node) = Σ (weight_c / (k + rank_c(node)))` with `k = RETRIEVAL_RRF_K` (default 60). `consensus_strength` per node = `(cols_with_node / cols_queried) × avg(normalized_rank)`, clipped to [0,1]. `AggregateConsensus` is the per-call mean — the headline signal Phase 14 + DH-005 retrieval-confidence dimension consume.
  - **`internal/retrieval/scoring_rrf.go`** — `Service.ScoreAndRankRRF`: 3 virtualColumns wrap the upstream-fused candidate set sorted by VectorSim/BM25Score/activation; Structural runs as a separate true-parallel column. `service.Retrieve` forks on `cfg.RetrievalColumnVotingEnabled` (default false) at the scorer call site. Fail-open to legacy `ScoreAndRank` on RRF error.
  - **Cache scorer-version namespace** (`cache.go::CacheKey(req, scorerVersion)`) — `Service.scorerVersion()` returns `"v0-linear"` or `"v1-rrf4"`. Critical for A/B correctness: flag flip = different namespace = no cross-contamination. Verified live via `scorer_version=v0-linear` ↔ `v1-rrf4` log lines on flag flip.
  - **TSDB V0017 `retrieval_audit` hypertable** — `audit_id CUIDv2 PK, recorded_at, space_id, query_text_hash, scorer_version, consensus_strength, per_column_latency JSONB, columns_queried/returned, top_k_node_ids[], total_latency_ms`. Hypertable on `recorded_at`, 7-day chunks, 3 indexes. `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION=17` default. `RetrievalAuditWriter` interface + `Service.SetRetrievalAuditWriter` setter; default off via `RETRIEVAL_AUDIT_ENABLED=false`.
  - **3 new Prometheus metrics**: `mdemg_retrieval_consensus_strength` (histogram), `mdemg_retrieval_column_latency_seconds{column}` (histogram per-column), `mdemg_retrieval_column_failed_total{column,reason}` (counter).
  - **15 new config knobs**: `RETRIEVAL_COLUMN_VOTING_ENABLED`, `RETRIEVAL_RRF_K`, `RETRIEVAL_COLUMN_TIMEOUT_FRACTION`, `RETRIEVAL_STRUCTURAL_HOPS`, `RETRIEVAL_COLUMN_{EMBEDDING,BM25,GRAPH,STRUCTURAL}_ENABLED`, `RETRIEVAL_RERANK_CONSUME_CONSENSUS`, `DH005_CONSUME_CONSENSUS`, `RETRIEVAL_AUDIT_ENABLED`. All default safe-off.
  - **A/B merge-gate verdict: FAIL.** Quick profile (16 questions × `whk-wms` space): baseline `phase13-baseline-linear` mean **0.396** vs candidate `phase13-candidate-rrf4` mean **0.358** = **−0.038** delta with **2 catastrophic per-question regressions to 0.000** (q `69` architecture_structure, q `hard_sym_4` computed_value). 13 of 16 questions produced bit-identical scores between linear and RRF — scorers converge on most queries; divergence concentrates on hard-symbol queries. **`RetrievalColumnVotingEnabled` default stays `false`** in the source (no conditional flip in this commit). Phase 13.1 (column-weight ablation) is the next sprint.
  - **Schema**: 16 → **17**. **Tests**: full `go test -race ./...` green; new tests in `internal/retrieval/{column,consensus,cache_scorer_version,scoring_rrf}_test.go`, `internal/config/config_phase13_test.go`. **Lint**: `golangci-lint run` clean. **Costs**: ~$1.50 OpenAI (16q × 2 runs × ~$0.04/q grader); ~25 min wall-clock for the A/B + 4 launchd kickstart cycles.

### Changed

- **HOTFIX 11.6.3.1: MLX always-on policy** (2026-05-02, commit `fc0961e`) — Per operator policy "MLX server should NEVER be down when mdemg framework is running" (memory: `feedback_mlx_required_when_mdemg_running.md`). The framework's 16 LLM call sites all depend on mlx; silent degradation when mlx is down is loss of function, not graceful failure. Three changes:
  1. **`MLX_WATCHDOG_ENABLED` default `false → true`** (`internal/config/config.go::FromEnv`). The Phase 11.6.3 soak gate is supplanted by this policy — the always-on rule IS the live-validation gate.
  2. **`launchdServices` `com.mdemg.mlx-server` `Optional: true → false`** (`internal/cli/service_darwin.go`). `mdemg service install` now FAILS LOUDLY with actionable guidance when `mlx_lm.server` cannot be located via `MDEMG_MLX_LM_BIN` env or PATH. Operators on hosts without mlx must explicitly bypass with `MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_MLX=1`.
  3. **New startup precondition** (`internal/cli/preflight_mlx.go`). `mdemg start/serve` probes `<LLM_ENDPOINT>/models` with a 3s timeout post-config-load; refuses to start if mlx unreachable. Error message includes resolved endpoint, underlying failure, and 3 ways to resolve. Bypass: `MDEMG_ALLOW_NO_MLX=1`.
  - **`CLAUDE.md`** MLX Watchdog subsection rewritten to reflect mandatory always-on contract.
  - **Live-verified**: `MDEMG_MLX_LM_BIN=/Users/reh3376/.venv/mdemg-ft-lora/bin/mlx_lm.server mdemg service install` → mlx auto-started via launchd, HTTP 200 on `/v1/models` within 8s, conservative Phase 12 flags (`--prompt-cache-size 256 --prompt-concurrency 2 --decode-concurrency 2`).
  - Tests: `internal/config/config_mlx_watchdog_test.go::TestMLXWatchdog_Defaults` updated to assert default-on contract; `go test -race ./...` green; `golangci-lint run` clean.

### Added

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11.6.3: MLX Watchdog (Operational Hygiene #2)** (2026-04-30) — Auto-restart + fast-fail + degraded-mode protection against the Metal-OOM cadence and retry-storm pattern uncovered in Phase 12. With the watchdog in place, transient `mlx_lm.server` death is survivable: detected within ~10s, mdemg short-circuits the 6-attempt × ~30s retry loop, launchd restarts mlx after 60s, operator is alerted but does not have to act. Plan: [`sprint_plan_phase_11_6_3.md`](docs/development/ft-lora/sprint_plan_phase_11_6_3.md). Post: [`phase_11_6_3_post.md`](docs/development/ft-lora/phase_11_6_3_post.md).
  - **`internal/mlxprobe` package** (~250 LOC) — goroutine polling `<endpoint>/v1/models` every 5s with a 2s timeout. State machine: `up → down` after 3 consecutive failures (or connection-refused), `down → up` after 2 consecutive successes (hysteresis). `up → degraded` after 3 slow successes (informational only — does NOT trigger fast-fail). Atomic state, supervisor-managed lifecycle, panic-recovered. Singleton wiring (`SetDefault`/`Default`) plus toggle (`SetFastFailEnabled`) + observer (`SetFastFailObserver`) so llmclient can read state without importing metrics.
  - **llmclient fast-fail gate** (`internal/llmclient/client.go:471`, `errors.go` new) — 10-LOC gate at top of `doWithRetry`: if `mlxprobe.FastFailEnabled() && Default().Endpoint() == c.baseURL && Default().State() == StateDown`, returns new `ErrMLXDown` sentinel without entering the retry math. Embeddings paths unaffected — gate keys on exact baseURL match (OpenAI endpoint is `https://api.openai.com/v1`, mlx is `http://127.0.0.1:8101/v1`).
  - **launchd plist + service install hook** — new `packaging/launchd/com.mdemg.mlx-server.plist` (and embed mirror) with `KeepAlive.SuccessfulExit=false` + `ThrottleInterval=60s` + the conservative Phase 12 mlx flags (`--prompt-cache-size 256 --prompt-concurrency 2 --decode-concurrency 2`). `launchdServices` slice extended with `Optional: true` entry — `mdemg service install` skips when `mlx_lm.server` isn't on PATH (`MDEMG_MLX_LM_BIN`/`MDEMG_MODEL_PATH` env overrides). Substitutes `__MLX_LM_BIN__` + `__MDEMG_MODEL_PATH__` at install time.
  - **`mdemg watchdog status` CLI** — new `internal/cli/watchdog.go` registered under the Advanced group. Reads probe state by parsing the running mdemg's `/metrics` exposition (line-oriented Prometheus parser), `launchctl print` for restart count + state, and `~/.mdemg/alerts/current.json` filtered to mlx-server entries (last 5). `--json` flag for machine-readable output (validates with `jq`).
  - **3 Prometheus metrics** — `mdemg_mlx_health_state{endpoint}` (gauge 0/1/2), `mdemg_mlx_fast_fail_total{caller_task}` (counter), `mdemg_mlx_state_transitions_total{from,to}` (counter).
  - **Alert wiring** — `internal/cli/serve.go` early-writer block constructs the prober, registers it with the supervisor (`mlx-watchdog`), and binds an `OnTransition` callback that fires `up→down`=High and `down→up`=Low alerts via the existing dispatcher (300s cooldown handles flap suppression during 60s launchd restart cycles). Late-bound `srv.AlertDispatcher()` lookup avoids initialization-order hazards.
  - **4 config knobs** — `MLX_WATCHDOG_ENABLED` (default `false` — safe-off rollout), `MLX_PROBE_INTERVAL_SEC` (default 5, min 1), `MLX_PROBE_TIMEOUT_SEC` (default 2, min 1, must be `<` interval), `MLX_FAIL_FAST_ENABLED` (default `true` — only effective when watchdog enabled). Cross-field validation enforces `timeout < interval`.
  - **Decision-fork outcomes** disclosed in PR: launchd-only over Go-binary supervisor; 5s/2s probe cadence; fast-fail only on `down` (degraded is log-only); `MLX_WATCHDOG_ENABLED=false` default until Live Smoke 2 passes; plist `Optional: true` so non-Apple-Silicon hosts skip.
  - **Schema**: unchanged at 16 (additive metrics + alerts only; no TSDB migration). **Tests**: full `go test -race ./...` green; new tests in `internal/mlxprobe/probe_test.go`, `internal/llmclient/client_fail_fast_test.go`, `internal/config/config_mlx_watchdog_test.go`, `internal/cli/{watchdog,service_darwin}_test.go`, `tests/integration/mlx_watchdog_test.go`. **Lint**: `golangci-lint` clean across all touched packages. **Costs**: $0 OpenAI; ~3 hr local compute (deferred to operator-led Live Smoke 2 8h soak).
  - **Phase 13 unblocked**: sustained live A/B testing for Note 04 Column-Voting Retrieval no longer risks 30-minute storms + manual recovery on every mlx crash.

- **POST-FT-LORA-PHASE12: UVTS Activation** (2026-05-01) — Sprint 2 of the post-FT-LORA roadmap. Promotes UVTS (Universal Validation Test Specification) from spec-only-with-stub-runner to active validation framework. The pre-existing 677-line `uvts_runner.py` was characterized as "stub-only for question generation" but live testing surfaced **5 latent defects** that prevented any end-to-end run from completing — fixed in commit `0a99f29`. Plan: [`sprint_plan_phase_12_uvts.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/sprint_plan_phase_12_uvts.md). Post: [`phase_12_uvts_post.md`](docs/development/post-ft-lora/phase_12_uvts_post.md). Shipped across 5 incremental commits (`0a99f29`, `4b27717`, `d6601b8`, `d10c1a5`, sprint-close).
  - **UVTS runner activation** — wired `grader_v4` + per-question scoring + threshold gate + canonical UxTS report. New CLI flags `--retrieve-timeout-s` (default 30s; 10s hardcoded was the culprit on slow dev pipelines), `--space-id` (override spec's `validation.space_id` for cross-space A/B), `--persist-tsdb` + `--branch-label` + `--codebase-sha` (V0016 row writes via psycopg).
  - **TSDB V0016 migration** — `uvts_runs` (run-level metadata + threshold_json + ab_baseline/candidate_run_id self-FKs for A/B linkage) + `uvts_results` (hypertable on `recorded_at`, 7-day chunks, `raw_grade` JSONB preserves grader output verbatim). `TSDB_REQUIRED_SCHEMA_VERSION` 15 → 16. Live-verified: 4 runs landed across 4 distinct branch_labels in this sprint's smokes.
  - **A/B compare harness** — new `docs/tests/uvts/runners/uvts_ab_compare.py` with the merge-gate criterion "B mean ≥ A mean AND no per-question regression > threshold". CI exit codes 0/1/2 (pass/fail/error). Question-set drift detection. Optional `--persist-tsdb` writes A/B verdict row with FK links to source runs. 3-case fixture smoke (pass/fail/drift) + 1 live A/B against real runs verified.
  - **Spec authoring** — `lnl_demo_validation.uvts.json` extended with `ab_mode` block (`enabled=false`, `regression_threshold_per_question=0.10` per Note 02 merge bar). New `polysemy_resolution.uvts.json` (8 polysemy-stratified categories, `partial_authoring=true` flag — full 40-question authoring deferred to Note 05 tee-up sprint).

Existing `uxts-canonical-specs.yml` workflow already covers `docs/tests/uvts/**` via `docs/tests/**` path trigger.
  - **ConflictTracker production wiring (Workstream C #1)** — Phase 11.6.x deferred this; folded in here. New `CONFLICT_TRACKER_ENABLED` config knob (default true). `*ConflictTracker` constructed in `internal/api/server.go::SetTSDBClient` and injected via setter into 3 Services. Three hook sites with conservative trigger conditions: `internal/ape/cycle.go::recordReflectDivergence` (opposing-pair RSIC actions), `internal/consulting/service.go::Suggest` (when `detectConflicts > 0`), `internal/jiminy/service.go::Guide` (low-confidence guidance emission). All async + rate-limited by the existing 1 row/space/minute limiter. 9 unit tests for ape's hook detection logic.
  - **Cross-cutting policy update** — Live system testing formalized as Tier-3 requirement in `CLAUDE.md` (commit `d10c1a5`). CMS observation `p5iv8effstxk5ujd1fa2qfy8`. Evidence basis: across Phase 11.6.x, 11.6.2, and 12.0–12.6, every major defect was caught in live smoke runs while unit/integration tests passed.
  - **Mid-sprint findings filed for follow-ups**: (a) MLX server fragility — Metal command-buffer OOM recurring every 30-60min under sustained load (Phase 11.6.3 candidate — full watchdog sprint); (b) retry-storm pattern when mlx is unreachable (1642% CPU, load 30+) — needs `connection refused` fast-fail in llmclient + degraded-mode policy.
  - **Schema**: 15 → **16**. **Tests**: full `go test ./...` green; +9 new in `internal/ape/conflict_tracker_hook_test.go`. **Costs**: $0 OpenAI (grader heuristic path).

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11.6.x: Operational hygiene bundle** (2026-05-01) — Five follow-ups closed in one sprint, forced by a Metal-OOM event observed mid-sprint on the production mlx (PID 11140) — exactly the failure mode Epic 1 prevents. Plan: [`sprint_plan_phase_11_6_x_hygiene.md`](docs/development/ft-lora/sprint_plan_phase_11_6_x_hygiene.md). Post: [`phase_11_6_x_post.md`](docs/development/ft-lora/phase_11_6_x_post.md).

New config knob `RSIC_LLM_CONCURRENCY_LIMIT` (default 2, min 1, max 8); new metric `mdemg_rsic_llm_semaphore_blocked_total`; ctx-cancellation path returns clean error. Five-test stress suite including the 8-goroutine sprint-plan stress test.

Both call sites flipped. New rows post-restart confirmed correctly tagged.

Pre+post DO blocks audit row counts and abort on any unrouted hash. **447 rows relabeled** on dev TSDB (109 + 248 + 90).

Subsystem callback wiring deferred to next sprint — recorder is ready for the 3-month observation window that empirically justifies Note 09 (FEP capstone).

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11.6: Production cutover — all 16 MDEMG LLM call sites now route at local `mdemg-llm-v1`** (2026-04-30) — Renamed Phase 5 dense to `mdemg-llm-v1` (stable production ID, symlink `.local-models/mdemg-llm-v1/` → `qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1/`). All 16 MDEMG LLM call sites switched from cloud gpt-5.4-mini to local model served by `mlx_lm.server` on host:8101. Goal of the entire FT-LORA project, achieved.
  - **Code patches (`internal/api/server.go`)**: 3 pre-existing config-wiring bugs fixed. `consulting.classify` (line 490), `jiminy.synthesize` (line 530), `ape.reflect` (line 769) were calling `cfg.OpenAIEndpoint` directly instead of `cfg.EffectiveLLMEndpoint()`. Bug masked when `LLM_ENDPOINT` was unset (fallback returns OpenAI), surfaced when the cutover routed those tasks to the wrong endpoint. Patched all 3.
  - **Compose template + live `docker-compose.yml`**: defaults moved `LLM_MODEL=gpt-5.4-mini` → `mdemg-llm-v1`; new `LLM_ENDPOINT` env passthrough (default `http://host.docker.internal:8101/v1` so containers reach host's mlx_lm.server); `RERANK_MODEL` cutover; `RERANK_TIMEOUT_MS` bumped 10000 → 60000.
  - **`.env` cutover** (gitignored, on operator machines): `LLM_MODEL`, `RERANK_MODEL`, `LLM_SUMMARY_MODEL`, `INTENT_MODEL`, `EMERGENCE_MODEL`, `GUARDRAIL_MODEL`, `JIMINY_SYNTHESIS_MODEL` all → `mdemg-llm-v1`. `RERANK_TOP_N` 50 → 20. Per-task timeouts bumped: `INTENT_TIMEOUT_MS=15000`, `RERANK_TIMEOUT_MS=120000`, `CONSULTING_CLASSIFY_TIMEOUT_MS=60000`, `JIMINY_SYNTHESIS_TIMEOUT_MS=180000`, `RSIC_LLM_REFLECT_TIMEOUT_MS=180000`, `EMERGENCE_TIMEOUT_MS=120000`. New `MDEMG_LLM_NAME=mdemg-llm-v1` and `MDEMG_LLM_PATH` for operational reference.
  - **Manifest at canonical path** (`.local-models/qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1/manifest.json`, gitignored): `production_id: mdemg-llm-v1`, full lineage (Phase 5 SFT → Phase 11 GRPO parked → Phase 11.5d distill rolled back), SHA256s of all weight files, augmented-eval aggregate (0.8389), production-use commands.
  - **Smoke-test verification**: 5 of 16 task surfaces fired with `model_name=mdemg-llm-v1` during the test window — `retrieval.query_classify` (9/9 OK, 453ms-4.2s), `retrieval.intent_translate` (14/16 OK, 602ms-15s), `retrieval.rerank_cross` (5/8 OK, 2.7s-60s), `ape.reflect` (2/13 OK, ~170s/call), `consulting.classify` (1 pre-patch call). The remaining 11 surfaces (consulting.synthesis, guardrail.evaluate, hidden.*, jiminy.codegen/evaluate*/synthesize, metalearn.generalize, summarize.generate) didn't fire during the smoke window but use identical infrastructure and will route correctly when they fire. `retrieval.rerank_nli` is deprecated (Ollama-only, dead in OpenAI production per 11.5e).
  - **Two architectural constraints surfaced**: (1) RSIC concurrent fan-out (5+ `ape.reflect` calls within 200ms) crashed `mlx_lm.server` with `[METAL] Insufficient Memory (kIOGPUCommandBufferCallbackErrorOutOfMemory)`. Workaround: `--prompt-concurrency 1 --decode-concurrency 1` flags serialize requests. Long-term fix needed: rate-limit RSIC scheduler in Go. (2) Local LLM is 10-50× slower than cloud (`ape.reflect` ~170s vs gpt-mini ~3-10s). Per-task timeouts needed bumping. Retrieval end-to-end now 5-29s (was sub-second on cloud).
  - **Container redeploy gated**: the running Docker mdemg container uses `image: ghcr.io/reh3376/mdemg:latest` from a pre-patch build. After this commit pushes + CI publishes a new GHCR image, operations should: `docker compose pull mdemg && docker compose up -d`. The native binary `bin/mdemg` carries the patches and was used for the smoke test.
  - **Costs**: $0 OpenAI. ~3 hr compute. 167 unit tests still green.
  - **Open follow-ups filed**: (a) RSIC concurrent fan-out rate-limit; (b) `--prompt-concurrency 1` should be canonical mlx_lm.server invocation; (c) Grafana panel for LLM latency by `model_name`; (d) prompt-cache investigation to amortize repeat-prefix calls.
  - **Docs**: `docs/development/ft-lora/phase_11_6_post.md`; `00_README_v2.md` v5.11 → v5.12; this CHANGELOG entry; `AGENT_HANDOFF.md` top entry; `CLAUDE.md` Testing section update.

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11.5e: Eval coverage augmentation + Phase 5 reinstated as production canonical** (2026-04-30) — Augmented `valid_clean.jsonl` from 180 rows × 9 tasks → 319 rows × 16 tasks via stale-hash rescue (40 rows for jiminy.evaluate/_llm via content-routing) + synthetic gpt-mini generation (99 rows across guardrail.evaluate, hidden.summarize, consulting.synthesis, metalearn.generalize, summarize.generate). 1 task deprecated (retrieval.rerank_nli, Ollama-only/dead in OpenAI production). Manifest v2.0 with per-task source breakdown; 0/319 leakage with all 9 train/valid sources.

The 11.5d Stage-1 promotion was based on a 9-task-subset; adding 7 more tasks of coverage flipped the verdict. Stage-1 archived to `.local-models/qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1-distill-stage1/` (was `-rl/`); **Phase 5 base reinstated as production canonical**: `mlx_lm.server --model .local-models/qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1 --host 127.0.0.1 --port 8101` (no `--adapter-path`).
  - **Production bug discovered: jiminy.evaluate ↔ jiminy.evaluate_llm task_name labels are SWAPPED** at production `WithContext()` call sites. TSDB rows tagged `jiminy.evaluate` contain `jiminy.evaluate_llm`'s production prompt (and vice versa); affects all rows logged through 2026-04-29. Workaround in this sprint: content-routing extractor (`scripts/x11_jiminy_evaluate_rescue.py`) matches by SHA256(system_prompt) against spec-known hashes. **Filed as production follow-up.**
  - **Phase 11 RL arc + 11.5d distillation: net-negative on broader eval.** Run 7 -0.82pp behind Phase 5; Stage-1 -0.95pp. The +0.26pp Stage-1 win in 11.5d full-sweep was eval-coverage-limited. Both archived; future RL/distillation requires fundamentally different design.
  - **Phase 5 wins 4 of 9 TSDB tasks**: `retrieval.intent_translate` (+21pp over gpt-mini), `retrieval.query_classify`, `hidden.reclassify`, `retrieval.rerank_cross` marginal. **Phase 5 specialty fine-tuning beats cloud teacher on narrow production-grounded tasks.**
  - **gpt-mini wins synthetic-eval tasks** (`guardrail.evaluate` +20pp, `consulting.classify` +13pp). Synthetic eval reflects gpt-mini self-evaluation bias — gpt-mini scores high on prompts it generated. Production-grounded TSDB rows (180 of 319) are the truer signal.
  - **`consulting.classify` distillation backfired**: Stage-1 went 0.712 (P5) → 0.660 (-5.2pp on this task). 36 distill pairs were 89% must/must_not but eval is 80% expected `none`. Class-distribution mismatch over-trained classification confidence. Filed as 11.5f candidate.
  - **New tooling**: `scripts/x10_synth_prompt_capture.py` (reusable: meta-synthesis at temp=0.9 → response capture → reward filter); `scripts/x11_jiminy_evaluate_rescue.py` (content-routing extractor for stale-hash rescue).
  - **Costs**: synthesis ~$0.60 + gpt-mini augmented re-baseline ~$3.20 = **~$3.80 OpenAI**. ~3.5 hr local MLX (3 sequential model swaps).
  - **Strategic decisions**: (1) Phase 5 base is production canonical; (2) Phase 11 RL parked; (3) Phase 11.5d distill rolled back; (4) eval coverage is highest-leverage improvement when training plateaus — flipped a production decision without retraining; (5) synthetic eval is informative but biased toward gpt-mini self-evaluation; weight TSDB rows accordingly.
  - **Open follow-ups filed**: jiminy task_name swap (production bug); consulting.classify distill class-balance redesign; query_classify 4-row eval-label inconsistency from 11.5d.
  - **Docs**: `docs/development/ft-lora/{sprint_plan_phase_11_5e.md, phase_11_5e_post.md}`; `training_data/eval/clean_v2_comparison.md`; `00_README_v2.md` v5.10 → v5.11; this CHANGELOG entry; `AGENT_HANDOFF.md` top entry; `CLAUDE.md` Testing section.

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11.5d: Branch B Revised — distill adapter shipped at gpt-mini parity** (2026-04-29) — Stage-1 distill adapter promoted to canonical `.local-models/qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1-rl/` (Run 7 archived to `-rl-run7`). Full-sweep aggregate **0.8578** (+0.26pp over Phase 5 dense, -0.09pp from gpt-5.4-mini ceiling — at parity, within noise). Real per-task wins on connection-layer tasks: `consulting.classify` +2.0pp (0.668 → 0.688; drives Jiminy guidance fidelity), `hidden.reclassify` +5.0pp (0.925 → 0.975; drives concept clustering). Adapter SHA256 `71821ee4cc7a6d74…`. Manifest at `qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1-rl/manifest.json` documents full lineage (Phase 5 SFT tier1 → Phase 11 Run 7 GRPO → Phase 11.5d gpt-5.4-mini SFT distillation).
  - **Benchmark row-sweep fix (highest-leverage outcome of the sprint).** `neural/benchmarks/run_benchmark.py` previously evaluated only `rows[0]` per spec (`# MVP — no row-level sweep`), reducing 20-row tasks to single-prompt-per-spec benchmarks. **Patched to iterate ALL matched rows by default**; added `RunnerOptions.rows_per_spec` field + `--rows-per-spec` CLI flag (default 0 = all rows). The fix retroactively reveals that the entire Phase 11 → 11.5 plateau narrative was optimizing against a phantom: Phase 5 honest aggregate is **0.8553** (not 0.8052 as 11.5c reported), and gpt-mini's **0.8587** is essentially tied (+0.34pp gap, within noise). Tests: 109 RL/DPO + 13 benchmark unit tests still green after patch.
  - **Phase 11 GRPO arc (Runs 1-7) net-zero on real data.** Run 7 full-sweep aggregate **0.8531** (-0.22pp behind Phase 5 alone). The previously claimed "+1.83pp Run 7 over Phase 5" gain was the same single-prompt artifact. Run 7 produced 2 task-wins (`hidden.reclassify` +2.5pp, `ape.reflect` +1.6pp) but 3 task-losses (`retrieval.intent_translate` -5.3pp, `retrieval.query_classify` -5.0pp, `jiminy.codegen` -1.1pp). Net negative; RL is parked.
  - **Phase 5 BEATS gpt-mini on 4 of 9 measured tasks** on full-sweep: `retrieval.intent_translate` +12.4pp, `retrieval.query_classify` +2.5pp, `retrieval.rerank_cross` +0.6pp, `jiminy.synthesize` +0.7pp. Cloud teacher not strictly superior on production traffic.
  - **Distillation pipeline shipped as reusable scripts.** `scripts/x9_distill_capture_v2.py` (TSDB extraction with system_prompt extraction from production Go code, leak filter against 10 sources, reward filter ≥0.8, mlx_lm.lora chat-format JSONL output, 90/10 split, manifest with SHA256s + production system prompt SHAs). `configs/sft_phase11_5d_distill.yaml` (lr=1e-5, epochs=2, batch=4, 7 LoRA modules, r=32, max_seq_length=8192 — bumped from initial 4096 after truncation warnings on long retrieval.rerank_cross prompts). Distill set: 100 train + 12 valid pairs (consulting.classify 36 + retrieval.rerank_cross 76 from 160 attempted; reward filter retained 70%). 0% leakage with valid_clean.jsonl + 9 train/valid sources per leak audit gate.
  - **SFT execution.** `mlx_lm.lora` proven path (sidesteps Phase 11 custom-trainer gradient-checkpoint closure-capture and `set_wired_limit` footguns documented in MEMORY). val_loss 0.685 → 0.417 (-39.1%) monotonic descent through all 50 iters. Peak memory 84.7 GB (8192 max_seq vs 79 GB at 4096). ~109 min wall-clock. Trainable params 128.451M (0.870% of 14.768B base).
  - **Costs.** Capture ~$1, full-sweep re-baseline gpt-mini ~$0.40, Epic 0 audit ~$0.10. Total **~$1.50 OpenAI**. Compute: ~3.5 hr local MLX (SFT + 4 full-sweep re-baselines).

Synthetic prompt augmentation deferred to Phase 11.5e or follow-up.

- **FT-LORA-PHASE11 follow-up: MLX adapter** (2026-04-24) — Makes the Phase 11 GRPO compute pass runnable. New module `neural/training/rl/mlx_adapter.py` (~330 LOC) implements `MLXGRPOAdapter` — a stateful class providing real MLX wiring for all four trainer Protocol callables against `mlx_lm==0.31.2`. `rollout_fn` generates via `mlx_lm.generate`, tokenizes, computes sum-of-token logprobs under current policy + frozen reference, stashes `_StashedSample` tensors for backprop. `optimizer_step_fn` rebuilds GRPO loss inside `mx.value_and_grad` using stashed tensors + advantages, runs `optimizer.update`, forces `mx.eval`; cross-checks MLX-recomputed loss against numpy scalar (warns on > 1e-2 delta). `eval_fn` wraps the Phase 10 benchmark runner as a subprocess; `checkpoint_fn` saves LoRA trainable params as safetensors. `sum_response_logprobs` exported as pure function for testing. Trainer Protocol `OptimizerStepFn` extended to pass `advantages` + `keep_mask` + `rollouts` as kwargs (required for MLX graph rebuild); 5 existing test lambdas updated to `lambda v, **_: ...`. 16 new unit tests with full MLX stub infrastructure (numpy-backed `mlx.core` / `mlx.nn` / `mlx.optimizers` / `mlx.utils` / `mlx_lm` installed into `sys.modules` — no Metal dependency, CI-green). Suite: 73 → 89 tests. Doc: `docs/development/ft-lora/phase_11_mlx_adapter.md`. Operator now wires `prompt_provider` + `reward_fn` + `eval_cmd` at sprint-invocation time and starts the compute pass.

Shared helper `_extract_violation_ids()` enforces the ULTS spec's nested shape. Registry 18 → 20. 13 new unit tests (72 total, all green). - **FT-LORA-PHASE10: Automated Benchmark Framework — EXECUTED, first authoritative baseline captured, Phase 11 GRPO unblocked** (2026-04-23 → 2026-04-24) — Supersedes the ad-hoc `docs/benchmarks/run_benchmark_v4.py` + `test_questions_120.json` pair. `.local-models/qwen3-14b-mdemg-v1/` scored **aggregate weighted 0.8338** across **16 of 17 ULTS specs × 5 runs = 80 rows**, all `finish_reason=stop` (zero truncations). Per-group means: **T=0.8404** (7 tasks, weight 0.50) / **C=0.8222** (6 tasks, weight 0.35) / **J=0.8389** (3 tasks, weight 0.15). Baseline artifact: `training_data/eval/benchmark_qwen3_14b_v1_baseline.json` (run_id `q283a23bz59mrg6faxo32ydx2`, config SHA `3716f9a4…`, golden SHA `8e44cdf9…`, file SHA `789459f1…`). **Phase 11 GRPO now unblocked** — consumes `benchmark_results.reward_vector` + `stddev` per task as advantage-normalization denominator.
  - **New module tree — `neural/benchmarks/`**: `run_benchmark.py` (runner + group-weighted aggregator + 3-row stagnation detection), `llm_judge.py` (`gpt-5.4-mini` at `temp=0`/`seed=run_idx`/`max_tokens=4000`/`latency_budget_ms=30000`, own-fixed sampling — never inherits task recipe so J-group `presence_penalty=1.5` can't leak into judge reasoning), `sampling_policy.py` (T/C/J group-aware kwargs; drops J-group `top_k=-1` sentinel that MLX rejects), `variance.py` (mean/stddev/min/max per task for GRPO), `preflight.py` (17-spec field + think-mode-aware floor enforcement), `judge_prompts/{coherence,depth,relevance,naturalness}.txt` (4 SHA-pinned templates).
  - **Config — `configs/benchmark_phase10.yaml`**: single source of truth for N_runs (5), stagnation thresholds (`aggregate_delta=0.005`, `per_task_regression=0.02`), sampling-group weights (T:0.50 / C:0.35 / J:0.15), judge kwargs, `performance_floors` (default `max_tokens≥3000`/`latency_budget_ms≥15000`; think_mode `≥10000`/`≥60000`). All CLI-overridable — zero hardcoded constants.
  - **Two silent scorer bugs fixed** in `neural/training/reward_functions.py`, each worth ~2-4% aggregate: (1) `classification_accuracy` treated `expected` as a bare label when the runner passes full assistant JSON — would score valid responses as 0 silently; now shape-detects and compares normalized JSON for equality, list/string keys handled (e.g. `retrieval.query_classify`'s `{"types":["symbol_lookup"],...}`); (2) `evaluation_accuracy` accepted only `expected_verdict` when the runner passes `expected=<json>` — would score every response 1.0 silently; now normalizes both paths. First baseline aggregated 0.7990 under the buggy scorers; post-fix 0.8338 (+0.0348, +4.4% relative). Regression tests added.
  - **`evaluate_ft.py` Epic-4 shadow refactor**: added `--scorer={heuristic,registry,dual}` flag. Default stays `heuristic` (bit-identical Phase 5 regression path). `dual` runs both in parallel with per-task `|delta|<1%` assertion; replayed Phase 5 dev set and confirmed parity. Flip to `registry` default deferred to Phase 11 after ≥3 benchmark rounds confirm stability.
  - **Think-mode performance floors enforced — 8 specs bumped** to `max_tokens≥10000` + `latency_budget_ms≥60000`: `consulting_synthesis`, `hidden_summarize`, `jiminy_synthesize`, `metalearn_generalize`, `summarize_generate` (T-group), plus `jiminy_evaluate`, `jiminy_evaluate_llm`, `retrieval_rerank_cross` (think-mode chain-of-thought). Preflight fails any spec that under-budgets its class (silent truncation → silent zero-credit was the failure mode).
  - **`hidden.reclassify` spec patched**: `reward_functions` trimmed from `["json_valid", "classification_accuracy"]` → `["json_valid"]` and notes annotated — output is an array of cluster objects, not a single classification; the accuracy reward was structurally inapplicable and silently produced 0.0. Cluster-overlap reward tracked for Phase 10.5 UBENCH (#215).

  - **Durable MEMORY guardrails added** (both forcing functions from Epic 6 incidents this sprint):
    - `feedback_min_max_tokens_3000.md` — never set `max_tokens < 3000` on any LLM call. Truncation is catastrophic: the partial response is discarded after the cost is already spent. Initial `summarize.generate` run truncated at `max_tokens=1500` (think_mode=True + long JSON schemas compete for token budget); bumped to 3000 and re-ran 200/200 clean (100% valid-rate).
    - `feedback_min_latency_budget_15000.md` — never set `latency_budget_ms < 15000` on any LLM call. On 2026-04-22 observed Qwen3.6 think_mode elapsed_ms up to 11,506ms on valid responses; a 10000ms budget would have dropped them as spurious timeouts. OpenAI gpt-5.4-mini responses sit at ~1-2s so 15s is a wide margin for both pathways. For think-mode with complex JSON schemas, 20000-30000ms is prudent.
    - 16 ULTS specs at `docs/tests/ults/specs/*.ults.json` and code-fallback defaults in `neural/training/*.py` audited + bumped to the new floors.

Sprint C now fully green across all three gates; FT-LORA line unblocked through Sprint D; Phase 5 SFT pre-Gate-3 blocker cleared. **Throughput sub-gate:** median **126.65 tok/s** over 5×500-token measurements at canonical T recipe (`temp=0.6, top_p=0.95, top_k=20`) after a discarded 200-token warm-up — 2.1× the ≥60 tok/s floor; per-run spread 122.75 / 126.65 / 128.91 / 124.38 / 126.74 (mean 125.85; all `finish_reason=length` at exactly 500 tokens). **Quality sub-gate: `clear_pass` band — gap = −13.83%** (Qwen3.6 **ahead** of gpt-5.4-mini on the 120-question whk-wms benchmark). Qwen mean score **0.0672** (std 0.0279) vs gpt-5.4-mini mean **0.0591** (std 0.0247). Per-question: **Qwen 78 wins / gpt 42 wins / 0 ties**. Per-category (all 8 Qwen-ahead): architecture_structure −13.05%, business_logic_constraints −13.01%, computed_value −17.59%, cross_cutting_concerns −23.76%, data_flow_integration −15.67%, disambiguation −1.31%, relationship −4.34%, service_relationships −13.50%. **Path A deviation — runbook-sanctioned drift-catch.** Pre-flight `--help` on `run_benchmark_v4.py` surfaced the architectural mismatch the runbook anticipated: `--model` accepts only `sonnet|opus|haiku` and synthesis is via `subprocess.run(["claude", "--model", ..., "--print", ...])` at `answer_generator.py:311` — no `LLM_BASE_URL` / `LLM_MODEL` env hook. Path A bypasses `run_benchmark_v4.py` entirely via a ~220-line direct driver that POSTs to mlx_lm.server :8200 (Qwen) and `api.openai.com/v1/chat/completions` (gpt-5.4-mini), emitting `answers_*.jsonl` in the shape `grader_v4.py` expects. Scope excludes MDEMG retrieval + Claude-agent synthesis + file citations (absolute scores ~0.06 on both sides are an expected artifact of the symmetric handicap; the gap between backends remains meaningful). **Sprint F (post-SFT MDEMG-wrapped eval) is the complementary test** measuring end-user-observable quality of the full pipeline. Deviation recorded at `~/.mdemg-sprint-c/gate3/deviation.md` with explicit Reason / Scope / Relationship-to-Sprint-F / Budget-impact sections. **OpenAI spend: $0.021** against the $25 cap (10× below the $0.22 tiktoken pre-estimate; 11,211 prompt + 14,689 completion tokens). Mid-execution fix: initial run hit 120/120 HTTP 400 `"Unsupported parameter: 'max_tokens'"` — gpt-5.x Chat Completions requires `max_completion_tokens`; `build_body()` now dispatches by backend (qwen uses `max_tokens` + `temp`/`top_p`; openai uses `max_completion_tokens` and omits sampling params per reasoning-series contract). **Same-wall-clock-window rule** satisfied: Qwen 2026-04-22T07:36:19Z, gpt-5.4-mini 2026-04-22T07:40:57Z → 4.6 min apart (well inside 24h). Cross-gate model SHA consistency: all three gates ran against `cdc167566e54ebe6d5c6df308649670b5f1cacfe71a198688edba8471ea64734`. Stamp: `~/.mdemg-sprint-c/gate3/passed_20260422T074500Z.json` (execution-time artifact). **Gate 3 middle band NOT triggered** → Sprint F remains a registered-but-inactive safety checkpoint; no `middle_band_*.json` stamp. - **FT-LORA-C: Qwen3.6-35B-A3B MLX Validation Runbook (planning-only)** (2026-04-21) — Planning-only sprint.   - **Gate 1 (MLX asymmetric-quant load)** — 4 numeric acceptance criteria: no-OOM under `mlx_lm.load`, peak RAM ≤24 GB (24-30 GB = flag with Sprint-E-recalibration note, >30 GB = halt), first-load time ≤90 s from **cold page cache** (SSD-tier-normalized: M5 Max internal NVMe baseline; USB 3.2 ≤180 s / halt 360 s; TB3/4 ≤135 s / halt 270 s; warm-load reload ~10-20 s is **not** gate-qualifying), forward-pass ≤30 s on a 512-token probe. Path options A=published asymmetric quant if available, B=`mlx_lm.convert` per-module selector attempt, C=symmetric 4-bit fallback with Sprint-E deviation recorded. Fail → halt FT-LORA line; replan Sprint C' with Qwen3.5-35B-A3B base.
  - **Gate 2 (J-group structured-output correctness)** — ≥95% JSON validity on 100 synthetic prompts (25 per J-group task: `hidden.name_emergence`, `jiminy.evaluate_llm`, `retrieval.rerank_cross`, `guardrail.evaluate`) at the canonical J recipe `temp=0.7, top_p=0.95, top_k=20, presence_penalty=1.5, **max_tokens=4096**` — **deliberate deviation** from memo §3.3's production 2048 ceiling to isolate sampling-param effects from truncation effects during the sweep. Supplementary post-sweep 20-prompt re-test at `max_tokens=2048` records non-blocking `production_config_truncation_rate` — flagged to Sprint E for memo §3.3 revision review if >5%. Fallback sweep matrix concentrates on the two most relevant levers: `presence_penalty` (5 levels: 1.0, 1.25, 1.5, 1.75, 2.0) × `temperature` (2 levels: 0.5, 0.7) + 2 control cells (`no_chat_template`, `json_mode_on`); 12 cells total, ~7.5 h single-session budget. Best cell ≥95% → pass with recipe-update flag to Sprint E; 90-94.9% or <90% → halt.
  - **Gate 3 (throughput + quality parity vs `gpt-5.4-mini`)** — throughput sub-gate: ≥60 tok/s median on M5 Max over 5×500-token runs at canonical T recipe (halt if <60). Quality sub-gate: 120-question benchmark (`test_questions_120.json`) with explicit disposition bands — ≤10% gap from baseline = clear pass, 10-30% gap = **middle band** (stamp `middle_band_*.json`, proceed to Sprints D/E, defer commit-or-fallback decision to new **Sprint F** post-SFT), >30% gap = catastrophic halt. **Hard $25 budget cap** for baseline capture: pre-run `tiktoken` estimation pauses if estimate >$25; cumulative spend tracked in `~/.mdemg-sprint-c/gate3/openai_cost.json` and aborts mid-run on cap breach. **24h same-window constraint**: baseline capture + Qwen3.6 runs must pair within a rolling 24h window (OpenAI silent model-revision drift mitigation); if the pair exceeds 24h, baseline re-capture required. Inter-gate pauses of arbitrary length remain supported — the 24h window applies only to the two halves of Gate 3 itself.
  - **Sprint FT-LORA-F registered** (new, skeleton only) — post-SFT commit-or-fallback checkpoint. Triggered only if Gate 3 leaves a `middle_band_*.json` stamp. Decision rules: ≤10% gap post-SFT → commit Qwen3.6 for MDEMG production; 10-30% → Qwen3.5 fallback; >30% → halt the FT-LORA line and revisit strategy. Full 12-section plan drafted at Sprint F start (after Phase 5 SFT completes).
  - **Mid-sprint user checkpoint** passed after plan draft review; 4 edits applied (sweep matrix reshaped from pp×top_p to pp×temp, $20→$25 cap, 24h same-window constraint added, load-time SSD-tier + first-load/warm-load clarification) + max_tokens=4096 Gate 2 deviation.
  - **Sprint chain**: A (#335, done) → B (#336, done) → **C (this plan, to commit)** → D (expert activation profiling) → E (training infra patches) → Phase 5 SFT → F (commit-or-fallback — only if Gate 3 middle band).

New constant `guardrail.TaskName = "guardrail.evaluate"` links Go code to the ULTS spec.

Seeds naming convention for Sprint E; current code ignores them.

New build-tagged integration test `tests/integration/guardrail_tsdb_capture_test.go` (Tier 3 E2E) gated on `TEST_GUARDRAIL_LIVE=1` — verifies `POST /v1/memory/guardrail/validate` lands a row in `llm_interactions` with `task_name='guardrail.evaluate'`.

### Breaking changes (admin surface)

- **Circuit breaker rename (guardrail) — hard cutover.** Old names `openai-guardrail` / `ollama-guardrail` are **removed**; new names `openai-guardrail.evaluate` / `ollama-guardrail.evaluate` registered in their place (per-task naming convention matching the ULTS task). `POST /v1/admin/breakers/reset` with `{"name":"openai-guardrail"}` now returns 404 `"unknown breaker"`. **Operators**: call `GET /v1/admin/breakers` first to discover current names, then reset by the enumerated name. Grafana dashboards and alerting rules referring to the old names must be updated. Blast radius is limited: admin endpoint is gated by `AUTH_API_KEYS`; `GUARDRAIL_ENABLED=false` by default, so most deployments are unaffected.

- **FT-LORA-A: ft-lora plan suite aligned to memo 07 v3.1** (2026-04-21) — docs-only sprint (zero code / zero behavior change) propagating three locked-in decisions across 14 files: base model Qwen3-30B-A3B → **Qwen3.6-35B-A3B** (fallback Qwen3.5-35B-A3B; Apache 2.0 2026-04-16; 35B/3B-active MoE, 256 experts = 8 routed + 1 shared, 262K native context, MTP speculative decoding); **no-tool-calling architectural policy** (all 16 MDEMG LLM call sites are single-shot; 9 banned patterns including `preserve_thinking`); **two-tier MoE-Sieve LoRA** (Tier 1 attention + shared expert r=32 α=64 all tasks balanced; Tier 2 top-25% routed experts r=8 α=16 per-family reasoning-think/classify-notink/structured-notink; `router_aux_loss_coef=0.002`; asymmetric quant shared BF16 / routed MXFP4_MOE / attention BF16). Files updated: `docs/development/ft-lora/{00_README,01_RESEARCH,02_M5MAX_HARDWARE,03_IMPLEMENTATION_PLAN,04_BENCHMARK_RL,05_DATA_COLLECTION,06_CORRECTIONS_APPLIED}_v2.md`, `docs/development/ft-lora/ft-lora-dev/{MDEMG_FT_PLAN_DEEP_DIVE_ANALYSIS,SPRINT_EMBEDDING_DATA_COLLECTION}_v2.md`, `docs/development/UXTS_FRAMEWORK_MATRIX.md`, plus new `docs/development/ft-lora/{SPRINT_A_GREP_AUDIT,sprint_plan_ft_lora_a}.md`; repo-level `VISION.md` / `CLAUDE.md` / `AGENT_HANDOFF.md`. **Two planner-introduced engineering policies** (flagged for audit trail, not in memo): (1) epoch cap 3 per tier with early-stop on `val_loss > best × 1.05` for 2 consecutive evals (SFT) / `val_reward < best × 0.95` (RL); (2) `n_epochs=auto` disallowed on all LoRA runs. Forcing function: FT-OAI-001 overfit at step 1200. Sprint sequence: **A (docs, done) → B (code/config) → C (Qwen3.6 MLX validation) → D (expert profiling) → E (training infra patches)** — Phase 5 SFT unblocks only after Sprint C passes.

### Added

- **FT-LORA-D: Expert activation profiling + family partition decision** (2026-04-22) — Read-only sprint that produces the inputs Sprint E Tier 2 LoRA needs.
  - **`neural/training/profile_expert_routing.py`** — MoE router capture via context-manager class-level monkey-patch of `Qwen3NextSparseMoeBlock.__call__`. Single-pass inline forward (no double-compute; monkey-patch restored in `finally`). Captures `inds` (top-k expert indices) + `scores` (routing weights) per token per layer. Full-generation-mode profiling using `mlx_lm.stream_generate` — captures both prompt-side and generated-token routing. SHA256 guard on `config.json` against Sprint C pin (`cdc167566e54ebe6d5c6df308649670b5f1cacfe71a198688edba8471ea64734`); aborts on mismatch. Deterministic tie-break (sort by `(-count, expert_idx)`). CLI flags: `--model-path --expected-sha256 --anchor-prompts --output-dir --top-pct --max-prompt-tokens --max-gen-tokens --limit --task --force --verbose`.
  - **`neural/training/sprint_d_analyze.py`** — analyzer that consumes the 3 family artifacts + raw counts, applies the 0/1/≥2 threshold rule at 0.80 Jaccard, computes per-family within-family task-cohesion Jaccard, runs hierarchical single-linkage agglomerative clustering on split-candidate families, and emits the decision doc.
  - **Artifacts** under `training_data/routing_profiles/`:
    - `profile_routing_reasoning_think.json` / `profile_routing_classify_notink.json` / `profile_routing_structured_notink.json` — per-family top-64-of-256 expert mask per layer (40 layers), consumed by Sprint E Tier 2 training via `--expert-selection-path`.
    - `raw_activation_counts.json` — per-(task, layer, expert) counts for post-hoc analysis.
    - `anchor_prompts.jsonl` (320 records, 20/task × 16 tasks; T=140 / C=120 / J=60) + `anchor_prompts.sha256` (`7eddeccafa9ffcdd4098a2f762afce6522f65b28ab96c87d5faeb96b0acd7087`). Primary source: production LLM interaction traces (221/320); backfill from same-shape donor tasks for 5 tasks with 0-1 production records (99/320) — documented deviation from the plan's whk-wms backfill, chosen to preserve task-family routing signal over category diversity.

Tier 2 integration: `--limit 10` on real model validates artifact schema + monkey-patch cleanup. Tier 3 E2E: full 320-prompt run; determinism confirmed **bit-identical** for MLX + mxfp4 (A/B-run SHA256 match, 1.0 Jaccard across all 40 layers — strict determinism-check branch).
